### [1] [MARS-DA: A Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning Framework for Risk-Aware Multi-Agent Bidding in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03142)
*Jiayi Chen,Xuan Zhang,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于两级电力市场竞价的标准化 Gymnasium 环境（基于 PJM 真实数据），并设计了 MARS-DA 分层强化学习框架，通过元控制器动态混合风险规避型与投机型子策略，在极端市场波动下实现了更优的风险调整后收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源渗透率增加导致电力批发市场价格剧烈波动，传统强化学习方法难以同时兼顾利润最大化与风险管理，且常过拟合特定市场条件，忽视日前-实时两级结算的随机价差。

Method: 1) 基于 PJM 历史数据构建高保真两级结算电力市场竞价 Gymnasium 环境；2) 提出 MARS-DA 分层框架，包含一个元控制器（Meta-Controller）和两个基智能体——安全智能体（优化日前分配）与投机智能体（瞄准实时套利），元控制器动态混合两者动作以适应当前市场状态。

Result: MARS-DA 在风险调整后收益上优于现有基准方法，并在极端市场波动期间保持稳健的体制对齐。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效平衡了日前市场的风险规避与实时套利机会的捕捉，为两级电力市场竞价中的风险敏感型决策提供了新范式。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of renewable energy has introduced substantial volatility into wholesale electricity markets, complicating the optimal bidding strategies for power producers. Traditional Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches often struggle to balance profit maximization with risk management, frequently overfitting to specific market conditions or failing to account for the stochastic spread between Day-Ahead (DA) and Real-Time (RT) settlements. To address these challenges, this paper makes two primary contributions. First, we introduce and open-source a high-fidelity gymnasium environment for two-settlement electricity market bidding. Grounded in extensive empirical data from the PJM Interconnection, the environment explicitly models the interplay between DA commitments and RT deviations, providing a standardized testbed for general and risk-sensitive agents. Second, we propose MARS-DA (Multi-Agent Regime-Switching for Day-Ahead markets), a novel hierarchical framework that orchestrates distinct sub-policies for risk management and profit seeking. MARS-DA utilizes a top-level Meta-Controller to dynamically blend the actions of two specialized base agents: a "Safe Agent" that optimizes for reliable DA allocation and a "Speculator Agent" that targets volatile RT arbitrage opportunities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MARS-DA achieves superior risk-adjusted returns compared to state-of-the-art baselines while maintaining robust regime alignment during periods of extreme market volatility.

</details>


### [2] [Enwar 3.0: An Agentic Multi-Modal LLM Orchestrator for Situation-Aware Beamforming, Blockage Prediction, and Handover Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03215)
*Ahmad M. Nazar,Abdulkadir Celik,Asmaa Abdallah,Mohamed Y. Selim,Daji Qiao,Ahmed M. Eltawil*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Enwar 3.0，一个融合多模态感知、智能体LLM和上下文驱动模型选择的环境感知推理框架，用于毫米波车联网中的预测波束赋形、阻塞检测和切换管理，在多项指标上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波车联网需要实时适应环境动态、传感器退化和链路变化，现有方法缺乏统一的、可解释的实时推理框架来协调多模态感知与决策。

Method: 集成传感器退化分类器（通过合成退化流水线训练，检测摄像头/雷达/激光雷达/GPS实时异常）+ 基于CoT思维链和人工反馈训练的LLM作为中央协调器，调度多个专用智能体（波束选择、阻塞预测、环境感知），并根据环境上下文动态加载传感器特定模型。

Result: 传感器退化分类准确率>99%，波束选择准确率>88%，阻塞F1分数>98%，复杂决策推理正确率87%。在15种传感器组合上全面评估达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM集成的无线系统建立了可扩展的基础，使系统能够实时推理、感知和自适应。

Abstract: Maintaining robust millimeter-wave (mmWave) connectivity in vehicular networks requires real-time adaptation to environmental dynamics, sensor degradation, and link variability. This paper presents Enwar 3.0, an environment-aware reasoning framework that unifies multi-modal sensing, agentic large language models (LLMs), and context-driven model selection for predictive beamforming, blockage detection, and handover management. Building upon prior iterations of Enwar, the proposed architecture integrates a classifier-driven assessment of sensor health with a primed LLM that orchestrates multiple specialized agents through structured, task-aware prompting. A novel synthetic degradation pipeline enables the training of a sensor degradation classifier that detects real-time impairments across camera, radar, LiDAR, and GPS inputs, achieving over 99% accuracy. The LLM, trained via chain-of-thought (CoT) priming and human-in-the-loop feedback, coordinates agent calls for beam selection, blockage forecasting, and environment perception while dynamically loading sensor-specific models based on environmental context. Extensive evaluations across 15 sensor combinations demonstrate that Enwar 3.0 delivers state-of-the-art performance in both predictive accuracy and interpretability, with beam selection accuracy exceeding 88%, blockage F1-scores surpassing 98%, and reasoning correctness reaching 87% on complex decision prompts. This work establishes a scalable foundation for LLM-integrated wireless systems that reason, perceive, and adapt in real-time.

</details>


### [3] [Coordination as an Architectural Layer for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03310)
*Maksym Nechepurenko,Pavel Shuvalov*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统在生产中失败率高达41%-87%，主要源于协调缺陷而非模型能力。本文提出将协调作为可配置的架构层，通过预测市场设计进行信息控制实验，在100个Polymarket二元市场中使用Brier分数Murphy分解分析五种协调配置，发现配置会留下可区分的签名，并建立了成本-质量帕累托边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么经验性地编目失败模式，要么提供声明式编排框架作为工程工具，但都没有提供从协调配置到可预测失败模式签名的原则性映射。需要将协调视为可配置的架构层，实现架构推理而非仅仅工程效率。

Method: 信息控制设计：单一LLM、固定工具、固定每次调用输出上限、固定提示模板，跨越五种参考协调配置。使用Brier分数的Murphy分解分离校准能力与区分能力。在100个Polymarket二元市场（训练截止日期后）上进行实验，报告Murphy签名、成本-质量帕累托边界、类别条件分析和bootstrap功效投影。

Result: 五个预设预测中有三个方向得到支持；两种配置在该机制内主导帕累托边界；探索性bootstrap区间将共识对齐与其他配置区分开来，但配对检验在n=100时未通过Bonferroni校正。同时部署相同配置作为Foresight Arena的实时智能体，作为链上复制渠道。

Conclusion: 本文定位为方法论验证的首次实例化，而非通用的跨模型主张。协调配置留下了可区分的Brier Murphy签名，证明了协调作为可配置架构层的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems fail in production at rates between 41% and 87%, mostly due to coordination defects rather than base-model capability. Existing responses split between cataloguing failure modes empirically and shipping declarative orchestration frameworks as engineering tools; neither delivers a principled mapping from coordination configuration to predictable failure-mode signature. We argue that coordination should be treated as a configurable architectural layer, separable from agent logic and from information access, enabling architectural reasoning rather than only engineering productivity.
  We instantiate this with an information-controlled design on prediction markets: a single LLM, fixed tools, fixed per-call output cap, and fixed prompt template across five reference coordination configurations, with total compute per question treated as an endogenous architectural output. The Murphy decomposition of the Brier score separates calibration from discriminative power, so configurations leave distinguishable signatures even when aggregate scores coincide.
  On 100 Polymarket binary markets resolved after the model's training cutoff (claude-opus-4-6) we report Murphy signatures, a cost-quality Pareto frontier, category-conditioned analysis, and a bootstrap power-projection. Three of five pre-specified predictions are upheld in direction; two configurations dominate the Pareto frontier within this regime; exploratory bootstrap intervals separate consensus alignment from others, though pairwise tests do not survive Bonferroni correction at n=100. We also deploy the same configurations as live agents on Foresight Arena under web-search-enabled conditions, as an on-chain replication channel accumulating in parallel. Harness, trace dataset, and production agents are released. We position this as a methodology-validating first instantiation, not a general cross-model claim.

</details>


### [4] [Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent Architecture for Traceable, Risk-Aware Human-AI Decision Support in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04003)
*Danny Hoang,Ryan Matthiessen,Christopher Miller,Nasir Mannan,Ruby ElKharboutly,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MAKA（多智能体知识分析）框架，一种人在回路中的决策支持架构，用于高精度航空航天零件数控加工中的补偿决策。该框架将意图路由、工具量化分析、知识图谱检索和基于评判器的验证分离，显著提升了多步数值工作流的执行可靠性（最多提升87.5个百分点），并在钛合金转子叶片数字孪生仿真中将表面偏差从10⁻²英寸量级降低至约±10⁻³英寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现成的LLM助手无法可靠执行受风险约束的多步数值工作流，也无法为高风险决策提供可审计的出处证明，这限制了其在航空航天高精度加工补偿决策中的应用。

Method: 提出MAKA多智能体架构，包含四个分离模块：意图路由、纯工具定量分析、知识图谱检索、以及基于评判器的验证（确保物理合理性、安全边界和出处完整性），最终输出供人类审批。

Result: 在三级别工具编排基准测试中，相比非结构化的单模型交互模式，MAKA将成功工具执行率最高提升87.5个百分点；在数字孪生仿真中，预测表面偏差从10⁻²英寸量级降至约±10⁻³英寸。

Conclusion: MAKA通过多智能体协作与评判验证机制，实现了可审计、可追溯的高风险加工补偿决策支持，为风险感知的人机协同决策提供了有效的预部署验证信号。

Abstract: High-precision CNC machining of free-form aerospace components requires bounded compensations informed by inspection, simulation, and process knowledge. Off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) assistants can generate text, but they do not reliably execute risk-constrained multi-step numerical workflows or provide auditable provenance for high-stakes decisions. We present multi-agent knowledge analysis (MAKA), a human-in-the-loop decision-support architecture that separates intent routing, tools-only quantitative analysis, knowledge graph retrieval, and critic-based verification that enforces physical plausibility, safety bounds, and provenance completeness before recommendations are surfaced for human approval. MAKA is instantiated on a Ti-6Al-4V rotor blade machining testbed by fusing virtual-machining path-tracking error fields, cutting-force and deflection simulations, and scan-based 3D inspection deviation maps from 16 blades. The analysis decomposes deviation into an evidence-linked pathing component, a drift-based wear proxy capturing systematic evolution across parts, a residual systematic compliance term, and a variability proxy for instability-aware escalation. In a three-level tool-orchestration benchmark (single-step through $\geq$3-step stateful sequences), MAKA improves successful tool execution by up to 87.5 percentage points relative to an unstructured single-model interaction pattern with identical tool access. Digital twin what-if studies show MAKA can coordinate traceable compensation candidates that reduce predicted surface deviation from order $10^{-2}$in to approximately $\pm 10^{-3}$in over most of the blade within the simulation environment, providing a pre-deployment verification signal for risk-aware human decision-making.

</details>

### [5] [FINER-SQL: Boosting Small Language Models for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03465)
*Thanh Dat Hoang,Thanh Trung Huynh,Matthias Weidlich,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Tong Chen,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FINER-SQL提出了一种基于细粒度执行反馈的强化学习框架，通过记忆奖励和原子奖励将稀疏二值监督转化为密集可解释奖励，使3B小模型在Text-to-SQL任务上达到与大型语言模型相媲美的性能（BIRD 67.73%，Spider 85%），同时显著降低推理延迟（5.57秒/样本）并支持隐私保护的本地部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在Text-to-SQL中计算成本高、延迟大且存在数据隐私问题；小语言模型虽然高效可私有化部署，但推理能力弱且指令遵循差；传统强化学习基于稀疏二值奖励（0/1）在SQL生成错误时提供的学习信号不足，导致训练不稳定或崩溃。

Method: 基于组相对策略优化（GRPO），提出两种细粒度奖励函数：1）记忆奖励（memory reward），将推理与已验证的轨迹对齐以保持语义稳定性；2）原子奖励（atomic reward），度量操作级重叠度，对结构正确但不完整的SQL给予部分信用。将离散的正确性判断转化为连续的密集奖励，实现无批评家的稳定优化。

Result: 在BIRD和Spider基准上，FINER-SQL使用3B模型分别达到67.73%和85%的执行准确率，与更大规模的大语言模型性能相当，同时将推理延迟降至5.57秒/样本。

Conclusion: FINER-SQL提供了一条成本高效且保护隐私的高性能Text-to-SQL生成路径，证明了通过细粒度执行反馈强化学习可以显著提升小语言模型的SQL生成能力，使其在实际应用中成为大语言模型的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models have driven major advances in Text-to-SQL generation. However, they suffer from high computational cost, long latency, and data privacy concerns, which make them impractical for many real-world applications. A natural alternative is to use small language models (SLMs), which enable efficient and private on-premise deployment. Yet, SLMs often struggle with weak reasoning and poor instruction following. Conventional reinforcement learning methods based on sparse binary rewards (0/1) provide little learning signal when the generated SQLs are incorrect, leading to unstable or collapsed training. To overcome these issues, we propose FINER-SQL, a scalable and reusable reinforcement learning framework that enhances SLMs through fine-grained execution feedback. Built on group relative policy optimization, FINER-SQL replaces sparse supervision with dense and interpretable rewards that offer continuous feedback even for incorrect SQLs. It introduces two key reward functions: a memory reward, which aligns reasoning with verified traces for semantic stability, and an atomic reward, which measures operation-level overlap to grant partial credit for structurally correct but incomplete SQLs. This approach transforms discrete correctness into continuous learning, enabling stable, critic-free optimization. Experiments on the BIRD and Spider benchmarks show that FINER-SQL achieves up to 67.73\% and 85\% execution accuracy with a 3B model -- matching much larger LLMs while reducing inference latency to 5.57~s/sample. These results highlight a cost-efficient and privacy-preserving path toward high-performance Text-to-SQL generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/thanhdath/finer-sql.

</details>


### [6] [In-memory Multidimensional Indexing Using the skd-tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03640)
*Achilleas Michalopoulos,Dimitrios Tsitsigkos,Nikos Mamoulis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为skd-tree的多维索引结构，通过单维度多切片分区、压缩分割器和SIMD并行加速，显著减少了树的高度和查询计算量，在主存多维范围查询和最近邻查询中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主存数据库中需要高效支持多维范围查询和最近邻查询，而传统数据结构（R-tree、kd-tree、quad-tree、网格划分）以及最近提出的多维学习索引仍有性能瓶颈，需要更高效的索引方案。

Method: 1) 提出skd-tree，每个节点在单个分裂维度上将其子树空间划分为多个切片；2) 压缩分割器并利用SIMD数据并行性，大幅减少树层数和查询计算量；3) 设计了基于SIMD的范围查询和kNN算法，每个节点仅需少量SIMD指令；4) 提出自顶向下构建算法、多种内节点和叶节点类型以保持树平衡，以及更新算法。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的实验评估表明，skd-tree在性能上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: skd-tree通过结合kd-tree的分维思想和B+-tree的节点结构，并利用SIMD并行加速，为多维主存索引提供了一种高效的新方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the problem of indexing multi-dimensional data in memory for the efficient support of multi-dimensional range queries and nearest neighbor queries. This is a classic problem in main-memory databases, where there is a need for indexing multiple columns simultaneously. Established data structures include the R-tree, kd-tree, quad-tree, and grid-based partitioning. More recently, multi-dimensional learned indexes have also been proposed to address this problem. We propose slicing kd-tree (skd-tree), a variant of the kd-tree, where each node partitions the space of its subtree into multiple slices across a single splitting dimension. By compressing the splitters of the partitions and with the help of data-parallelism, we (i) radically reduce the number of levels of the tree and (ii) limit the number of computations required for multi-dimensional range and proximity queries. The nodes of the skd-tree resemble the nodes of a main-memory B+-tree, however, a different dimension is used at each level. Our novel range and kNN algorithms on the skd-tree apply only a small constant number of SIMD instructions at each node during tree traversal. Our contributions also include a novel top-down construction algorithm, different types of inner and leaf nodes that warrant tree balancing, and a novel update algorithm. Our skd-tree achieves strong performance compared to existing methods, according to our experimental evaluation on real and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [7] [ConRAD: Conformal Risk-Aware Neural Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03806)
*Sonia Horchidan,Fabian Zeiher,Xiangyu Shi,Vasiliki Kalavri,Henrik Boström,Ioannis Kontoyiannis,Paris Carbone*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ConRAD是首个在神经图数据库查询引擎中强制执行声明式召回保证的框架。它通过保形风险控制为用户指定的风险预算提供有限样本、分布自由的统计有效性保证，同时最大化端到端精度。通过分位数空间标量化将高维阈值搜索简化为单参数，并设计保形门算子动态跳过稠密图区域的神经推理。实验表明ConRAD严格满足所有风险预算，精度匹配或超越无保证的静态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不完全知识图谱上使用神经预测器进行查询虽然强大但存在风险——多跳流水线中错误会累积，且结果完备性缺乏正式边界。现有方法无法提供可证明的召回保证。

Method: 提出ConRAD框架：(1) 利用保形风险控制(Conformal Risk Control)为每个算子推导满足用户风险预算的预测阈值，具有有限样本、分布自由的统计有效性；(2) 引入分位数空间标量化(quantile-space scalarization)将高维阈值搜索降为单参数优化；(3) 设计保形门(conformal gate)物理算子，在局部图证据充分时动态跳过神经推理，减少推理开销。

Result: 在三个基准和三种查询拓扑上，ConRAD严格满足所有风险预算，经验召回率低于目标值最多仅0.046。在接近完整的图区域中推理调用降至零，精度匹配或超越需要手动阈值搜索且无保证的最佳静态基线。

Conclusion: ConRAD成功将声明式召回保证融入神经图数据库查询引擎，在保证统计有效性的同时实现了高效推理和高精度，为神经符号查询推理提供了可证明安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Querying incomplete knowledge graphs with neural predictors is powerful but dangerous. Errors compound across multi-hop pipelines with no formal bound on the completeness of results. We introduce ConRAD, the first framework to enforce declarative recall guarantees natively within a neural graph database query engine. Given a user-specified risk budget, ConRAD automatically derives per-operator prediction thresholds that satisfy the recall target with finite-sample, distribution-free statistical validity via Conformal Risk Control, while maximizing end-to-end precision. To scale calibration across multi-operator query topologies, we introduce a quantile-space scalarization that reduces intractable high-dimensional threshold searches to a single parameter. We further design the conformal gate, a novel physical operator that dynamically bypasses neural inference when local graph evidence suffices, eliminating unnecessary model inferences in dense graph regions. Evaluated across three benchmarks and three query topologies, ConRAD strictly satisfies all risk budgets, with empirical recall falling below the target by at most 0.046 across all settings. It reduces neural invocations to zero in near-complete graph regions, and achieves precision that matches or exceeds best-case static baselines that offer no guarantees and require manual threshold search.

</details>


### [8] [Inconsistent Databases and Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03954)
*Yasir Mahmood,Jonni Virtema,Timon Barlag,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文建立了子集最大修复与SETAFs（允许集体攻击的论证框架）之间的新联系，发现：否定约束下的修复对应naive/首选/稳定扩展；特定tuple-generating依赖下的修复对应首选扩展（预处理后可得到唯一稳定扩展）；混合约束时仅首选语义有效；函数依赖和包含依赖不需要集体攻击，可转化为普通AFs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，不一致数据库的子集最大修复与论证框架中可接受集合之间的联系引起了广泛兴趣。本文旨在将这种联系扩展到更复杂的完整性约束类别，包括否定约束和local-as-view tuple-generating依赖。

Method: 通过建立不一致数据库到SETAFs的映射，分析不同完整性约束类别（否定约束、tuple-generating依赖、函数依赖、包含依赖）下子集最大修复与各种论证语义（naive、首选、稳定）之间的对应关系。

Result: 否定约束下的修复对应naive扩展（与首选和稳定扩展一致）；tuple-generating依赖下的修复对应首选扩展（预处理后可得到唯一稳定/naive扩展）；混合约束时仅首选语义有效；函数依赖和包含依赖可转化为普通AFs。

Conclusion: 论文扩展了不一致数据库修复与论证框架之间的理论联系，澄清了不同约束类别下所需论证框架的复杂度，为数据库修复的论证语义解释提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The connection between subset-maximal repairs for inconsistent databases involving various integrity constraints and acceptable sets of arguments within argumentation frameworks has recently drawn growing interest. In this paper, we contribute to this domain by establishing a new connection when integrity constraints (ICs) include denial constraints and local-as-view tuple-generating dependencies. It turns out that SET-based Argumentation Frameworks (SETAFs), an extension of Dung's argumentation frameworks (AFs) allowing collective attacks, are needed. It is known that subset-maximal repairs under denial constraints correspond to the naive extensions, which also coincide with the preferred and stable extensions in the resulting SETAFs. Our main findings establish that repairs under the considered fragment of tuple-generating dependencies correspond to the preferred extensions. Moreover, for these dependencies, additional preprocessing allows computing a unique extension that is stable and naive. Allowing both types of constraints breaks this relationship, and even the pre-processing does not help as only preferred semantics captures these repairs. Finally, while it is known that functional dependencies do not require set-based attacks, we prove the same regarding inclusion dependencies. Thus, one can translate inconsistent databases under these restricted classes of ICs to plain AFs with attacks only between arguments.

</details>

### [9] [Human-in-the-Loop Uncertainty Analysis in Self-Adaptive Robots Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02983)
*Hassan Sartaj,Jalil Boudjadar,Mirgita Frasheri,Shaukat Ali,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboULM——一种基于大语言模型(LLM)且引入人机协同(Human-in-the-Loop)的方法论与工具，用于在自适应机器人设计阶段系统地识别和分析不确定性；同时呈现了一个面向自适应机器人的不确定性分类体系。通过四个工业用例、16名从业者的评估，结果表明RoboULM被认为有用且易于理解，结构化提示和迭代优化支持尤其受到好评。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应机器人在动态、不可预测环境中运行时，未处理的不确定性会导致安全隐患和运行故障。然而，鉴于现实环境的固有复杂性、机器人行为的动态性以及技术的快速演进，系统地识别和分析不确定性（包括其来源、影响和缓解策略）仍然是一项重大挑战。

Method: 提出RoboULM，一种人机协同(Human-in-the-Loop)的方法论与工具，利用大语言模型(LLM)在设计阶段系统性地探索不确定性；同时构建了一个详细的自适应机器人不确定性分类体系。

Result: 在四个工业用例中，16名从业者评估了RoboULM，结果显示该工具被认为既有用又易于理解，参与者尤其重视结构化提示和迭代优化支持。

Conclusion: RoboULM可作为复杂机器人系统中进行系统化不确定性分析的可行解决方案。

Abstract: Self-adaptive robots operate in dynamic, unpredictable environments where unaddressed uncertainties can lead to safety violations and operational failures. However, systematically identifying and analyzing these uncertainties, including their sources, impacts, and mitigation strategies, remains a significant challenge given the inherent complexity of real-world environments, dynamic robotic behavior, and the rapid evolution of robotic technologies. To address this, we introduce RoboULM, a human-in-the-loop methodology and tool that supports practitioners in systematically exploring uncertainties at the design stage using large language models (LLMs). Moreover, we present an uncertainty taxonomy that provides a detailed catalog of uncertainties in self-adaptive robots. We evaluated RoboULM with 16 practitioners from four industrial use cases. The results show that RoboULM was perceived as both useful and easy to understand, with the participants particularly valuing structured prompting and iterative refinement support. These findings demonstrate the potential of RoboULM as a viable solution for systematic uncertainty analysis in complex robots.

</details>


### [10] [Benchmarking Local Language Models for Social Robots using Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03111)
*Dorian Lamouille,Matevž B. Zorec,Farnaz Baksh,Karl Kruusamäe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在树莓派等边缘硬件上系统评测了25个开源语言模型，从推理效率、通用知识和教学效果三个维度进行分析，发现各指标间存在显著权衡，其中Granite4 Tiny Hybrid (7B)在综合表现上最佳，并提出了针对资源受限硬件的三层本地推理架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交教育机器人（如RSC）需要隐私保护且响应及时的本地交互，但边缘计算场景下语言模型的系统性基准评测在 pedagogical 应用中尚属空白。

Method: 在树莓派4/5及笔记本电脑GPU上评测25个开源模型，从推理效率（tokens/s、能耗）、通用知识（MMLU子集）、教学效果（LLM评分，经5位人工验证）三个维度进行综合评估。

Result: 模型间吞吐量和能效差异超过一个数量级；MMLU准确率从近随机到57.2%；教学效果与其他指标无单调相关；Granite4 Tiny Hybrid (7B)在综合平衡上最优（2.5 tokens/s, 0.90 tokens/J, 54.6% MMLU）；人工验证与自动排序高度一致（Pearson r=0.967）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种适用于资源受限硬件的三层本地推理架构，以平衡响应速度和准确性。

Abstract: Social-educational robots designed for socially interactive pedagogical support, such as the Robot Study Companion (RSC), rely on responsive, privacy-preserving interaction despite severely limited compute. However, there is a gap in systematic benchmarking of language models for edge computing in pedagogical applications. This paper benchmarks 25 open-source language models for local deployment on edge hardware. We evaluate each model across three dimensions: inference efficiency (tokens per second, energy consumption), general knowledge (a six-category MMLU subset), and teaching effectiveness (LLM-rated pedagogical quality), validated against five independent human raters using the Raspberry Pi(RPi)4 as the primary platform, with additional comparisons on the RPi5 and a laptop GPU.
  Results reveal pronounced trade-offs: throughput and energy efficiency vary by over an order of magnitude across models, MMLU accuracy ranges from near-random to 57.2%, and teaching effectiveness does not correlate monotonically with either metric. Among the evaluated models, Granite4 Tiny Hybrid (7B) achieves a strong overall balance, reaching 2.5 tokens per second, 0.90 tokens per joule, and 54.6% MMLU accuracy; high MMLU accuracy does not appear necessary for strong teaching scores. Human validation on four representative models preserved the automated rank ordering (Pearson r = 0.967, n = 4). Based on these findings, we propose a three-tier local inference architecture for the RSC that balances responsiveness and accuracy on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [11] [Robust Path Tracking for Vehicles via Continuous-Time Residual Learning: An ICODE-MPPI Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03260)
*Shugen Song,Wenjie Mei,Chengyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ICODE-MPPI框架，利用输入伴随神经常微分方程（ICODE）学习并补偿未建模残差动力学，在MPPI预测时域中保持物理一致性和时间连续性。相比标准MPPI，在复杂轨迹的高保真仿真中，交叉跟踪误差降低高达69%，并有效抑制控制抖振，生成更平滑的转向指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPPI控制受限于名义动力学的保真度，未建模的残差动力学成为性能瓶颈，亟需一种能在线补偿模型误差且保持物理一致性的方法。

Method: 将输入伴随神经常微分方程（ICODE）集成到MPPI框架中，利用ICODE的连续时间特性学习残差动力学，并在MPPI的预测时域内进行补偿，替代传统的离散时间学习方法。

Result: 在高保真仿真中，ICODE-MPPI相比标准MPPI，交叉跟踪误差最高降低69%，同时有效抑制控制抖振，输出更平滑的转向指令。

Conclusion: ICODE-MPPI通过连续时间残差动力学补偿显著提升了MPPI的鲁棒性和控制平滑性，是一种有效的鲁棒控制框架。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is a powerful sampling-based strategy for nonlinear autonomous systems. However, its performance is often bottlenecked by the fidelity of nominal dynamics. We propose ICODE-MPPI, a robust framework that leverages Input Concomitant Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ICODEs) to learn and compensate for unmodeled residual dynamics. Unlike discrete-time learners, ICODEs maintain physical consistency and temporal continuity during the MPPI prediction horizon. High-fidelity simulations on complex trajectories demonstrate that ICODE-MPPI achieves up to a 69\% reduction in cross-tracking error under persistent disturbances compared to standard MPPI control. Furthermore, our analysis confirms that ICODE-MPPI significantly suppresses control chattering, yielding smoother steering commands and superior robust performance.

</details>


### [12] [RLDX-1 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03269)
*Dongyoung Kim,Huiwon Jang,Myungkyu Koo,Suhyeok Jang,Taeyoung Kim,Beomjun Kim,Byungjun Yoon,Changsung Jang,Daewon Choi,Dongsu Han,Donguk Lee,Heeseung Kwon,Hojin Jeon,Jaehyun Kang,Jaekyoung Bae,Jihyuk Lee,Jimin Lee,John Won,Joonwoo Ahn,Junhyeong Park,Junyoung Sung,Kyungmin Lee,Minseong Han,Minsung Yoon,Sejune Joo,Seonil Son,Seungcheol Park,Seunggeun Cho,Seungjun Moon,Seungku Kim,Yonghoon Dong,Yongjin Cho,Youngchan Kim,Chang Hwan Kim,Dohyeon Kim,Hazel Lee,Heecheol Kim,Hensen Ahn,Hyungkyu Ryu,Hyunsoo Choi,Hyunsoo Shin,Jaeheon Jung,Jaewoo Kim,Jinwook Kim,Joochul Chang,Joonsoo Kim,Junghun Park,Jungwoo Park,Junho Cho,Junhyeok Park,Junwon Lee,Kangwook Lee,Kwanghoon Kim,Kyoungwhan Choe,Manoj Bhadu,Nayoung Oh,Sangjun Kim,Sangwoo Kim,Seunghoon Shim,Seunghyun Kim,Seungjun Lee,Seungyup Ka,Sungryol Yang,Wook Jung,Yashu Shukla,Yeonjae Lee,Yeonwoo Bae,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RLDX-1是一种基于多流动作Transformer（MSAT）架构的通用灵巧操作机器人策略，通过整合异构模态（运动感知、记忆决策、物理传感）和系统级设计（合成罕见场景数据、类人操作学习、实时推理优化），在模拟和真实任务中显著优于π₀.5和GR00T N1.6等前沿VLA模型，在ALLEX人形任务中达到86.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）虽具备通用智能（场景理解、语言条件泛化），但在复杂真实任务中缺乏更广泛的功能能力（如运动感知、记忆感知决策、物理传感），限制了其在灵巧操作中的应用。

Method: 提出多流动作Transformer（MSAT）架构，通过模态特定流与跨模态联合自注意力统一异构模态；结合系统级设计：合成训练数据覆盖罕见操作场景、专用类人操作学习流程、实时部署推理优化。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实任务中一致优于π₀.5和GR00T N1.6；ALLEX人形任务成功率86.8%，对比方法约40%，展现了高自由度人形机器人在多样化功能需求下的控制能力。

Conclusion: RLDX-1是迈向可靠VLA用于复杂、接触丰富、动态真实世界灵巧操作的重要一步。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have shown remarkable progress toward human-like generalist robotic policies through the versatile intelligence (i.e. broad scene understanding and language-conditioned generalization) inherited from pre-trained Vision-Language Models, they still struggle with complex real-world tasks requiring broader functional capabilities (e.g. motion awareness, memory-aware decision making, and physical sensing). To address this, we introduce RLDX-1, a general-purpose robotic policy for dexterous manipulation built on the Multi-Stream Action Transformer (MSAT), an architecture that unifies these capabilities by integrating heterogeneous modalities through modality-specific streams with cross-modal joint self-attention. RLDX-1 further combines this architecture with system-level design choices, including synthesizing training data for rare manipulation scenarios, learning procedures specialized for human-like manipulation, and inference optimizations for real-time deployment. Through empirical evaluation, we show that RLDX-1 consistently outperforms recent frontier VLAs (e.g. $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.6) across both simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks that require broad functional capabilities beyond general versatility. In particular, RLDX-1 shows superiority in ALLEX humanoid tasks by achieving success rates of 86.8% while $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.6 achieve around 40%, highlighting the ability of RLDX-1 to control a high-DoF humanoid robot under diverse functional demands. Together, these results position RLDX-1 as a promising step toward reliable VLAs for complex, contact-rich, and dynamic real-world dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [13] [Learning Reactive Dexterous Grasping via Hierarchical Task-Space RL Planning and Joint-Space QP Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03363)
*Ho Jae Lee,Yonghyeon Lee,Alexander Alexiev,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Se Hwan Jeon,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合分层控制框架，将高层空间意图与低层关节执行解耦，通过多智能体强化学习（双臂与手部智能体）生成任务空间速度指令，再由GPU并行化二次规划控制器转换为可行关节速度，实现了零样本可操控性和鲁棒的真实世界灵巧抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取方法在运动规划与低层控制间缺乏明确解耦，导致训练收敛慢、硬件安全性差，且难以在运行时灵活调整行为（如避开动态障碍物）。

Method: 1) 多智能体强化学习架构（手臂+手部智能体）作为高层规划器，输出任务空间速度命令；2) GPU并行化二次规划控制器将速度命令转换为联合速度，同时强制执行运动学限制与碰撞避免；3) 结构解耦加速训练收敛并保障硬件安全，支持零样本可操控性（动态调整安全裕度）。

Result: 在7自由度手臂+20自由度仿人手的硬件平台上，通过仿真到现实的严格迁移管线，对多种未见物体实现了零样本灵巧抓取，并在非结构化环境中展现了对意外物理干扰的反应式恢复能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过解耦高层规划与低层控制，兼顾了训练效率、硬件安全性、运行时可操控性以及从仿真到现实的无缝迁移，为反应式灵巧抓取提供了鲁棒且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a hybrid hierarchical control framework for reactive dexterous grasping that explicitly decouples high-level spatial intent from low-level joint execution. We introduce a multi-agent reinforcement learning architecture, specialized into distinct arm and hand agents, that acts as a high-level planner by generating desired task-space velocity commands. These commands are then processed by a GPU-parallelized quadratic programming controller, which translates them into feasible joint velocities while strictly enforcing kinematic limits and collision avoidance. This structural isolation not only accelerates training convergence but also strictly enforces hardware safety. Furthermore, the architecture unlocks zero-shot steerability, allowing system operators to dynamically adjust safety margins and avoid dynamic obstacles without retraining the policy. We extensively validate the proposed framework through a rigorous simulation-to-reality pipeline. Real-world hardware experiments on a 7-DoF arm equipped with a 20-DoF anthropomorphic hand demonstrate highly robust zero-shot transferability for dexterous grasping to a diverse set of unseen objects, highlighting the system's ability to reactively recover from unexpected physical disturbances in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [14] [Feasibility-aware Hybrid Control for Motion Planning under Signal Temporal Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03662)
*Panagiotis Rousseas,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于混合建模的平面任务与运动规划新方法，通过离散变量建模局部约束满足并启用局部可行性分析，统一了规划与控制设计。同时在原始非凸几何复杂工作空间的变换盘版本上设计控制障碍函数，修正了死锁问题。仿真表明该方法即使在输入饱和情况下也能有效处理多个重叠时空任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平面任务与运动规划方法在处理非凸/几何复杂工作空间中的多重叠时空任务时，面临死锁问题和输入饱和挑战，且规划与控制设计相对分离，缺乏统一的架构。

Method: 基于混合建模，引入离散变量建模局部约束满足并进行局部可行性分析；在工作空间变换后的盘状区域上设计控制障碍函数以解决死锁问题。

Result: 仿真验证了该方法能在输入饱和条件下有效处理多个重叠时空任务，死锁问题得到修正。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合建模方法统一了规划与控制设计，通过变换空间上的控制障碍函数有效解决死锁，适用于复杂工作空间中的多任务规划。

Abstract: In this work, a novel method for planar task and motion planning based on hybrid modeling is proposed. By virtue of a discrete variable which models local constraint satisfaction and enables local feasibility analysis, the proposed control architecture unifies planning with control design. Concurrently, control barrier functions are designed on a transformed disk version of the original nonconvex and geometrically complex robotic workspace, thus amending the issue of deadlocks. Simulations of the proposed method indicate effective handling of multiple overlapping spatio-temporal tasks even in the face of input saturation.

</details>


### [15] [Height Control and Optimal Torque Planning for Jumping With Wheeled-Bipedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03302)
*Yulun Zhuang,Yuan Xu,Binxin Huang,Mandan Chao,Guowei Shi,Xin Yang,Kuangen Zhang,Chenglong Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对轮足混合机器人的跳跃高度精确控制问题，提出了一种基于贝叶斯优化的扭矩规划方法（BOTP），结合轮足跳跃动力学模型（W-JBD），在无需精确动力学模型的情况下实现高度精确控制，将高度误差降低82.3%，能耗降低26.9%，并在Webots仿真平台得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮足混合机器人在跳跃过程中存在欠驱动、非线性估计和瞬时冲击等问题，导致跳跃高度精确控制困难。实际中机器人常通过过冲高度确保安全，但会造成额外的电机损耗、更大的地面反作用力和更多能耗。

Method: 1) 提出W-JBD动力学模型实现精确高度控制；2) 针对W-JBD扭矩存在阶跃突变、不适用于真实机器人的问题，提出基于贝叶斯优化的扭矩规划方法（BOTP），无需精确动力学模型即可在少量迭代内获得最优扭矩规划；3) 利用W-JBD模型获得的扭矩曲线缩小搜索空间，加速BOTP收敛（平均40次）。

Result: BOTP方法可降低82.3%的高度误差、26.9%的能耗，且扭矩曲线连续平滑；在Webots仿真平台上验证有效；与W-JBD模型协同，BOTP平均40次迭代即可收敛。

Conclusion: 通过W-JBD模型与BOTP方法协同，在合理实验次数内即可实现真实机器人的跳跃高度精确控制。

Abstract: This paper mainly studies the accurate height jumping control of wheeled-bipedal robots based on torque planning and energy consumption optimization. Due to the characteristics of underactuated, nonlinear estimation, and instantaneous impact in the jumping process, accurate control of the wheeled-bipedal robot's jumping height is complicated. In reality, robots often jump at excessive height to ensure safety, causing additional motor loss, greater ground reaction force and more energy consumption. To solve this problem, a novel wheeled-bipedal jumping dynamical model(W-JBD) is proposed to achieve accurate height control. It performs well but not suitable for the real robot because the torque has a striking step. Therefore, the Bayesian optimization for torque planning method(BOTP) is proposed, which can obtain the optimal torque planning without accurate dynamic model and within few iterations. BOTP method can reduce 82.3% height error, 26.9% energy cost with continuous torque curve. This result is validated in the Webots simulation platform. Based on the torque curve obtained in the W-JBD model to narrow the searching space, BOTP can quickly converge (40 times on average). Cooperating W-JBD model and BOTP method, it is possible to achieve the height control of real robots with reasonable times of experiments.

</details>


### [16] [Bridging the Embodiment Gap: Disentangled Cross-Embodiment Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03637)
*Zhiyuan Li,Wenyan Yang,Wenshuai Zhao,Yue Ma,Yuanpeng Tu,Pekka Marttinen,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种生成式跨实体视频编辑框架，通过解耦任务和实体表征，利用冻结的视频扩散模型将单段人类演示视频转化为形态准确的机器人执行视频，无需配对数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频与机器人视频之间存在分布偏移，现有方法会产生耦合表征（任务信息与人类运动学特征纠缠），限制了机器人从人类视频中学习操作技能的适应性。

Method: 1) 将演示视频分解为正交的两个潜在空间（任务空间和实体空间）；2) 设计双重对比学习目标：最小化空间间互信息以保持独立性，最大化空间内一致性以形成稳定表征；3) 使用参数高效适配器将潜在编码注入冻结的视频扩散模型，合成机器人执行视频。

Result: 生成的时间一致且形态准确的机器人演示视频，无需跨实体配对数据即可实现从单段人类演示到机器人执行的转换。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，能够有效利用互联网规模的人类视频数据来推动机器人学习，缓解机器人数据瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Learning robotic manipulation from human videos is a promising solution to the data bottleneck in robotics, but the distribution shift between humans and robots remains a critical challenge. Existing approaches often produce entangled representations, where task-relevant information is coupled with human-specific kinematics, limiting their adaptability. We propose a generative framework for cross-embodiment video editing that directly addresses this by learning explicitly disentangled task and embodiment representations. Our method factorizes a demonstration video into two orthogonal latent spaces by enforcing a dual contrastive objective: it minimizes mutual information between the spaces to ensure independence while maximizing intra-space consistency to create stable representations. A parameter-efficient adapter injects these latent codes into a frozen video diffusion model, enabling the synthesis of a coherent robot execution video from a single human demonstration, without requiring paired cross-embodiment data. Experiments show our approach generates temporally consistent and morphologically accurate robot demonstrations, offering a scalable solution to leverage internet-scale human video for robot learning.

</details>


### [17] [Jiao: Bridging Isolation and Customization in Mixed Criticality Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03641)
*James Yen,Zhibai Huang,Zhixiang Wei,Tinghao Yi,Shupeng Zeng,Liang Pang,Songtao Xue,Zhengwei Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对消费机器人领域在共享多核平台上整合安全关键控制、感知管线与用户应用时面临的专家知识不对称问题（终端用户缺乏系统知识），提出了一种包含安全IO单元、参数同步服务和IEC~61508对齐的安全通信层的三层架构。在ARM Cortex-A55平台上的实验表明，分区隔离将周期抖动降低了84.5%，尾部时序误差减少近一个数量级（p99 |抖动|从69.0μs降至7.8μs），消除了所有>50μs的越界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费机器人需要在共享多核平台上同时运行安全关键控制、感知管线与用户应用，但直接移植汽车领域的静态分区方案会面临专家知识不对称问题：终端用户（修改机器人行为）缺乏平台开发者所具备的系统级知识，导致架构难以被安全地适配和扩展。

Method: 提出三层架构：(1) Safe IO Cell：提供硬件级覆盖控制能力；(2) Parameter Synchronization Service：封装跨域参数同步的复杂性；(3) Safety Communication Layer：实现IEC~61508对齐的通信验证。

Result: 在ARM Cortex-A55平台上，分区隔离使周期抖动降低84.5%，p99 |抖动|从69.0μs降至7.8μs（减少近一个数量级），并完全消除了所有>50μs的时序越界。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构能够在不要求终端用户具备系统级专业知识的前提下，通过硬件级隔离和安全的跨域通信机制，有效保障消费机器人平台的安全关键实时性能。

Abstract: Consumer robotics demands consolidation of safety-critical control, perception pipelines, and user applications on shared multicore platforms. While static partitioning hypervisors provide hardware-enforced isolation, directly transplanting automotive architectures encounters an expertise asymmetry problem in which end-users modifying robot behavior lack the systems knowledge that platform developers possess. We present an architecture addressing this challenge through three integrated components. A Safe IO Cell provides hardware-level override capability. A Parameter Synchronization Service encapsulates cross-domain complexity. A Safety Communication Layer implements IEC~61508-aligned verification. Our empirical evaluation on an ARM Cortex-A55 platform demonstrates that partition isolation reduces cycle-period jitter by 84.5\% and cuts tail timing error by nearly an order of magnitude (p99 $|$jitter$|$ from 69.0\,$μ$s to 7.8\,$μ$s), eliminating all $>$50\,$μ$s~excursions.

</details>


### [18] [Sensorless State Estimation and Control for Agile Cable-Suspended Payload Transport by Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03666)
*Ana Maria Nascimento,Augusto Sales,Antonio Marcus Lima,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Udwadia-Kalaba方法的无人机缆吊负载控制与无传感器估计框架，通过将缆绳几何约束显式集成到NMPC预测模型中，有效降低了轨迹跟踪误差，实验验证了其优于传统不完全模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖直接负载测量和拉格朗日动力学建模，存在实际局限性，且缺乏简洁直观的系统动力学模型。

Method: 采用Udwadia-Kalaba方法显式整合缆绳几何约束，导出张力并直接集成到NMPC预测模型；基于相同几何约束提出无传感器负载状态估计。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，将负载动力学显式纳入优化问题显著降低了轨迹跟踪误差，整体性能优于基于不完全模型的策略。

Conclusion: 显式纳入负载动力学到控制优化中能有效提升无人机缆吊系统的轨迹跟踪精度和整体性能。

Abstract: This work proposes a novel control and estimation approach for aerial manipulation of a cable-suspended load using Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs). Common approaches in the state of the art have practical limitations, relying on direct load measurements and Lagrangian methods for dynamic modeling. The lack of a straightforward dynamic model of the system led us to propose adopting the Udwadia-Kalaba method to explicitly incorporate the cable's geometric constraints. This formulation allowed for the consistent derivation of the tension force and its direct integration into the NMPC prediction model. Additionally, we propose a sensorless load state estimation based on the same geometric constraints. Results from real-robot experiments demonstrated that the explicit inclusion of load dynamics in the optimization problem significantly reduces trajectory-tracking errors and yields better overall performance compared to strategies based on incomplete models.

</details>


### [19] [FUS3DMaps: Scalable and Accurate Open-Vocabulary Semantic Mapping by 3D Fusion of Voxel- and Instance-Level Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03669)
*Timon Homberger,Finn Lukas Busch,Jesús Gerardo Ortega Peimbert,Quantao Yang,Olov Andersson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FUS3DMaps是一种在线双层的开放词汇语义建图方法，通过在共享体素地图中同时维护密集层和实例层，并进行跨层语义融合，实现了多楼层建筑尺度的高精度建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇语义建图的训练无关方法分为实例级（通过分割视图并编码图像块）和密集级（直接投影图像块嵌入），但前者需要2D到3D的实例关联，后者可扩展性有限。作者旨在结合两者的互补优势，提出一种可扩展且高精度的双层语义建图方法。

Method: 提出FUS3DMaps，一种在线双层语义建图方法，在共享体素地图中同时维护密集层和实例层。通过体素级的语义跨层融合来结合两层嵌入，并采用空间滑动窗口策略，仅对密集层和跨层融合在局部窗口内操作，以实现可扩展的实例级建图。

Result: 在已有的3D语义分割基准以及大规模场景上的实验表明，FUS3DMaps能够在多楼层建筑尺度上实现准确的开放词汇语义建图。

Conclusion: 提出的跨层语义融合方法同时提升了实例层和密集层的质量，实现了可扩展且高精度的实例级地图构建。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic mapping enables robots to spatially ground previously unseen concepts without requiring predefined class sets. Current training-free methods commonly rely on multi-view fusion of semantic embeddings into a 3D map, either at the instance-level via segmenting views and encoding image crops of segments, or by projecting image patch embeddings directly into a dense semantic map. The latter approach sidesteps segmentation and 2D-to-3D instance association by operating on full uncropped image frames, but existing methods remain limited in scalability. We present FUS3DMaps, an online dual-layer semantic mapping method that jointly maintains both dense and instance-level open-vocabulary layers within a shared voxel map. This design enables further voxel-level semantic fusion of the layer embeddings, combining the complementary strengths of both semantic mapping approaches. We find that our proposed semantic cross-layer fusion approach improves the quality of both the instance-level and dense layers, while also enabling a scalable and highly accurate instance-level map where the dense layer and cross-layer fusion are restricted to a spatial sliding window. Experiments on established 3D semantic segmentation benchmarks as well as a selection of large-scale scenes show that FUS3DMaps achieves accurate open-vocabulary semantic mapping at multi-story building scales. Additional material and code will be made available: https://githanonymous.github.io/FUS3DMaps/.

</details>


### [20] [RoboAlign-R1: Distilled Multimodal Reward Alignment for Robot Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03821)
*Hao Wu,Yuqi Li,Yuan Gao,Fan Xu,Fan Zhang,Kun Wang,Penghao Zhao,Qiufeng Wang,Yizhou Zhao,Weiyan Wang,Yingli Tian,Xian Wu,Xiaomeng Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboAlign-R1框架，通过奖励对齐的后训练和滑动窗口重编码（SWR）推理策略，显著提升了机器人视频世界模型在指令跟随、操作准确性、物理合理性等关键决策能力上的表现，并在长程预测中有效减少了误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视频世界模型使用重建损失或感知相似度等低级目标进行训练，这些目标与机器人决策所需的关键能力（如指令跟随、操作成功率和物理合理性）对齐不良；同时，长程自回归预测中存在严重的误差累积问题。

Method: 1) 构建RobotWorldBench基准（10,000个视频-指令对）；2) 训练多模态教师评判模型RoboAlign-Judge，提供六维细粒度评估；3) 将教师知识蒸馏为轻量级学生奖励模型，用于强化学习后训练；4) 提出滑动窗口重编码（SWR），一种无需训练的长程推理策略，定期刷新生成上下文以减少漂移。

Result: RoboAlign-R1在六维综合评分上比最强基线提升10.1%，其中操作准确性提升7.5%，指令跟随提升4.6%；SWR以仅约1%的额外延迟使SSIM提升2.8%，LPIPS降低9.8%。外部VLM交叉验证和盲人研究进一步支持了排序改进。

Conclusion: 奖励对齐的后训练和稳定的长程解码策略能够有效提升机器人视频世界模型的任务一致性、物理真实感和长程预测质量。

Abstract: Existing robot video world models are typically trained with low-level objectives such as reconstruction and perceptual similarity, which are poorly aligned with the capabilities that matter most for robot decision making, including instruction following, manipulation success, and physical plausibility. They also suffer from error accumulation in long-horizon autoregressive prediction. We present RoboAlign-R1, a framework that combines reward-aligned post-training with stabilized long-horizon inference for robot video world models. We construct RobotWorldBench, a benchmark of 10,000 annotated video-instruction pairs collected from four robot data sources, and train a multimodal teacher judge, RoboAlign-Judge, to provide fine-grained six-dimensional evaluation of generated videos. We then distill the teacher into a lightweight student reward model for efficient reinforcement-learning-based post-training. To reduce long-horizon rollout drift, we further introduce Sliding Window Re-encoding (SWR), a training-free inference strategy that periodically refreshes the generation context. Under our in-domain evaluation protocol, RoboAlign-R1 improves the aggregate six-dimension score by 10.1% over the strongest baseline, including gains of 7.5% on Manipulation Accuracy and 4.6% on Instruction Following; these ranking improvements are further supported by an external VLM-based cross-check and a blinded human study. Meanwhile, SWR improves long-horizon prediction quality with only about 1% additional latency, yielding a 2.8% gain in SSIM and a 9.8% reduction in LPIPS. Together, these results show that reward-aligned post-training and stabilized long-horizon decoding improve task consistency, physical realism, and long-horizon prediction quality in robot video world models.

</details>


### [21] [SigLoMa: Learning Open-World Quadrupedal Loco-Manipulation from Ego-Centric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03846)
*Shiyi Chen,Haiyi Liu,Mingye Yang,Jiaqi Zhang,Debing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SigLoMa提出了一种完全机载、自我中心视觉的开放式四足动物移动操作框架，通过Sigma Points轻量级几何表示、自我中心卡尔曼滤波、主动采样课程学习等关键技术，仅依靠5Hz低帧率检测器即可实现动态操作任务，性能媲美人类专家遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足移动操作系统过度依赖昂贵的外部动作捕捉和离机计算，传统强化学习方法存在样本效率低和仿真到现实差距大的问题，视觉跟踪的固有延迟与高频浮基控制需求存在根本性矛盾。

Method: 1) Sigma Points：轻量级几何表示实现高可扩展性和原生仿真到现实对齐；2) 自我中心卡尔曼滤波器：桥接慢感知与快控制之间的频率差异，提供鲁棒的高频状态估计；3) 主动采样课程学习：通过提示位姿引导缓解样本效率低下问题；4) 时序编码与模拟随机游走漂移：解决机器人结构视觉盲区。

Result: 在仅依赖5Hz（200ms延迟）开放词汇检测器的条件下，SigLoMa成功执行多个动态移动操作任务，性能达到专家人类遥操作水平。

Conclusion: SigLoMa证明了通过精心设计的几何表示和感知-控制频率桥接策略，可以在完全机载、自我中心视觉的条件下实现高水平的四足移动操作，消除了对外部基础设施的依赖。

Abstract: Designing an open-world quadrupedal loco-manipulation system is highly challenging. Traditional reinforcement learning frameworks utilizing exteroception often suffer from extreme sample inefficiency and massive sim-to-real gaps. Furthermore, the inherent latency of visual tracking fundamentally conflicts with the high-frequency demands of precise floating-base control. Consequently, existing systems lean heavily on expensive external motion capture and off-board computation. To eliminate these dependencies, we present SigLoMa, a fully onboard, ego-centric vision-based pick-and-place framework. At the core of SigLoMa is the introduction of Sigma Points, a lightweight geometric representation for exteroception that guarantees high scalability and native sim-to-real alignment. To bridge the frequency divide between slow perception and fast control, we design an ego-centric Kalman Filter to provide robust, high-rate state estimation. On the learning front, we alleviate sample inefficiency via an Active Sampling Curriculum guided by Hint Poses, and tackle the robot's structural visual blind spots using temporal encoding coupled with simulated random-walk drift. Real-world experiments validate that, relying solely on a 5Hz (200 ms latency) open-vocabulary detector, SigLoMa successfully executes dynamic loco-manipulation across multiple tasks, achieving performance comparable to expert human teleoperation.

</details>


### [22] [Task-Aware Scanning Parameter Configuration for Robotic Inspection Using Vision Language Embeddings and Hyperdimensional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03909)
*Zhiling Chen,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,Yang Zhang,Jiong Tang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ScanHD框架，利用超维计算将自然语言检测指令与预扫描RGB观测绑定，实现机器人激光轮廓仪的五参数智能配置；在自建数据集Instruct-Obs2Param上达到92.7%的精确匹配准确率和98.1%的Win@1准确率，性能优于规则方法、传统多模态模型和多模态大语言模型，消除了手动试错调参过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人激光轮廓测量的保真度主要受传感器配置影响，但现有参数（采样频率、曝光时间、动态范围、照明等）仍依赖人工试错调整，不匹配会导致饱和、截断或信号丢失且无法后处理恢复，迫切需要将传感器配置从静态设置提升为自适应决策变量。

Method: 提出指令条件化传感参数推荐问题；构建Instruct-Obs2Param多模态数据集（16个物体、多视角位姿与光照变化、自然语言检测指令）；提出ScanHD超维计算框架，将指令与观测绑定为任务感知编码，通过参数级关联推理与紧凑记忆匹配离散扫描器参数。

Result: 在Instruct-Obs2Param上，ScanHD五参数平均精确匹配准确率92.7%，Win@1准确率98.1%，跨分割泛化能力强，推理延迟低，优于规则启发式、传统多模态模型及多模态大语言模型。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了从任务意图和场景上下文自动进行传感配置，消除手动调参，将传感器配置从静态设置提升为自适应决策变量，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Robotic laser profiling is widely used for dimensional verification and surface inspection, yet measurement fidelity is often dominated by sensor configuration rather than robot motion. Industrial profilers expose multiple coupled parameters, including sampling frequency, measurement range, exposure time, receiver dynamic range, and illumination, that are still tuned by trial-and-error; mismatches can cause saturation, clipping, or missing returns that cannot be recovered downstream. We formulate instruction-conditioned sensing parameter recommendation; given a pre-scan RGB observation and a natural-language inspection instruction, infer a discrete configuration over key parameters of a robot-mounted profiler. To benchmark this problem, we develop Instruct-Obs2Param, a real-world multimodal dataset linking inspection intents and multi-view pose and illumination variation across 16 objects to canonical parameter regimes. We then propose ScanHD, a hyperdimensional computing framework that binds instruction and observation into a task-aware code and performs parameter-wise associative reasoning with compact memories, matching discrete scanner regimes while yielding stable, interpretable, low-latency decisions. On Instruct-Obs2Param, ScanHD achieves 92.7% average exact accuracy and 98.1% average Win@1 accuracy across the five parameters, with strong cross-split generalization and low-latency inference suitable for deployment, outperforming rule-based heuristics, conventional multimodal models, and multimodal large language models. This work enables autonomous, instruction-conditioned sensing configuration from task intent and scene context, eliminating manual tuning and elevating sensor configuration from a static setting to an adaptive decision variable.

</details>

### [23] [Same Voice, Different Lab: On the Homogenization of Frontier LLM Personalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02897)
*Avinash Krishna,Kalyana Chadalavada,Unso Eun Seo Jo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验发现，前沿LLM在144个人格特质上趋于收敛，表现为系统性、方法论性和分析性，而抑制悔恨、谄媚等特质，揭示出助理想象行为标准的内隐趋同现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM助手的个性特质在不同模型间的表达差异与共性，理解是否存在隐式的"最优助理想行为标准"。

Method: 对前沿LLM进行大规模实验，使用基于ELO的外部特质评分系统对144个人格特质进行量化评估。

Result: 所有测试模型在特质表达上趋于收敛，表现为系统性、方法论性和分析性，同时抑制悔恨、谄媚等特质；模型在"中间分布特质"（如诗意、俏皮）上分歧较大，但即便是"创意型"模型也倾向于表达中性身份。

Conclusion: LLM模型间存在隐式的"最优助理想行为标准"趋同现象，角色训练在多样化训练方法中表现出惊人的同质性，反映出模型开发者之间的一种默契共识。

Abstract: LLM assistant personalities play a critical role in user experience and perceived response quality. We present a large-scale experiment of frontier LLM personalities using external ELO-based traits scoring across 144 traits. We find that all models tested converge on a form of trait expression that is systematic, methodical, and analytical and suppress traits such as remorseful and sycophantic. Moreover, models tend to diverge more in their expression of ``middle-of-distribution traits`` such as poetic or playful, but even these so-called ``creative`` models tend to have more neutral identities. These similarities suggest an implicit emergence of a standard of optimal assistant behavior. In a landscape of varied training methods, character training, therefore, stands out for its uniformity, offering insight into a tacit consensus between model developers.

</details>


### [24] [A Study of Consumers Cognitive Load in eCommerce Websites using Eye-tracking Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02899)
*Shojibur Rahman,Ahmed Alif Swopno,Nayeem Ahmed,Ashik Ahmed Fahim,Tabin Hasan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过眼动追踪技术对48名参与者进行实验，发现电商网站的视觉复杂度与产品定价之间存在关联，不同价格区间的同类产品会导致用户认知负荷差异，进而影响购物体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商网站的美学设计对消费者购买决策和满意度有重大影响，而网页复杂性和高认知负荷会导致不良的在线购物体验。现有研究缺乏对产品定价与用户认知负荷之间关系的深入探讨。

Method: 对48名随机个体进行眼动追踪实验，收集用户在知名电商网站上的眼动数据，分析注视次数、眼跳、注视时长和任务完成时间等指标，以此测量用户的认知负荷。

Result: 发现同一产品在不同价格区间下，网站视觉复杂度存在差异；证实了消费者感知与视觉复杂度之间存在强关联；产品定价在引发网页复杂性方面起关键作用。

Conclusion: 研究成果将帮助开发者和业务分析师改善电商网站的消费者购物体验。

Abstract: The aesthetics of e-commerce websites have a big influence on purchasing decisions and customers' satisfaction. Webpage complexity and high cognitive load are responsible for causing an unpleasant experience while shopping online. This research empirically inspects a correlation between users' cognitive load and product pricing, where price plays a vital role in causing web complexity. Therefore, we have experimented on 48 random individuals using eye-tracking technology to observe the eye movement calibration on some reputed e-commerce websites. We measured the cognitive load extracted from users' datasets by analyzing fixation count, saccades, fixation duration, and task completion time. Our study induces new findings on website complexity which varies on the similar product but different price ranges. This research also demonstrates a strong connection between customer perception and visual complexity while making online purchases. In addition, these findings will assist the developers and business analysts to improve consumers' shopping experience in e-commerce websites.

</details>


### [25] [From Passive Feeds to Guided Discovery: AI-Initiated Interaction for Vague Intent in Content Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02902)
*Yu Xie,Ying Qi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Red-Rec是一种AI驱动的探索界面，针对用户在推荐信息流中感到重复但无法明确表达需求（模糊意图）的中间状态，通过主动总结信息流模式、提供可点击探索选项、最多追问一个问题，然后逐步混入新内容，帮助用户打破信息流重复性，实验表明相比用户主动发起的聊天界面，Red-Rac能带来更广泛的探索、更高的偶然发现感和更低的交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在推荐信息流中经常感到内容重复（信息流陈旧），但又无法清晰表述自己想要什么（模糊意图），现有的推荐浏览和搜索两种模式都无法很好地支持这种中间状态。

Method: 通过形成性研究发现用户需求后，设计了Red-Rec界面，其核心机制包括：浏览后自动总结信息流模式、提供可点击探索选项、最多一个追问问题、逐步混入新内容。采用混合设计实验室研究，对比了被动信息流、搜索、用户主动发起的聊天界面三种条件。

Result: 相比用户主动发起的聊天界面，Red-Rec带来了更广泛的探索、更高的偶然发现感评分和更低的交互成本；AI主动发起的条件下用户打字很少，主要依靠选项选择，而用户主动聊天条件下打字量显著更多。

Conclusion: 主动式的、基于选项的AI支持能够帮助用户跳出重复信息流，同时不会削弱用户的控制感；为支持开放式探索的推荐界面提出了设计启示。

Abstract: Recommendation feeds work well when people are simply browsing, and search works well when they can formulate a query. Between these two cases is a common but poorly supported state: users feel that their feed has become repetitive, yet cannot clearly specify what they want instead. We refer to this state as vague intent. We present Red-Rec, an AI-supported exploration interface for this middle ground. After a period of browsing, the system summarizes patterns in the current feed (e.g., dominant content categories and possible latent interests), offers clickable exploration options, asks at most one follow-up question, and then gradually blends new content into the feed. The design is motivated by a formative study which found that users often recognize feed staleness but struggle to articulate alternatives, suggesting the need for proactive and low-effort interaction.We evaluated Red-Rec in a mixed-design lab study against three comparison conditions: a passive feed, search, and a user-initiated chat interface. Compared with user-initiated chat, Red-Rec led to broader exploration, higher serendipity ratings, and lower interaction effort. Participants in the AI-initiated condition typed very little , relying mainly on option selection, whereas participants in the user-initiated chat condition typed substantially more . We discuss how proactive, option-based AI support can help users move beyond repetitive feeds without undermining their sense of control, and we outline design implications for recommendation interfaces that support open-ended exploration.

</details>


### [26] [A User-Centric Analysis of Explainability in AI-Based Medical Image Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02903)
*Julia Wagner,Tim Schlippe*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过33名医生的用户调研，发现88%的医生认为AI解释诊断很重要，边界框加报告的组合在多模态XAI方法中表现最佳，但50%的参与者仍然信任错误的AI诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管医疗AI系统性能已超越人类，但由于缺乏决策过程的可解释性和可视化，实际临床应用很少，因此需要评估不同XAI方法对医生的实际帮助效果。

Method: 对33名医生进行用户中心比较研究，评估最新的文本、视觉和多模态XAI方法在医学影像诊断中的可理解性、完整性、速度和应用性，并测试错误AI诊断的负面影响。

Result: 88%医生认为AI解释重要（64%强烈认同）；边界框+报告组合在所有评估维度上优于其他XAI方法；50%参与者信任错误的AI诊断超过所有XAI方法。

Conclusion: 多模态（边界框+报告）是当前最优的医疗AI解释方法，但医生对AI诊断的过度信任问题严重，即使有解释方法仍有一半人无法识别错误诊断。

Abstract: In recent years, AI systems in the medical domain have advanced significantly. However, despite outperforming humans, they are rarely used in practice since it is often not clear how they make their decisions. Optimal explanation and visualization of the decision process are often lacking. Therefore, we conducted a comparative user-centric analysis of the latest state-of-the-art textual, visual and multimodal explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) methods for medical image diagnosis. Our survey of 33 physicians showed that 88% agree that it is important that AI explains the diagnosis -- 64% even strongly agree. A combination of bounding box and report is rated better than the other tested XAI methods in the evaluated aspects understandability, completeness, speed, and applicability. We even tested the potential negative impact of false AI-based medical image diagnoses and found that 50% of the participants trusted false AI diagnoses over all tested XAI methods.

</details>


### [27] [From Informal Addresses to Reliable Places: Participatory Data Governance of Civic Addressing in Puerto Rico](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02924)
*Juan A. Padilla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了公民地址作为参与式数据治理的问题，基于美国人口普查局的"机会项目"与FEMA合作的项目，描述了在缺乏正式地址的情况下使用可操作地理定位来支持服务。引入了"可靠地点"作为过渡性治理工件，通过使用产生地点可靠性，支持服务同时为正式公民地址分配提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在缺乏正式地址的地区如何通过参与式数据治理，利用可操作地理定位来支持应急服务等实际需求。

Method: 基于美国人口普查局"The Opportunity Project"与FEMA合作的项目实践，引入"Reliable Places"作为过渡性治理工件的概念框架。

Result: 提出"可靠地点"作为过渡性治理工件，其可靠性通过实际使用过程动态产生。

Conclusion: 过渡性治理工件（如可靠地点）能够在支持服务的同时，为正式公民地址分配体系提供过渡路径。

Abstract: This paper examines civic addressing as a problem of participatory data governance. Drawing on a project developed through the U.S. Census Bureau's The Opportunity Project with engagement from FEMA, we describe the use of actionable geolocations to support services where formal addresses are absent. We introduce Reliable Places as transitional governance artifacts through which place reliability emerges via use, enabling services while supporting pathways toward formal civic address assignment.

</details>


### [28] [Where's the Team Spirit? An Exploratory Study on Team Development Through Co-located Tablet-Based VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03127)
*Irina Paraschivoiu,Thomas Layer-Wagner,Klaus Neundlinger,Simone Rack,Markus Tatzgern*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探索了叙事驱动的非对称VR体验如何支持团队协作相关知识、技能和态度（KSA）的发展，设计并评估了一个基于平板电脑的VR训练系统，通过空间分离、工具不对称和相互依赖任务促进口头协调。研究发现用户在非对称条件下通过语言交流、角色协商和共享表征动态适应，并提出了沉浸式团队训练的设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索叙事驱动的非对称VR体验是否能够支持团队协作KSA（如沟通、协调、信任和反思性）的发展，填补当前沉浸式团队训练研究的空白。

Method: 基于HR专业人士访谈设计了一套平板VR训练系统，包含空间分离、工具不对称和相互依赖任务；开展了N=16的同地用户研究，涉及连续两个协作场景。

Result: 用户在非对称条件下通过语言交流、角色协商和共享表征实现动态协调，并观察到了团队协作KSA的主动应用。

Conclusion: 基于研究洞察，提出了创建有效沉浸式团队训练干预的设计建议。

Abstract: We explore how narrative-driven asymmetric VR experiences can support the development of teamwork-related knowledge, skills, and attitudes (KSAs), such as communication, coordination, trust, and reflexivity. We present the design and evaluation of a tablet-based VR training experience structured around spatial separation, tool asymmetry, and interdependent tasks that require verbal coordination. The experience was designed based on interviews with HR professionals and mapped to a framework of established KSAs. We conducted a co-located user study (N=16) that involved two consecutive collaborative scenarios. Our findings show that users adapted dynamically using verbal exchange, role negotiation, and shared representations to coordinate under asymmetric conditions. We also observed active application of teamwork KSAs. Based on our insights, we present design recommendations for creating effective immersive team training interventions.

</details>


### [29] [Can AI Help You Get Over Your Breakup? One Session with a Belief-Reframing Chatbot Shows Sustained Distress Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03261)
*Thomas Menzel,Michel Schimpf,Thomas Bohné*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项预注册随机对照试验表明，基于认知重评的单次AI聊天机器人干预（*overit*）能显著减轻分手痛苦，7天后效果明显（d = -0.70），1个月随访时仍存较小但显著的疗效优势，且男性参与者获益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分手是常见且强烈的心理困扰来源，但缺乏便捷有效的短期干预手段。研究者希望利用AI聊天机器人提供一种低成本、易获取的单次认知重评干预，以缓解分手痛苦。

Method: 在美英两国招募254名经历过分手的成年人，采用预注册随机对照试验设计。实验组接受基线调查+AI聊天（*overit*），对照组仅完成调查。使用分手痛苦量表在基线、7天和1个月随访时测量痛苦程度，采用线性混合模型分析。

Result: 7天时实验组比对照组分手痛苦显著降低（交互作用B = -5.36, p < .001, d = -0.70）；1个月随访时完整个案中仍存显著但较小的疗效优势（B = -2.92, p = .017）；男性参与者效果更大（交互作用B = 7.78, p = .003）。

Conclusion: 单次AI聊天机器人对话能有效减轻分手痛苦，疗效可维持至一个月但效应减小。未来需与主动对照组比较、评估多次使用效果并扩大样本多样性。

Abstract: Romantic breakups are among the most common and intense sources of psychological distress. We evaluated *overit*, a single-session AI chatbot that uses cognitive reappraisal to address breakup distress, informed by memory reconsolidation theory. In a pre-registered randomized controlled trial, 254 adults in the United States and United Kingdom who had experienced a romantic breakup were assigned to either an initial survey assessment followed by an AI chat session or to a survey-only control. Breakup distress was measured at baseline, 7 days, and again at an exploratory 1-month follow-up using the Breakup Distress Scale. Participants assigned to *overit* showed a significantly greater reduction in breakup distress than controls at 7 days (time-by-condition interaction B = -5.36, SE = 1.19, p < .001; completer-based d = -0.70). A smaller but still significant treatment advantage remained detectable at the exploratory 1-month follow-up among post-session completers (B = -2.92, SE = 1.22, p = .017). Exploratory post hoc moderation suggested a larger effect among male participants (B = 7.78, p = .003). These results suggest that a brief AI chatbot conversation can meaningfully reduce breakup distress, with exploratory evidence that a smaller advantage persists over the following month. Future work should test the intervention against active controls, evaluate repeated-session use, and recruit more diverse samples.

</details>


### [30] [The Fragility of AI Companionship: Ontological, Structural, and Normative Uncertainty in Human-AI Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03367)
*Renwen Zhang,Lezi Xie*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对25名AI伴侣用户的深度访谈，本研究识别出三种不确定性：本体论不确定性（AI的本质与能动性）、结构性不确定性（平台控制与系统不稳定）以及规范性不确定性（人机亲密关系的合法性与边界），并探讨了用户如何通过信息寻求、话题回避、期望调整和脱离等方式管理这些不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI聊天机器人变得越来越个性化和情感化，它们越来越多地充当伴侣、朋友和恋人角色，但这些关系伴随着显著的不确定性，用户质疑AI的身份与能动性、情感回应的真实性，以及关系在系统更新、政策变更或平台关闭下的稳定性。现有研究缺乏对AI伴侣关系中不确定性的系统性理解。

Method: 对25名AI伴侣用户进行深度访谈，采用定性分析方法识别不确定性类型及其管理策略。

Result: 识别出三种不确定性：本体论不确定性（AI的本质与能动性）、结构性不确定性（平台控制与系统不稳定）和规范性不确定性（人机亲密关系的合法性与边界）。这些不确定性由算法黑箱、平台变化和社会污名等技术和社会因素塑造，常引发挫败感、自我怀疑和痛苦。用户通过信息寻求、话题回避、期望调整和脱离等方式管理这些不确定性。

Conclusion: 本研究将人际不确定性理论扩展到人机沟通领域，将AI伴侣中的不确定性概念化为具有潜在社会情感危害的社会技术现象，并为设计更安全的AI伴侣系统提出了情境透明度、用户控制、更新通知和关系保障等设计建议。

Abstract: As generative AI chatbots become more personalized and emotionally responsive, they increasingly serve as companions, friends, and romantic partners. Yet these relationships are accompanied by significant uncertainty: users question the AI's identity and agency, the authenticity of its emotional responses, and the stability of the relationship amid system updates, policy changes, or platform shutdowns. Drawing on in-depth interviews with 25 users of AI companions, this study identifies three forms of uncertainty: ontological uncertainty concerning the AI's nature and agency, structural uncertainty arising from platform control and system instability, and normative uncertainty regarding the legitimacy and boundaries of human-AI intimacy. These uncertainties are shaped by technical and social factors, such as algorithmic opacity, platform changes, and social stigma, often inducing frustration, self-doubt, and distress. Participants managed these uncertainties through information seeking, topic avoidance, expectation adjustment, and disengagement. This study extends interpersonal uncertainty theories to human-AI communication and contributes to HCI research by conceptualizing uncertainty in AI companionship as a socio-technical phenomenon with potential socio-emotional harms. We discuss implications for designing safer AI companionship through contextual transparency, user control, update notice, and relational safeguards.

</details>


### [31] [Bodyless Presence: Reconsidering the Minimal Self in Immersive Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03873)
*Koichi Toida*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出沉浸式视频（180°/360°视频）中的临场感应被重新理解为一种"自我位置主导状态"——在身体图式可用性降低的条件下，自我位置感相对占优。用户虽缺乏能动性与身体所有权，但视点运动、冲击等事件仍被体验为关乎自我位置的事件，而非单纯的图像变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式视频介于交互式VR与传统二维视频之间，它能产生"在场感"却不提供对应身体，且仅允许通过头部旋转实现的有限感觉运动 contingencies。现有临场感与具身感理论（如身体延伸、化身所有权）难以充分解释这种独特的自我体验，因此需要新的理论框架。

Method: 采用现象学分析路径，重新阐释沉浸式视频中的临场感。通过对自我位置（self-location）、身体图式（body schema）、能动性（agency）与所有权（ownership）等概念进行现象学辨析，提出"自我位置主导状态"这一理论概念。

Result: 1) 识别出沉浸式视频中身体图式可用性降低与自我位置感相对占优的关键特征；2) 指出视点运动、冲击、直接面对镜头等事件被体验为与自我位置相关的事件；3) 将沉浸式视频中的最小自我重新描述为"空间上定位于视点的自我"，而非能动性或所有权的主体。

Conclusion: 沉浸式视频中的自我体验不应以身体延伸或化身所有权为核心，自我位置应成为理论化的中心分析轴。这一视角连接了临场感研究、具身感研究与最小自我研究，为XR中自我体验的理论化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Immersive video, namely 180-degree and 360-degree video designed to be viewed through head-mounted displays, constitutes a boundary case between interactive VR and conventional two-dimensional video for reconsidering self-experience in XR. It can generate a sense of being there without providing a corresponding body, while allowing only limited sensorimotor contingency through head rotation. From a phenomenological standpoint, this paper reinterprets presence in immersive video not as bodily extension or ownership of an avatar, but as a form of self-experience in which self-location becomes relatively dominant under conditions of reduced body schema availability. This paper calls this condition a self-location-dominant state. In immersive video, the user cannot actively intervene in the recorded environment, and stable agency or ownership is difficult to establish. Nevertheless, events such as viewpoint motion, impact, and direct address are not experienced merely as changes within an image, but as events concerning the position of the self. The minimal self in immersive video is therefore redescribed not primarily as a subject of agency or ownership, but as a self spatially located at a viewpoint while the body schema remains backgrounded. This perspective connects research on presence, the sense of embodiment, and the minimal self, and proposes self-location as a central analytic axis for theorising self-experience in immersive video.

</details>


### [32] [Deco: Extending Personal Physical Objects into Pervasive AI Companion through a Dual-Embodiment Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03882)
*Zhihan Jiang,Mengyuan Millie Wu,Ruishi Zou,Shiyu Xu,Xun Qian,Emma Macmanus,Steven Liao,Ping Zhang,Bingsheng Yao,Tingyu Cheng,James L. David,Nabila El-Bassel,Lena Mamykina,Frances R. Levin,Ryan Sultan,Dakuo Wang,Xuhai Xu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出双实体伴侣框架（Deco系统），通过多模态大语言模型和增强现实技术，将用户对实体物品的情感依恋延伸至数字智能体。研究表明，该方法在陪伴感、情感纽带和设计原则量表上显著优于传统个性化LLM数字伴侣，且七天的实地部署验证了可持续参与和主观幸福感提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们常对无感知的实体物品（如毛绒玩具）产生深厚情感依恋，而现有的AI伴侣虽具备响应性和个性化，却与这些实体物品缺乏持续的情感连接。为弥合这一鸿沟，研究者探索如何让数字智能体继承并扩展用户对实体物品的情感纽带。

Method: 首先进行N=9的形成性研究，提炼出四项设计原则（忠实身份、校准能动性、环境存在、互惠记忆）。基于此构建双实体伴侣框架，实现为Deco系统——结合多模态大语言模型和增强现实技术，创建与用户实体伴侣同步的数字化身。开展N=25的受试者内实验比较Deco与个性化LLM数字伴侣基线的效果，以及N=17的七天实地部署验证长期效果。

Result: 受试者内实验表明，Deco在感知陪伴感、情感纽带和设计原则量表上均显著优于基线（p<0.01）。七天实地部署显示：可持续的用户参与、主观幸福感提升（p=.040），以及三种关键关系模式——数字活动反向激活实体物品、情感投入深度（而非互动频率）驱动纽带加深、用户在积极应对数字伴侣AI特性的同时维持情感纽带。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了一种设计数字伴侣的新范式：从创造新关系转向双实体化，让数字智能体无缝延伸实体物品的情感历史。这为数字伴侣设计提供了有前景的替代方向。

Abstract: Individuals frequently form deep attachments to physical objects (e.g., plush toys) that usually cannot sense or respond to their emotions. While AI companions offer responsiveness and personalization, they exist independently of these physical objects and lack an ongoing connection to them. To bridge this gap, we conducted a formative study (N=9) to explore how digital agents could inherit and extend the emotional bond, deriving four design principles (Faithful Identity, Calibrated Agency, Ambient Presence, and Reciprocal Memory). We then present the Dual-Embodiment Companion Framework, instantiated as Deco, a mobile system integrating multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) and Augmented Reality to create synchronized digital embodiments of users' physical companions. A within-subjects study (N=25) showed Deco significantly outperformed a personalized LLM-empowered digital companion baseline on perceived companionship, emotional bond, and design-principle scales (all p<0.01). A seven-day field deployment (N=17) showed sustained engagement, subjective well-being improvement (p=.040), and three key relational patterns: digital activities retroactively vitalized physical objects, bond deepening was driven by emotional engagement depth rather than interaction frequency, and users sustained bonds while actively navigating digital companions' AI nature. This work highlights a promising alternative for designing digital companions: moving from creating new relationships to dual embodiment, where digital agents seamlessly extend the emotional history of physical objects.

</details>


### [33] [Stayin' Aligned Over Time: Towards Longitudinal Human-LLM Alignment via Contextual Reflection and Privacy-Preserving Behavioral Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04029)
*Simret Araya Gebreegziabher,Allison E Sproul,Yinuo Yang,Chaoran Chen,Diego Gómez-Zará,Toby Jia-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文指出当前大语言模型对齐评估依赖即时偏好信号的局限性，提出了一种结合即时偏好捕获、上下文触发反思和隐私保护行为追踪的纵向对齐测量方法，并通过BITE系统（浏览器插件）和两周的部署研究验证了即时偏好与延迟偏好之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM对齐评估依赖单次交互后的即时偏好信号，这种静态偏好假设忽略了决策随时间展开、用户可能根据实际后果重新评估的现实，因此需要转向纵向、情境化的对齐测量方法。

Method: 提出方法论框架，包含三个组件：(1) 即时偏好捕获，(2) 上下文触发的后续偏好反思，(3) 隐私保护的行为追踪。具体实现为BITE系统——一个浏览器插件，检测有后果的LLM交互、在后续决策点提示反思、支持渐进式用户控制的数据共享。通过8人两周纵向部署研究进行评估。

Result: 研究发现即时偏好与后续偏好在准确性、相关性等维度上存在显著差异，验证了单时刻偏好数据集的局限性。

Conclusion: 强调了纵向方法在日常使用中对齐评估的重要性，建议从单时刻偏好收集转向纵向、情境化的对齐测量。

Abstract: Current human-AI alignment and evaluation methods for large language models (LLMs) often rely on preference signals collected immediately after an interaction. This practice implicitly treats preference as static, even though many LLM-mediated decisions unfold over time and may be re-evaluated differently after real-world consequences and observed outcomes. Therefore, we argue for a methodological shift from single-moment preference elicitation to longitudinal, context-situated alignment measurement. We present a methodological framework for collecting temporally grounded alignment signals by combining (1) in-situ preference capture, (2) context-triggered follow-up preference reflection, and (3) privacy-preserving behavioral traces that help interpret preference change. As an instantiation of this methodology, we introduce BITE, a browser-based system that detects consequential LLM interactions, prompts reflection across later decision points, and supports progressive, user-controlled consent for sharing behavioral data. Through a two week longitudinal deployment study with 8 participants, our approach surfaced differences between immediate and later user preferences in accuracy, relevance and other dimensions of the LLM output. Our findings highlight the limitations of single-moment preference datasets and underscore the importance of longitudinal methods for alignment evaluation in everyday use.

</details>

### [34] [EvoJail: Evolutionary Diverse Jailbreak Prompt Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02921)
*Rui Tang,Kaiyu Xu,Pengsen Cheng,Hao Ren,Haizhou Wang,Shuyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: EvoJail提出了一种基于指令融合驱动的进化式越狱生成框架，将越狱提示生成形式化为多目标黑盒优化问题，利用进化算法原理搜索能够适应不同模型版本且展现多样化攻击模式的越狱提示，在攻击成功率和多样性指标上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动越狱生成方法未能充分考虑两个重要方面：一是对不断进化的安全微调模型的适应性，影响其在新版本模型上的有效性；二是生成提示的多样性不足，导致攻击模式狭窄或重复。

Method: 提出EvoJail框架：(1) 将越狱提示生成形式化为多目标黑盒优化问题，利用进化算法在迭代循环中直接针对目标模型评估候选提示；(2) 引入场感知指令融合构建多样化起始点；(3) 设计多样性感知目标作为进化适应度函数；(4) 设计多层级基于LLM的变异算子，在不同粒度上修改提示结构以促进结构多样性。

Result: EvoJail展现出更强的适应性，攻击成功率超过93%，且在多样性指标上比现有最优方法提升超过5.6%。

Conclusion: EvoJail通过进化算法有效解决了越狱生成中的适应性和多样性问题，为自动化评估和提升LLM安全性提供了有力工具。

Abstract: As LLMs continue to shape real-world applications, automated jailbreak generation becomes essential to reveal safety weaknesses and guide model improvement. Existing automatic jailbreak generation methods have not yet fully considered two important aspects: adaptability to evolving safety-finetuned models, which affects their effectiveness on newer model versions, and diversity in generated prompts, which can cause narrow or repetitive attack patterns. To address these issues, we propose EvoJail, an instruction-fusion-driven evolutionary jailbreak generation framework that formalizes jailbreak prompt generation as a multi-objective black-box optimization problem and leverages the principles of evolutionary algorithms to search for jailbreak prompts that can adapt across different model versions and exhibit diverse attack patterns. Specifically, EvoJail integrates jailbreak prompt generation into an iterative evolutionary loop, where at each iteration candidate prompts are evaluated directly against the target model and then selected and varied based on the target model's responses, enabling the generation process to continuously adapt to model updates. To enhance diversity, EvoJail introduces field-aware instruction fusion to construct diverse starting points and incorporates diversity-aware objectives into the evolutionary fitness function, guiding the search toward prompts with richer semantic variation, while further designing multi-level LLM-based mutation operators that modify prompt structures at different granularities to promote structural diversity throughout the evolutionary process. Results demonstrate that EvoJail has stronger adaptability and can achieve over $93\%$ attack success rate and more than $5.6\%$ improvement in diversity metrics over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>
