### [35] [The Algebra of Iterative Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03176)
*Kevin Batz,Benjamin Lucien Kaminski,Lucas Kehrer,Gerwin Klein,Henning Urbat,Todd Schmid*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了迭代构造代数（AIC），一个纯粹代数的方法来推理完全格上连续自映射的不动点迭代。AIC通过等式逻辑推导构造性不动点定理，避免了显式索引计算。作者展示了AIC在多个不动点定理上的应用，包括Tarski-Kantorovich原理和k-归纳法，并提出了一个新的不动点定理。该代数已在Isabelle/HOL中机械化实现，且Sledgehammer工具能自动找到证明。此外，作者还研究了AIC公理化的完备性，证明了有限个有穷公理对标准模型是不完备的，而无穷公理集是完备的；并证明了无穷公理是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不动点是计算机科学中的核心概念，通常通过种子初始化的不动点迭代构造。现有方法依赖显式索引计算，缺乏纯粹的代数推理框架。作者希望发展一个纯代数的方法，使得不动点定理的推导能通过等式逻辑完成，从而简化推理过程并便于机械化验证。

Method: 提出了迭代构造代数（AIC），一个基于等式逻辑的代数系统，用于推理完全格上连续自映射的不动点迭代。AIC通过代数运算（如◇）编码不动点构造，避免了显式索引。作者将其在Isabelle/HOL中机械化，并使用Sledgehammer工具自动搜索证明。同时，通过模型论方法研究了公理化的完备性问题。

Result: (1) AIC能代数地证明多个不动点定理，包括Kleene不动点定理、Tarski-Kantorovich原理、k-归纳法，以及一个基于极限下确界和极限上确界的新不动点定理。(2) Isabelle/HOL的Sledgehammer能全自动找到证明。(3) 有穷公理集是可靠但不完备的；无穷公理集是完备的；且不存在有限个有穷公理的完备公理化。

Conclusion: AIC为推理不动点迭代提供了一个简洁、代数化的框架，避免了显式索引和复杂的归纳论证。该方法具有通用性，能涵盖多种不动点定理，并且适合机械化证明。同时，完备性结果为AIC的公理化基础提供了深刻的理论洞见。

Abstract: Fixed points are a recurring theme in computer science and are often constructed as limits of suitably seeded fixed point iterations. We present the algebra of iterative constructions (AIC) -- a purely algebraic approach to reasoning about fixed point iterations of continuous endomaps on complete lattices. AIC allows derivations of constructive fixed point theorems via equational logic and avoids explicit computations with indices. For example, $$F \,\Diamond\, F^{*} \bot = \Diamond\, F^{*} \bot$$ states in AIC that $\sup_n F^n (\bot)$ -- a construction known from the Kleene fixed point theorem -- is a fixed point of $F$. We demonstrate the applicability of AIC by providing algebraic proofs of several well- and less-well-known fixed point theorems: Among others, we prove the Tarski-Kantorovich principle -- a generalization of the Kleene fixed point theorem -- as well as a fixed point-theoretic generalization of $k$-induction --a technique used in software verification.
  We moreover present a novel fixed point theorem. Under suitable continuity conditions, it obtains fixed points as lattice-theoretic limit inferiors and limit superiors of iterating an endomap on an arbitrary seed element. We have mechanized our algebra in Isabelle/HOL. Isabelle's sledgehammer tool is able to find proofs of the above fixed point theorems fully automatically.
  Finally, we investigate the completeness of our axiomatization of AIC. We prove that our finite set of finitary axioms is (a) sound but incomplete for standard models of AIC (sequences of elements from a complete lattice) and that (b) a different finite set of infinitary axioms is complete. We also prove that infinitary axioms are unavoidable: there exists no complete axiomatization of standard models given by finitely many finitary axioms.

</details>


### [36] [A Fast Model Counting Algorithm for Two-Variable Logic with Counting and Modulo Counting Quantifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03391)
*Shixin Sun,Astrid Klipfel,Ondřej Kuželka,Yuanhong Wang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出 IncrementalWFOMC3，一种直接在 Scott 范式上处理计数量词的有界一阶模型计数算法，将 $\mathbf{C}^2$ 的数据复杂度从二次降至线性，首次证明 $\mathbf{C}^2_{\text{mod}}$ 是域可提升的，并在实验中获得数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 $\mathbf{C}^2$ 的 WFOMC 算法通过多阶段归约消除计数量词，引入基数约束导致域规模增大时计算开销过大，且 $\mathbf{C}^2_{\text{mod}}$ 的域可提升性尚未得到证明。

Method: 提出 IncrementalWFOMC3 算法，直接对保留计数量词的 Scott 范式进行操作，避免归约消除步骤；通过增量式推理直接处理计数量词，并扩展到支持模计数的 $\mathbf{C}^2_{\text{mod}}$。

Result: $\mathbf{C}^2$ 的 WFOMC 数据复杂度从二次降至线性（关于计数参数）；$\mathbf{C}^2_{\text{mod}}$ 被证明是域可提升的；实验表明相比现有 WFOMC 算法和命题模型计数器，获得数量级的运行时提升和更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 直接处理计数量词的增量式方法能显著提升 $\mathbf{C}^2$ 的 WFOMC 效率，并将域可提升性扩展至带有模计数支持的 $\mathbf{C}^2_{\text{mod}}$ 片段。

Abstract: Weighted first-order model counting (WFOMC) is a central task in lifted probabilistic inference: It asks for the weighted sum of all models of a first-order sentence over a finite domain. A long line of work has identified domain-liftable fragments of first-order logic, that is, syntactic classes for which WFOMC can be solved in time polynomial in the domain size. Among them, the two-variable fragment with counting quantifiers, $\mathbf{C}^2$, is one of the most expressive known liftable fragments. Existing algorithms for $\mathbf{C}^2$, however, establish tractability through multi-stage reductions that eliminate counting quantifiers via cardinality constraints, which introduces substantial practical overhead as the domain size grows. In this paper, we introduce IncrementalWFOMC3, a lifted algorithm for WFOMC on $\mathbf{C}^2$ and its modulo counting extension, $\mathbf{C}^2_{\text{mod}}$. Instead of relying on reduction techniques, IncrementalWFOMC3 operates directly on a Scott normal form that retains counting quantifiers throughout inference. This direct treatment yields two main results. First, we derive a tighter data-complexity bound for WFOMC in $\mathbf{C}^2$, reducing the degree of the polynomial from quadratic to linear in the counting parameters. Second, we prove that $\mathbf{C}^2_{\text{mod}}$ is domain-liftable, extending tractability from $\mathbf{C}^2$ to a richer fragment with native modulo counting support. Finally, our empirical evaluation shows that IncrementalWFOMC3 delivers orders-of-magnitude runtime improvements and better scalability than both existing WFOMC algorithms and state-of-the-art propositional model counters.

</details>


### [37] [A formulation of D-institution using functor categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03597)
*Go Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出在制度理论中通过泛化函子范畴直接引入变量结构，而非传统的签名态射扩展方式，定义了谓词逻辑范畴并将复合句引入形式化为函子，同时引入证明系统并证明了完备性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统制度理论通过签名态射扩展来处理变量，虽然看似简单，但需要大量条件来描述变量结构，使讨论变得复杂。本文旨在通过直接引入变量结构来简化这一过程。

Method: 利用函子范畴的泛化直接引入变量结构，定义谓词逻辑范畴，将复合句的引入形式化为函子，并引入相应的证明系统。

Result: 成功定义了包含直接变量结构的谓词逻辑范畴，并建立了相应的证明系统，证明了完备性定理。

Conclusion: 直接引入变量结构的方法比传统的签名态射扩展方式更为简洁有效，能够简化对变量结构的讨论。

Abstract: Variables are a crucial element in logic and are also addressed in institution theory, an effort to axiomatize logic. In institution theory, we typically use extensions (signature morphisms) obtained from variables instead of introducing variables directly. While this approach appears simple at first glance because it does not introduce new structures, it often requires numerous conditions to describe variable structures, which can actually complicate the discussion. In this paper, we propose introducing variable structures directly by utilizing a generalization of category of functors. We define a category of predicate logics and formulate the introduction of compound sentences as a functor. We also introduce a proof system and prove a completeness theorem.

</details>


### [38] [Set-like operations on propositional logic programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03613)
*Christian Antić*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出了一套针对命题Horn逻辑程序的集合式操作框架，允许对规则体进行结构化操作。主要技术成果是证明了每个最小模型程序可分解为Krom程序（每条规则至多一个体原子），并能从各组件的 least model 重构原程序的 least model；对于任意程序，则给出近似结果。这为逻辑程序的组合推理与模块化构造提供了新的代数基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏一个系统的代数框架来组合和分解逻辑程序，这限制了以模块化方式分析和构建程序的能力。

Method: 引入针对命题Horn逻辑程序的集合式操作，允许结构化操作规则体；通过将程序分解为Krom程序（每条规则最多一个体原子）的组件，从组件的 least model 重建或近似原程序的 least model。

Result: 每个最小模型程序均可分解为Krom程序，且其 least model 可从各组件的 least model 精确重构；对于任意程序，给出了相应的近似结果。

Conclusion: 为逻辑程序提供了新的代数视角，为组合推理和程序构造奠定了基础。

Abstract: A systematic algebraic framework for composing and decomposing logic programs is currently missing, limiting our ability to analyze and construct programs in a modular way. In this paper, we introduce set-like operations for (propositional Horn) logic programs that allow for a structured manipulation of rule bodies. Our main technical result shows that programs can be decomposed into simpler components in such a way that their least model semantics can be reconstructed or approximated from the semantics of these components. In particular, we prove that every minimalist program can be decomposed into Krom programs -- consisting only of rules with at most one body atom -- such that its least model can be computed from the least models of its components. For arbitrary programs, we obtain corresponding approximation results. These results provide a new algebraic perspective on logic programs and lay the groundwork for compositional reasoning and program construction.

</details>


### [39] [Induction rules for Transition Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03628)
*Go Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过限制(Star)规则为归纳并使用相继式证明系统，为Transition Algebra构建了一个紧致的证明系统，并引入了使其完备的语义学，进而给出了Craig插值的一个模型论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transition Algebra的现有自然演绎证明系统虽对可数签名完备，但缺乏紧致性，不适合实际应用。因此需要创建一个紧致的证明系统。

Method: 1) 将(Star)规则限制为归纳形式；2) 采用相继式证明系统替代自然演绎；3) 引入新的语义学以确保该系统的完备性。

Result: 成功构建了一个紧致的证明系统，并利用新语义学给出了Craig插值定理的模型论证明。

Conclusion: 通过限制规则和改用相继式系统，Transition Algebra获得了紧致性，使其更适用于实际应用，且新语义学支持模型论意义上的插值定理证明。

Abstract: Transition Algebra (TA) is a type of infinite logic introduced to discuss rewriting systems. The natural deductive proof systems already introduced in TA satisfy completeness for countable signatures. However, it lacks compactness, making it unsuitable for practical applications. This time, we will create a compact proof system by restricting the (Star) rule to induction. We will also use a sequent proof system instead of natural deduction. Furthermore, we will introduce a semantics that makes this system complete, and its application (a model-theoretic proof of Craig interpolation).

</details>


### [40] [Backtrackable Inprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03654)
*Alexander Nadel*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出了一种回溯式处理（BI）框架，允许在增量SAT求解过程中任意决策层级下应用处理技术，突破了传统只能在全局决策层级进行处理的限制，在BMC基准测试中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统增量SAT求解中，处理技术（inprocessing）只能在全局决策层级（decision level 0）进行，这严重限制了其在求解过程中的有效性。作者希望打破这一限制，使处理技术能在任意决策层级下应用，从而更充分地发挥其潜力。

Method: 提出Backtrackable Inprocessing (BI)框架，主要围绕三种高效核心技术：包含消去（subsumption）、自包含消解（self-subsuming resolution）和有界变量消去（BVE）。通过确保处理过程中的回溯正确性，实现在当前trail的任意决策层级、增量SAT求解的任意时刻应用处理技术。

Result: 在IntelSAT的分支Island SAT求解器上实现BI，基于HWMCC 2017的BMC基准测试，BI相比基线全局层级增量预处理器能解决约1.5倍更多的困难边界（bounds）。

Conclusion: BI框架成功打破了增量SAT求解中处理技术只能用于全局决策层级的长期限制，显著提升了增量预处理的效果，特别在假设传播后的增量预处理场景下表现出色。

Abstract: We introduce Backtrackable Inprocessing (BI), a framework that enables applying inprocessing under the current trail at any decision level, at any point during incremental SAT solving. Our approach lifts the long-standing restriction that inprocessing must be performed only at the global decision level, thereby substantially increasing its potential effectiveness. We focus on three highly efficient core techniques: subsumption, self-subsuming resolution, and Bounded Variable Elimination (BVE). We show how to ensure sound backtracking in the presence of inprocessing, and demonstrate that applying BI for incremental preprocessing after propagating assumptions yields significant performance improvements on Bounded Model Checking (BMC) benchmarks from the Hardware Model Checking Competition 2017. Implemented in the Island SAT solver (IntelSAT's fork), BI enables solving $\sim$1.5$\times$ as many difficult bounds as the baseline global-level incremental preprocessor.

</details>

### [41] [DITRON: Distributed Multi-level Tiling Compiler for Parallel Tensor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02953)
*Size Zheng,Xuegui Zheng,Hanshi Sun,Qi Hou,Wenlei Bao,Shiyu Li,Haojie Duanmu,Jin Fang,Chenli Xue,Chenhui Huang,Yuanqiang Liu,Renze Chen,Ningxin Zheng,Dongyang Wang,Li-Wen Chang,Liqiang Lu,Yun Liang,Jidong Zhai,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出DITRON，一个可扩展的tile级编译器，通过引入Core/Device/Task三层分层编程抽象，弥合了高性能库（如CuBLAS/NCCL）与张量编译器之间的鸿沟。实验表明，DITRON在独立kernel上实现6%-30%加速，在vLLM端到端推理中实现5%-30%加速，并在训练任务中提升10%以上的MFU，每月节省约50万GPU小时训练成本，已在企业级部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM扩展受限于分布式编程的僵化性：高性能库（CuBLAS/NCCL）缺乏灵活性，无法适应快速演变的模型架构；现有张量编译器不能有效处理分布式集群的复杂内存层次结构，亟需一种兼顾灵活性与高性能的分布式kernel开发方案。

Method: DITRON提出了一种跨Core（核心）、Device（设备）、Task（任务）三个层级的分层编程抽象，将张量程序高效映射到异构分布式硬件上。该抽象在屏蔽节点间和节点内通信复杂性的同时，支持多种并行化策略。

Result: 在大型集群上评估：独立kernel加速6%-30%，vLLM端到端推理加速5%-30%；训练任务MFU提升超10%，每月节省约50万GPU小时；同时支持NVIDIA和AMD平台。

Conclusion: DITRON成功实现了兼顾灵活性与高性能的分布式kernel开发，达到了与专家手工调优CUDA库相当甚至更优的性能，并在企业级训练和推理场景中取得了显著的成本节约和性能提升。

Abstract: The scaling of large language models (LLMs) is currently bottlenecked by the rigidity of distributed programming. While high-performance libraries like CuBLAS and NCCL provide optimized primitives, they lack the flexibility required for rapidly evolving model architectures. Conversely, existing tensor compilers fail to address the complex memory hierarchy of distributed clusters effectively. To bridge this gap, we propose DITRON, a scalable tile-level compiler that democratizes high-performance distributed kernel development. DITRON introduces a novel hierarchical programming abstraction spanning Core, Device, and Task levels to map tensor programs efficiently onto heterogeneous distributed hardware. This abstraction allows DITRON to support diverse parallelism strategies while abstracting away the complexity of inter-node and intra-node communication.
  Evaluated across large-scale clusters, DITRON achieves performance parity with or exceeding expert-tuned CUDA libraries, delivering speedups of $6\%-30\%$ on isolated kernels and $5\%-30\%$ on end-to-end inference in vLLM. Furthermore, DITRON demonstrates strong portability, achieving significant speedups on both NVIDIA and AMD platforms. \ours{} has been deployed at the enterprise level for both training and inference. It achieves an MFU improvement of over 10\% in training tasks, saving approximately 500,000 GPU hours of training cost per month. For inference tasks, it delivers an end-to-end gain of over 20\% and has been applied to cloud service inference and edge inference scenarios.

</details>


### [42] [Towards Definitional Interpreters for Hoare Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02963)
*Ke Sun,Di Wang,Yuyan Bao,Meng Wang,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文探索了将内在定义解释器（基于类型推导而非抽象语法树）扩展到霍尔逻辑推导的方法，提出了“入口索引”（entry-indexing）技术，并在Rocq中实现了两种霍尔逻辑解释器：一个基础版本和一个包含堆、动态帧、良基函数和行为子类型的现实版本。后者首次形式化了具有动态帧、良基函数、行为子类型和完全正确性的霍尔逻辑，也是首个完全机械化的动态帧霍尔逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在定义解释器已被证明是定义编程语言动态语义的有效方法论，其关键优势在于类型安全性能够交互式地指导和约束解释器的构建。类比于类型关系，霍尔逻辑被广泛用于保证程序正确性。本文的核心动机是探究是否能够实现内在定义解释器，使其在霍尔逻辑推导上运行，从而将这种方法论的优势扩展到程序正确性证明领域。

Method: 本文采用内在定义解释器的方法论，在Rocq定理证明器中开发了两种霍尔逻辑解释器。核心技术创新是“入口索引”（entry-indexing）技术，用于解释完全正确性推导和良基函数。第一个解释器针对基础霍尔逻辑，第二个则扩展到了包含堆、动态帧（dynamic frames）的局部推理、良基函数和行为子类型的现实逻辑系统。

Result: 成功在Rocq中构建了两个霍尔逻辑的内在定义解释器。第二个开发成果是：据作者所知，首次形式化了结合动态帧、良基函数、行为子类型和完全正确性的霍尔逻辑，同时也是首个完全机械化的动态帧霍尔逻辑。

Conclusion: 内在定义解释器的方法论可以成功地从类型系统领域扩展到霍尔逻辑领域，入口索引技术是实现这一扩展的关键。这项工作为程序正确性证明的机械化提供了新的方法论基础，展示了内在定义方法在程序逻辑中的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Intrinsic definitional interpreters, definitional interpreters that operate on typing derivations instead of abstract syntax trees, have recently been studied as a promising methodology for defining dynamic semantics of programming languages. A key benefit is that type safety interactively guides and constrains the interpreter's construction. Analogously to typing relations, Hoare logic is widely used to guarantee program correctness. Can intrinsic definitional interpreters be realized to operate over Hoare-logic derivations?
  We explore this question in depth by developing definitional interpreters in Rocq for (i) a basic Hoare logic, and (ii) a realistic logic featuring heaps, dynamic-frame-based local reasoning, well-founded functions, and behavioral subtyping. Central to our approach is a novel technique we call entry-indexing, which we use to interpret total-correctness derivations and well-founded functions. Our second development yields, to our knowledge, the first formalization of a dynamic-frame-based Hoare logic with well-founded functions, behavioral subtyping, and total correctness, as well as the first fully mechanized Hoare logic with dynamic frames.

</details>


### [43] [Pact: A Choreographic Language for Agentic Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03143)
*Kiran Gopinathan,Jack Feser,Michelangelo Naim,Zenna Tavares,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pact，一种将博弈论融入编排编程的语言，用于设计多智能体系统中自利参与者遵循协议的协议。Pact协议可映射为形式化博弈，支持协议设计者推理博弈论属性并计算决策策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编排编程假设参与者完全合作，缺乏对智能体自利行为的建模能力。在多智能体开放系统中，参与者可能出于自身利益不遵循协议，因此需要一种能描述智能体选择与偏好的协议设计方法。

Method: Pact将编排编程语言扩展了描述智能体选择与偏好的操作，借鉴博弈论。每个Pact协议映射为形式化博弈，支持协议设计者推理博弈论属性（如求解决策策略）。实现了一个有界理性求解器，用于计算Pact协议上的决策策略。

Result: 将Pact语言应用于多方的自利智能体参与者协调场景，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: Pact通过融合编排编程与博弈论，为自利智能体参与的多方协议提供了正确性保障的设计方法，实现了协议设计与博弈论推理的统一。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have led to the rise of software systems (i.e. agents) that execute with increasing autonomy on behalf of users in open, multi-party settings, interacting with untrusted counterparts and managing private information. Choreographic programming offers correct-by-construction protocol-design for such settings, but assumes cooperative participants -- it has no notion of agent self-interest, that is, why an agent will follow a protocol.
  In this talk we introduce Pact, a choreographic language extended with operations to describe agent choices and preferences, drawing from the rich literature of game theory. Every Pact protocol maps to a formal game, allowing protocol designers to reason about game-theoretic properties of their protocols, such as solving for decision policies. We present Pact's design and a preliminary implementation -- a bounded-rational solver that computes decision policies over Pact protocols -- and findings from applying this language to multi-party coordination with self-interested agentic participants.

</details>
