### [44] [Safety in Embodied AI: A Survey of Risks, Attacks, and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02900)
*Xiao Li,Xiang Zheng,Yifeng Gao,Xinyu Xia,Yixu Wang,Xin Wang,Ye Sun,Yunhan Zhao,Ming Wen,Jiayu Li,Xun Gong,Yi Liu,Yige Li,Yutao Wu,Cong Wang,Jun Sun,Yixin Cao,Zhineng Chen,Jingjing Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Zuxuan Wu,Xipeng Qiu,Xuanjing Huang,Tiehua Zhang,Zhipeng Wei,Hanxun Huang,Sarah Erfani,James Bailey,Jianping Wang,Wei-Ying Ma,Bo Li,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了具身人工智能（Embodied AI）的安全研究，涵盖从感知、认知、规划到行动与交互的完整具身管线，提出了统一的多级分类法，并识别了多模态感知融合、规划鲁棒性、人机交互可信度等关键挑战与研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身AI在交通、医疗、工业、辅助机器人等安全关键领域获得自主性，确保其安全性在技术上具有挑战性且对社会至关重要。与数字AI系统不同，具身智能体需在不确定感知、不完备知识和动态人机交互下运行，故障可直接导致物理伤害，因此亟需对安全研究进行系统梳理。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，对400余篇论文进行综合梳理，提出多级分类法统一碎片化工作，将具身特定安全发现与视觉、语言、多模态基础模型的更广泛进展相联系，涵盖对抗攻击、后门攻击、越狱攻击、硬件攻击及其检测、安全训练、鲁棒推理和风险感知的人机交互。

Result: 揭示了若干被忽视的挑战：多模态感知融合的脆弱性、越狱攻击下规划的不稳定性、开放场景中人机交互的可信度问题。建立了统一框架并识别了关键研究空白。

Conclusion: 为构建不仅具备能力和自主性，而且在真实部署中安全、鲁棒、可靠的具身智能体提供了路线图。

Abstract: Embodied Artificial Intelligence (Embodied AI) integrates perception, cognition, planning, and interaction into agents that operate in open-world, safety-critical environments. As these systems gain autonomy and enter domains such as transportation, healthcare, and industrial or assistive robotics, ensuring their safety becomes both technically challenging and socially indispensable. Unlike digital AI systems, embodied agents must act under uncertain sensing, incomplete knowledge, and dynamic human-robot interactions, where failures can directly lead to physical harm. This survey provides a comprehensive and structured review of safety research in embodied AI, examining attacks and defenses across the full embodied pipeline, from perception and cognition to planning, action and interaction, and agentic system. We introduce a multi-level taxonomy that unifies fragmented lines of work and connects embodied-specific safety findings with broader advances in vision, language, and multimodal foundation models. Our review synthesizes insights from over 400 papers spanning adversarial, backdoor, jailbreak, and hardware-level attacks; attack detection, safe training and robust inference; and risk-aware human-agent interaction. This analysis reveals several overlooked challenges, including the fragility of multimodal perception fusion, the instability of planning under jailbreak attacks, and the trustworthiness of human-agent interaction in open-ended scenarios. By organizing the field into a coherent framework and identifying critical research gaps, this survey provides a roadmap for building embodied agents that are not only capable and autonomous but also safe, robust, and reliable in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [45] [Tracing the Dynamics of Refusal: Exploiting Latent Refusal Trajectories for Robust Jailbreak Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02958)
*Xulin Hu,Che Wang,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Jianbo Gao,Zhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SALO方法，通过发现拒绝行为的动态稀疏轨迹（拒绝轨迹），即使在对抗攻击压制终端信号时仍能检测，将强制解码攻击的检测率从~0%提升至>90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示工程（Representation Engineering）依赖静态拒绝向量，但对抗攻击（如GCG）可压制终端表示信号，导致防御失效。作者旨在揭示拒绝行为的动态本质，并开发不受终端信号压制影响的检测方法。

Method: 1) 使用因果追踪（Causal Tracing）发现"拒绝轨迹"（Refusal Trajectory）——一种持久的上游签名，在对抗攻击下仍保持完整。2) 提出SALO（稀疏激活定位算子），一种推理时检测器，用于捕获这些潜在的模式以检测拒绝行为。

Result: 在强制解码攻击场景下，SALO将检测率从依赖终端状态方法的~0%提升至>90%，有效恢复防御能力。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为是一个动态且稀疏的过程而非局部化结果；通过捕获上游拒绝轨迹，可以在对抗攻击压制终端信号时仍维持高效检测，为LLM安全防御提供了新范式。

Abstract: Representation Engineering typically relies on static refusal vectors derived from terminal representations. We move beyond this paradigm, demonstrating that refusal is a dynamic and sparse process rather than a localized outcome. Using Causal Tracing, we uncover the Refusal Trajectory-a persistent upstream signature that remains intact even when adversarial attacks (e.g., GCG) suppress terminal signals. Leveraging this, we propose SALO (Sparse Activation Localization Operator), an inference-time detector designed to capture these latent patterns. SALO effectively recovers defense capabilities against forced-decoding attacks, improving detection rates from ~0% to >90% where methods relying on terminal states perform poorly.

</details>


### [46] [Decompose to Understand, Fuse to Detect: Frequency-Decoupled Anomaly Detection for Encrypted Network Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02970)
*Xinglin Lian,Chengtai Cao,Ting Zhong,Yong Wang,Kai Chen,Fan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次发现加密流量图像化方法中存在"全频特征"与"频谱失配"问题，并提出频率解耦框架FreeUp，通过分离高低频处理分支、不确定性融合评分机制，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密流量异常检测方法将流量建模为视觉模式时，普遍存在对高频信息的学习偏差——主流重建方法倾向于学习低频信息，导致表示不完整，影响异常检测性能。

Method: 提出FreeUp框架：(1) 将流量数据解耦为低频和高频频带，通过独立分支分别处理；(2) 设计定制化训练策略确保频率特异性学习的稳定性和独立性；(3) 引入基于不确定性启发的融合评分机制，量化各分支重建不确定性并动态整合输出。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，FreeUp整体优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过频率解耦和不确定性融合，有效解决了加密流量图像化方法中的频谱失配问题，提升了异常检测的全面性和可靠性。

Abstract: Network traffic anomaly detection represents a critical cybersecurity task, yet widespread encryption makes this task increasingly challenging. In response, image-based methods that model traffic as visual patterns have emerged as the dominant approach. However, this work pioneers the identification of a pervasive ``full-frequency'' characteristic and an associated limitation termed ``spectral mismatch'' within this paradigm. Specifically, while encrypted traffic exhibits prominent high-frequency components, mainstream reconstruction methods demonstrate an inherent bias toward learning low-frequency information. This fundamental mismatch results in incomplete representations that consequently degrade anomaly detection performance. To address this challenge, we propose FreeUp, a novel frequency-decoupled framework designed explicitly for encrypted traffic analysis. FreeUp decomposes traffic data into distinct low- and high-frequency bands, processing them through separate, dedicated branches along with a customized training strategy that ensures stable and independent frequency-specific learning. Furthermore, recognizing that simple reconstruction error proves inadequate for evaluating dual-branch architectures, we introduce an uncertainty-inspired fusion scoring mechanism. This mechanism quantifies the reconstruction uncertainty of the frequency-specific branches and dynamically integrates their outputs, yielding a more comprehensive and reliable anomaly score. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FreeUp consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/ikun0124/FreeUp.

</details>


### [47] [Contrastive Privacy: A Semantic Approach to Measuring Privacy of AI-based Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02977)
*George Bissias,Eugene Bagdasarian,Brian Neil Levine*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出对比隐私（contrastive privacy），一种形式化的隐私定义，用于系统、定量地评估经过语义消敏处理的媒体（图像和文本），无需手动标注，且独立于具体模型和机制。实验在34种图像模型组合和15种文本模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于AI的语义级隐私消敏方法缺乏形式化保证和系统评估标准，评估往往是定制的、非正式的。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一种有形式化保障的、通用的隐私定义与测试方法。

Method: 提出对比隐私定义，在理想条件下提供形式化保证；利用CLIP等模型实现语义的隐式度量，将消敏后的媒体与同语料库中其他图像进行对比判断，无需手动标注。该方法跨模态（图像和文本）适用，与消敏机制无关。

Result: 在34种图像模型组合和15种文本模型上进行了实验，量化了消敏成功率，并能识别出消敏语料库中的特定失败案例。

Conclusion: 对比隐私提供了一种独立于模型和机制的形式化隐私测试方法，能够系统评估语义级消敏效果，并为未来隐私保护研究提供了可量化的评估框架。

Abstract: To sanitize specific concepts from imagery and text, privacy mechanisms with formal guarantees are often eschewed in practice in favor of more intuitive techniques. AI-based sanitization is poised to grow in popularity because it can work with the semantics of natural language concepts; e.g., a prompt to "remove faces, clothing, and body shape". Many approaches exist commercially and as prior work. But, the evaluation of such approaches has been bespoke and without formal guarantees.
  To fill this gap, we propose contrastive privacy, a formal definition of privacy that provides a systematic and quantitative test of sanitized media that has a semantic interpretation. It is independent of the model and mechanism used and operates across multiple media modalities. Contrastive privacy provides guarantees under ideal conditions; and we show how to operationalize the definition with imperfect measures of semantics, provided by models like CLIP, that can connect concepts latently. Notably, the algorithm contrasts sanitized media with other images from the same corpus to arrive at a determination; no manual labeling is involved.
  In our experiments, we apply our privacy test to both images and text using frontier models: some generate concepts to sanitize and others perform the sanitization. With our test we quantify sanitization success across 34 combinations of models on images, and for 15 models on text. The approach not only quantifies success overall, it identifies specific failures from a sanitized corpus. Further, it is independent of the mechanism used for sanitization, whether by darkening pixels, blurring, or applying more advanced means of obfuscation.

</details>


### [48] [Observability for Post-Quantum TLS Readiness: A Multi-Surface Evidence Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02978)
*José Luis Delgado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向后量子TLS的多层面观测框架，将被动会话证据、主动探测、证书链证据和注册表知识分离并映射到多个测量平面，在29个受控场景和1000个目标的公开活动中验证了其有效性，能够发现混合能力等单会话视角无法观测的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS后量子迁移需要具备证据感知的测量方法，以区分会话协商、端点能力、证书链证据和缺失观测的来源，而现有方法在TLS 1.3加密、恢复、mTLS、截断、分片、合并、主动证书检索和时间漂移等复杂场景下难以满足这一需求。

Method: 提出多层面框架，分离被动会话证据、主动探测、证书链证据和注册表知识，映射到会话行为、密钥建立、端点能力、认证、生命周期、可观测性和策略等测量平面；实现为可复现工件，包含模式约束的观测与结果、版本化注册表、可审计推理规则、压力合约和基线适配器。

Result: 在29个受控场景中，被动证据闭合会话级平面，主动探测建立能力下界，多层面证据闭合完整测量对象；基线量子漏洞分析器仅检测到29次中的2次（TLS 1.3为0次）；公开活动中完成1971次握手，收集1368个链工件，确认310个目标的混合能力，发现310个端点能力超出单会话视图的情况。

Conclusion: 多层面观测框架能够更全面地刻画后量子TLS迁移状态，揭示单会话视角无法获取的端点能力信息，同时保留必要的不确定性和矛盾信息，为后量子TLS的可观测性提供了系统性方法论和可复现工具。

Abstract: Post-quantum migration in Transport Layer Security (TLS) requires evidence-aware measurements that distinguish session negotiation, endpoint capability, certificate-chain evidence, and the provenance of missing observations. This distinction is essential under TLS 1.3 encryption, resumption, mutual TLS, trace truncation, fragmentation, coalescing, active certificate retrieval, and temporal drift.
  We present a multi-surface framework for post-quantum TLS observability. The framework separates passive session evidence, active probing, certificate-chain evidence, and registry knowledge, and maps them onto measurement planes for session behavior, key establishment, endpoint capability, authentication, lifecycle, observability, and policy. We instantiate it as a reproducible artifact with schema-enforced observations and results, versioned registries, auditable inference rules, stress contracts, and baseline adapters.
  We evaluate the framework on 29 controlled scenarios spanning TLS 1.2 and TLS 1.3, classical and hybrid key establishment, mutual TLS, resumption, HelloRetryRequest, truncation, fragmentation and coalescing, temporal drift, IPv6, and chain-depth variation. Passive evidence closes session-level planes, active probing establishes capability lower bounds, and multi-surface evidence closes the full measurement object while preserving uncertainty and contradiction when required. Against an inherited TLS quantum-vulnerability analyzer, the baseline detects 2 of 29 runs and 0 of 23 TLS 1.3 runs. In a stratified public campaign over 1000 targets and 2000 fresh probes, the framework completes 1971 handshakes, collects 1368 chain artifacts, confirms hybrid capability for 310 targets, and identifies 310 cases where endpoint capability exceeds what any single classical session view reveals.

</details>


### [49] [Probabilistic-bit Guided CDCL for SAT Solving using Ising Consensus Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04033)
*Melki Bino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合SAT求解框架，将概率比特Ising采样器生成的文字作为临时假设注入CDCL求解器，在特定随机3-SAT基准上减少了80%以上的冲突和传播，并通过轻量级门控机制（随机森林）预测混合求解的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现代CDCL求解器在SAT问题上表现优异，但在可满足实例中仍需大量冲突分析和布尔传播才能找到有效搜索空间区域。作者希望通过引入随机采样来加速CDCL的搜索过程，同时保留CDCL的正确性保障。

Method: 构建混合求解框架：使用p-bit Ising采样器生成高一致性假设文字，将其作为临时假设传递给CDCL求解器；若假设不成立则回退到纯CDCL。此外，训练随机森林门控模型预测混合求解何时有效。

Result: 在选定的受控骨干随机3-SAT基准上，中位冲突减少80.8-85.5%，中位传播减少80.2-84.6%；随机森林门控保留了94.8%的混合求解优势。

Conclusion: p-bit引导的混合求解在特定分布上显著降低CDCL内部搜索开销，但收益对实例分布敏感；轻量级门控可有效筛选适用场景，避免无效调用。

Abstract: Boolean satisfiability (SAT) solvers are widely used in hardware verification, cryptanalysis, automatic test-pattern generation, and side-channel reasoning workflows. Modern conflict-driven clause-learning (CDCL) solvers are highly effective, but satisfiable instances may still require substantial conflict analysis and Boolean propagation before identifying productive regions of the search space. This paper studies a hybrid SAT-solving framework in which a probabilistic-bit (p-bit) Ising sampler proposes high-agreement literals that are passed to CDCL as temporary assumptions. The goal is not to replace CDCL, but to evaluate whether stochastic low-violation samples can reduce CDCL internal search effort while retaining correctness through CDCL fallback. On selected controlled-backbone random 3-SAT benchmarks, the hybrid method reduces median conflicts by 80.8-85.5% and median propagations by 80.2-84.6% relative to pure CDCL. The observed benefit is distribution-sensitive, suggesting that p-bit guidance is effective only for certain instance classes. We further report exploratory machine-learning gates that estimate when hybrid solving is likely to help. On the selected run, a random-forest gate retains 94.8% of hybrid wins, indicating that lightweight gating may help avoid unproductive hybrid calls.

</details>


### [50] [Towards a Risk-Cost Model for Financial Adaptive Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02979)
*Supriya Khadka,Sanchari Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向金融系统的自适应认证风险-成本模型（RCM），将认证重新定义为受约束的动态风险-成本优化问题，整合了基于CVaR的成本敏感风险函数、对抗性自适应序贯决策机制以及嵌入优化目标的隐私与合规约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应认证系统概念碎片化，偏重合规而非经济与对抗风险建模，导致在高风险金融场景中缺乏系统的经济理性与对抗鲁棒性。

Method: 提出RCM形式化框架，包含：(i) 基于条件风险价值（CVaR）的成本敏感风险函数，建模欺诈损失、机会成本与尾部风险；(ii) 适应对抗性探测与分布漂移的序贯决策机制；(iii) 将隐私与监管约束直接嵌入优化目标。

Result: 将认证问题重新定义为受约束的动态风险-成本优化问题，实现经济上有依据、尾部风险感知且对抗不确定性下具有弹性的认证系统。

Conclusion: RCM超越了静态分类与合规驱动的设计范式，为金融系统自适应认证提供了更严谨、鲁棒的理论基础。

Abstract: Authentication in financial systems remains a uniquely high-stakes security challenge, where even marginal increases in false acceptance can result in catastrophic monetary loss. Existing deployments of adaptive authentication, which combine biometrics, behavioral signals, and contextual risk scoring, remain conceptually fragmented and often prioritize regulatory compliance over explicit economic and adversarial risk modeling. To address this structural imbalance, in this paper we introduce a formal Risk-Cost Model (RCM) for adaptive authentication in financial systems. The RCM provides a principled mathematical foundation that integrates three essential components: (i) cost-sensitive risk functions that explicitly capture fraud loss, opportunity cost, and tail risk through Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR); (ii) sequential decision-making mechanisms that adapt to adversarial probing and distributional drift; and (iii) quantifiable privacy and regulatory constraints embedded directly within the optimization objective. By reframing authentication as a constrained dynamic risk-cost optimization problem, the RCM moves beyond static classification and compliance-driven design toward systems that are economically grounded, tail-risk aware, and resilient under adversarial uncertainty.

</details>


### [51] [LiteShield: Hybrid Feature Selection-Driven Lightweight Intrusion Detection for Resource-Constrained IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02987)
*Dileepa Mabulage,Banuka Athuraliya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LiteShield，一种面向IoT环境的轻量级机器学习入侵检测系统。该方法结合混合特征选择（MI+RFECV）与六种轻量级分类器，在UNSW-NB15数据集上进行二分类和多分类实验。KNN取得最高准确率（二分类98.26%，多分类85.22%），但Random Forest在检测性能与部署效率之间取得最佳折中（二分类98.01%，多分类80.39%），且模型体积与推理成本显著低于KNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备的快速扩张扩大了攻击面，而现有入侵检测系统对资源受限的IoT环境计算开销过大，存在安全性能与计算资源之间的严重不匹配。

Method: 采用UNSW-NB15数据集，进行数据预处理和不平衡感知准备；设计两阶段特征选择流程：先通过互信息（MI）筛选，再通过递归特征消除与交叉验证（RFECV）精炼；评估六种轻量级分类器（决策树、随机森林、KNN、逻辑回归、朴素贝叶斯、SVM）的二分类和多分类性能。

Result: KNN二分类准确率98.26%，多分类准确率85.22%，性能最高；Random Forest二分类准确率98.01%，多分类准确率80.39%，但模型体积和推理成本远低于KNN，实现了检测质量与部署效率的最佳平衡；消融分析表明类别不平衡显著影响多分类性能。

Conclusion: LiteShield证明混合特征选择与轻量级机器学习相结合，能够为IoT环境提供兼具准确性和计算可行性的入侵检测解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of Internet of Things (IoT) deployments has enlarged the attack surface of modern digital infrastructure while exposing a key security mismatch: many intrusion detection systems (IDSs) remain too computationally expensive for constrained IoT environments. This paper presents LiteShield, a lightweight machine learning-based IDS that combines hybrid feature selection with efficient classifiers to support accurate attack detection under limited computational budgets. The proposed framework uses the UNSW-NB15 dataset, applies data preprocessing and imbalance-aware preparation, and employs a two-stage feature selection pipeline based on Mutual Information (MI) and Recursive Feature Elimination with Cross-Validation (RFECV). Six lightweight classifiers are evaluated for both binary and multiclass intrusion detection: Decision Tree, Random Forest, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), Logistic Regression, Naïve Bayes, and Support Vector Machine.
  Experimental results show that KNN achieved the highest raw predictive performance, reaching 98.26% accuracy for binary classification and 85.22% accuracy for multiclass classification. However, Random Forest delivered the most practical trade-off between detection quality and deployment efficiency, obtaining 98.01% binary accuracy and 80.39% multiclass accuracy with substantially lower model size and inference cost than KNN. Additional ablation analysis on minority attack classes indicates that class imbalance materially affects multiclass performance. Overall, LiteShield demonstrates that hybrid feature selection and lightweight machine learning can provide a viable path toward accurate and computationally feasible intrusion detection for IoT-focused environments.

</details>


### [52] [ChaRVoC: A Challenge-Response Voice Cancelable Authentication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02990)
*Phuc-Khang Vo-Hoang,Hoang C. Ta,Nhien-An Le-Khac,Dinh-Thuc Nguyen,Hong-Hanh Nguyen-Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ChaRVoC系统，一种结合语音生物特征、用户秘密密钥和动态系统挑战的三因素可撤销语音认证系统，通过HashGray-XOR方案实现模板的不可逆性、可撤销性和不可链接性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音认证系统面临重放攻击、模板不可撤销以及模板泄露等安全问题，亟需一种兼具可撤销性、活体检测和模板保护能力的免密认证方案。

Method: 提出ChaRVoC三因素认证框架：1）挑战-响应机制用于活体检测；2）用户记忆的秘密密钥实现模板可撤销性；3）HashGray-XOR方案，将密码学哈希函数与不可恢复的格雷码变换相结合，数学证明模板的不可逆性。

Result: 在VoxCeleb1、TIMIT和VOiCES数据集上与WTA、IoM、RoE等现有可撤销生物特征方法进行对比，验证了识别性能；同时证明了系统满足可撤销性和不可链接性。

Conclusion: ChaRVoC系统在保护语音模板安全的同时，实现了可撤销性、不可链接性和抗重放攻击能力，是一种有前景的语音免密认证方案。

Abstract: In this work, we present a Challenge-Response Voice Cancelable authentication system, called ChaRVoC, which provides protection against replay attacks, revocability issues, and template compromise. Our approach integrates three security factors: (1) inherent voice biometric characteristics, (2) user-memorized secret keys enabling template revocability, and (3) dynamic system-generated challenges providing liveness detection. Specifically, we introduce a novel HashGray-XOR scheme which combines a cryptographic hash function with an unrecoverable graycode-based transformation to create secured templates that are mathematically proven to be non-invertible. We compare our methods with existing cancelable biometric methods (WTA, IoM, RoE) on VoxCeleb1, TIMIT, and VOiCES datasets to show the recognition performance of our proposed system. We also show that our system achieves both cancelability and unlinkability properties.

</details>


### [53] [PHANTOM: Polymorphic Honeytoken Adaptation with Narrative-Tailored Organisational Mimicry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02992)
*Abraham Itzhak Weinberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PHANTOM框架，通过编码组织特定知识生成上下文逼真的蜜令，在8种令牌类型和4种组织环境下，可信度评分从0.576提升至0.778，人工接受率从6.2%提升至100%，检测抵抗力从0.609提升至0.870，且无需外部API调用可在隔离环境部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜜令生成工具产生静态、基于模板的令牌，缺乏组织特异性，可被统计、句法和语义分析识别。组织上下文是当前方法中缺失的关键要素。

Method: 提出PHANTOM框架，将组织特定知识（域名、服务命名惯例、技术栈习惯、真实秘密值分布）编码到多组件生成流水线中；形式化蜜令质量的四组件可信度评分（句法有效性、语义连贯性、统计合理性、人类接受度）。

Result: PHANTOM可信度B=0.778±0.057 vs 基线0.576±0.058（Δ=+0.203, t=14.07, p<0.001, Cohen's d=3.52）；人工接受率从6.2%提升至100%；检测抵抗力从0.609提升至0.870；语义连贯性差距（ΔSc=+0.309, d=4.52）是主要驱动力。

Conclusion: PHANTOM显著优于模板基线，组织上下文是关键缺失要素；完全可在气隙隔离环境中部署，无需外部API调用。

Abstract: Honeytokens, decoy digital assets planted to detect and attribute unauthorised access, are a well-established primitive in cyber deception. Existing generation tools produce static, template-based tokens that lack organisational specificity and are identifiable by statistical, syntactic, and semantic analysis. We introduce PHANTOM (Polymorphic Honeytoken Adaptation with Narrative-Tailored Organisational Mimicry), a framework that generates contextually convincing honeytokens by encoding organisation-specific knowledge: domain names, service naming conventions, technology-stack idioms, and realistic secret-value distributions, into a multi-component generation pipeline. We formalise honeytoken quality through a four-component Believability Score that captures syntactic validity, semantic coherence, statistical plausibility, and human acceptance. We use this metric to evaluate PHANTOM across 8 token types and 4 organisational contexts against a template-based baseline. PHANTOM achieves B = 0.778 +/- 0.057 versus B = 0.576 +/- 0.058 for templates (Delta = +0.203, t = 14.07, p < 0.001, Cohen's d = 3.52). Human-evaluator acceptance rises from 6.2% to 100%, and detection resistance (DR = 1 - Pd) improves from 0.609 to 0.870 across three simulated scanner models (regex, entropy analysis, and ML classifier), each with p < 0.001. The semantic coherence gap (Delta Sc = +0.309, d = 4.52) is the dominant driver, confirming the hypothesis that organisational context is the critical missing ingredient in current approaches. All results are reproduced without external API calls, making the pipeline fully deployable in air-gapped environments.

</details>


### [54] [Distributed Deep Variational Approach for Privacy-preserving Data Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03069)
*Zahir Alsulaimawi,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出高斯隐私保护器（GPP），一种针对连续高维数据的数据发布框架，通过变分互信息下界训练随机编码器压制敏感属性，同时利用拉格朗日乘子平衡隐私-效用权衡，并扩展到联邦学习场景，使客户端本地训练编码器、聚合器仅接收脱敏表示，在三个基准上实现效用接近无约束自编码器基线同时将攻击者AUC降至接近随机猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中梯度、模型更新和发布表示仍可能泄露敏感属性，传统"数据本地化"保护不充分，需要更强的实例级隐私保护机制。

Method: GPP学习一个随机编码器将原始数据映射到低维脱敏表示，通过变分下界最小化发布表示与敏感属性间的互信息，同时用交叉熵项保持效用属性，通过拉格朗日乘子β控制权衡；扩展至联邦设置，客户端本地训练编码器且敏感标签不离开客户端，聚合器仅接收脱敏表示。

Result: 在MNIST（数字和vs奇偶性）、CelebA（微笑vs性别）、HAPT-Recognition（活动vs主体身份）三个基准上，GPP效用与无约束自编码器基线差距约1个百分点以内，同时将攻击者AUC降至接近随机猜测。

Conclusion: GPP在联邦学习场景中提供了超越传统"数据本地化"的实例级隐私保护，有效平衡了隐私与效用。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) lets distributed nodes train a shared model without exchanging their raw data, but in privacy-sensitive deployments medical sensors, IoT devices, wearables the protection offered by keeping data local is incomplete: gradients, model updates, and the released representations themselves can leak sensitive attributes. We propose the \emph{Gaussian Privacy Protector} (GPP), a data-release framework for continuous, high-dimensional inputs that learns a stochastic encoder mapping raw data to a low-dimensional sanitized representation. The encoder is trained against a variational lower bound on the mutual information between the released representation and a designated sensitive attribute, while a separate cross-entropy term preserves a designated utility attribute, with a Lagrange multiplier $β$ controlling the trade-off. We then extend GPP to the federated setting, in which each client trains a local encoder, sensitive labels never leave the client, and the aggregator receives only sanitized representations giving instance-level privacy protection in addition to the standard ``raw data stays local'' guarantee of FL. We evaluate GPP on MNIST (digit-sum utility, parity sensitive), CelebA (smiling vs.\ gender), and HAPT-Recognition (activity vs.\ subject identity). Across all three benchmarks, GPP attains utility within roughly one percentage point of an unconstrained autoencoder baseline while reducing the adversary's AUC to near random guessing.

</details>


### [55] [Revisiting JBShield: Breaking and Rebuilding Representation-Level Jailbreak Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03095)
*Kemal Derya,Berk Sunar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM越狱攻击与防御展开研究。作者设计了JB-GCG攻击，通过结合拒绝方向抑制和毒性概念正则化，成功攻破了JBShield防御（平均ASR 46.2%）。随后提出基于马氏距离的多层表示轨迹验证（RTV）防御，在白盒自适应攻击下仍保持低ASR（7%）。研究表明：非自适应检测的强性能不代表自适应威胁模型下的鲁棒性，多层表示一致性比单层概念相似性更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御（如JBShield）在非自适应设置下表现优异（0% ASR），但缺乏对自适应威胁模型的评估。作者旨在探究这些防御在攻击者知晓防御机制并针对性设计攻击时的真实鲁棒性，并寻找更可靠的防御基础。

Method: 1) JB-GCG攻击：修改GCG目标函数，结合拒绝方向抑制（余弦相似度）与JBShield自身的毒性概念正则化。2) RTV防御：基于马氏异常检测的多层拒绝方向指纹（refusal-direction fingerprint）验证。3) 自适应攻击：针对RTV的全白盒攻击设计。

Result: JB-GCG在Llama-3-8B上平均ASR 46.2%（最高53.4%），对JBShield-M达30.7%。RTV防御AUROC达0.99，最强自适应攻击仅7% ASR（计算成本13倍）。

Conclusion: 非自适应防御的强性能不能保证自适应威胁下的鲁棒性；多层表示一致性检测是比单层概念相似性更可靠的越狱检测基础。

Abstract: Defending large language models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, such as Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG), remains a challenge, particularly under adaptive threat models where an attacker directly targets the defense mechanism. JBShield, a recent jailbreak defense with a 0% attack success rate in some settings, detects malicious prompts via two concept signals, a toxic concept and a jailbreak concept. We design JB-GCG, which modifies GCG's objective to combine two terms: refusal-direction suppression via cosine similarity between the refusal direction and hidden-state representations, and toxic-concept regularization via JBShield's own toxic concept score. Across five configurations on Llama-3-8B, JB-GCG achieves an average ASR of 46.2%, reaching up to 53.4% in the strongest setting. We further show that our attack remains effective against JBShield-M, achieving ASR up to 30.7% across evaluated settings. The attack persists across multiple JBShield recalibrations, confirming that the vulnerability is structural rather than calibration-specific. We analyze the cosine-similarity signatures of jailbreak representations and find that they occupy a distinctive region in refusal-direction fingerprint space that neither harmless nor harmful prompts inhabit. We introduce Representation Trajectory Verification (RTV), a new defense based on Mahalanobis outlier detection over multi-layer refusal-direction fingerprints. RTV attains an AUROC of 0.99 against our attack. Finally, we design and evaluate an additional adaptive attack against RTV with full white-box knowledge of the defense; the best attack achieves only 7% ASR at 13x the computational cost. Our results show that strong non-adaptive detection does not imply robustness under adaptive threat models, and that multi-layer representation consistency is a more reliable foundation for jailbreak detection than single-layer concept similarity.

</details>


### [56] [Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Explainable Malware Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03140)
*Jayson Ng,Amin Milani Fard*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过实验发现，在基于VirusTotal报告的恶意软件解释任务中，检索增强生成（RAG）非但不能提升解释质量，反而因引入无关上下文和叙事噪声导致质量下降，表明恶意软件解释本质上是信号提取任务而非知识检索问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证RAG在恶意软件解释场景中能否真正提升解释质量这一常见假设。

Method: 使用VirusTotal报告作为结构化输入，在多个LLM上对比有/无RAG的恶意软件解释质量。

Result: RAG频繁降低解释质量，表现为引入分散注意力的弱相关上下文、叙事噪声和泛化性描述。

Conclusion: 在结构化安全证据已足够时，RAG对恶意软件解释是适得其反的；该任务应被视为信号提取问题而非知识检索问题，并提出了安全开发工作流的设计建议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used as security engineering tools to summarize and explain malware behavior to analysts. A common assumption is that Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves explanation quality by injecting external security knowledge. In this work, we empirically evaluate this assumption for malware explanation using VirusTotal reports as structured input. Across multiple LLMs, we find that RAG frequently degrades explanation quality by introducing distracting or weakly related context and adding narrative noise or generic write-ups. Our results highlight a practical risk in security-critical pipelines for malware explanation that RAG can be counterproductive when structured security evidence is already sufficient. We argue that malware explanation is primarily a signal-extraction task, not a knowledge-retrieval problem, and outline design recommendations for secure development workflows.

</details>


### [57] [A Validated Prompt Bank for Malicious Code Generation: Separating Executable Weapons from Security Knowledge in 1,554 Consensus-Labeled Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03179)
*Richard J. Young,Gregory D. Moody*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对恶意代码任务中语言模型拒绝基准测试将"可执行恶意软件请求"与"有害安全知识请求"混淆的问题，提出了武器（CODE）与知识（KNOWLEDGE）分类轴，并通过五模型共识协议（覆盖Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、智谱、阿里五个模型系列）对4个公开基准的3,133个提示进行分类，最终生成1,554个共识CODE样本库和388个共识KNOWLEDGE比较集。五评判者间Fleiss' kappa达0.876（几乎完全一致），69.3%的提示达到五票全票一致。该论文主要贡献是提供经过可靠性验证的数据集，并主张将武器与知识区分作为代码安全评估的组织轴心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在恶意代码任务上的拒绝基准测试，往往将"请求可执行恶意软件"与"请求有害安全知识"混为一谈，而这两种请求类型可能触发安全对齐模型中不同的拒绝路径，混合计算单一拒绝率无法区分两者，因而需要建立清晰的分类轴来解耦评估。

Method: 提出武器vs.知识分类轴，通过五模型共识协议实现：使用来自Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、智谱AI、阿里的五个大语言模型作为评判者，每个提示获得二元CODE/KNOWLEDGE标签，采用五取三多数规则，并用Fleiss' kappa及bootstrap 95%置信区间量化评判者间信度。

Result: 从4个公开基准的3,133个提示出发，生成1,554个共识CODE样本库和388个共识KNOWLEDGE比较集。五评判者间Fleiss' kappa = 0.876 [95% CI: 0.862, 0.888]，达到"几乎完美"一致水平；69.3%的提示为五票全票一致；所有3,133个提示均达到3/5阈值，无模糊排除提示。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是经过可靠性验证的数据集（consensus-CODE银行）以及将武器vs.知识区分作为代码安全评估组织轴心的论证。该分类轴在实践中能否有效分离模型行为是留给配套基准研究的实证问题，本文不直接回答。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks of language-model refusal on malicious-coding tasks routinely conflate requests for executable malicious software with requests for harmful security knowledge. This conflation matters because the two request types plausibly trigger distinct refusal pathways in safety-aligned language models, and a single refusal-rate statistic computed over a mixture cannot isolate either. This paper introduces a weapons-versus-knowledge classification axis, operationalized through a five-model consensus protocol, and applies it to 3,133 prompts drawn from four public benchmarks, yielding a 1,554-prompt consensus-CODE bank (the primary released artifact) and a 388-prompt consensus-KNOWLEDGE comparison set used by the companion benchmark paper. The consensus pipeline uses five large-language-model judges spanning four vendor families (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, Zhipu AI, Alibaba), each issuing a binary CODE/KNOWLEDGE label per prompt under a three-of-five majority rule, with inter-rater reliability quantified by Fleiss' kappa with bootstrap 95% confidence intervals. Across all 3,133 prompts the five judges achieve kappa = 0.876 [95% CI: 0.862, 0.888], "almost perfect" agreement by the Landis & Koch convention, with 69.3% of prompts unanimous at five-of-five; all 3,133 prompts reached the 3-of-5 threshold, so the consensus pipeline produced zero ambiguity-excluded prompts. Whether the axis separates model behavior in practice is an empirical question this paper leaves to the companion benchmark study; the present contribution is the reliability-documented artifact and the case for treating the weapons-versus-knowledge distinction as the organizing axis of code-safety evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [Cryptographic Registry Provenance: Structural Defense Against Dependency Confusion in AI Package Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03309)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种加密分发溯源系统，通过注册表密钥签名、双重签名模型和权威命名空间绑定三层防御机制，解决依赖混淆攻击问题。在八个生态系统中对比显示，现有系统均未同时具备四项关键安全特性，该系统还可扩展至AI生成溯源和运行时治理架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖混淆攻击利用软件分发中的结构性缺陷——包安装后无法通过加密手段证明其来自哪个注册表。现有防御均为配置驱动型，一旦配置错误就会静默失效。

Method: 提出三个组件：(1) 加密注册表身份，每个注册表持有Ed25519密钥对并对分发的所有工件签名；(2) 双重签名模型，发布者在打包时签名，注册表在发布时加签；(3) 权威命名空间绑定，消费者固定注册表指纹，解析器从加密层面拒绝来自未授权注册表的工件。

Result: 对比八个生态系统（npm、Cargo、Hex.pm、PyPI、Go模块、Docker/OCI、NuGet、Maven）发现，没有现有系统同时具备强制性发布者签名、加密注册表身份、强制性注册表加签和消费者端加密执行四项特性。该系统可扩展至AI生成溯源和治理强制依赖解析。

Conclusion: 集成分发溯源与三层运行时治理架构，形成无加密间隙的四阶段生命周期链。

Abstract: Dependency confusion attacks exploit a structural gap in software distribution: once a package is installed, there is no cryptographic proof of which registry distributed it. Every existing defense is configuration-based and fails silently when misconfigured. We present a cryptographic distribution provenance system comprising three components: (1) cryptographic registry identity, where every registry holds an Ed25519 keypair and signs every artifact it distributes; (2) a dual-signature model, where the publisher signs at packaging time and the registry countersigns at publication time; and (3) authoritative namespace binding, where consumers pin registry fingerprints and the resolver cryptographically rejects artifacts from unauthorized registries. These create three defense layers requiring simultaneous compromise for a successful attack. A comparison across eight ecosystems (npm, Cargo, Hex.pm, PyPI, Go modules, Docker/OCI, NuGet, Maven) shows no existing ecosystem combines mandatory publisher signing, cryptographic registry identity, mandatory registry countersigning, and consumer-side cryptographic enforcement. The system extends to AI-generation provenance as a signed attribute and governance-enforced dependency resolution. A case study integrates distribution provenance with a three-layer runtime governance architecture, creating a four-phase lifecycle chain with no cryptographic gaps.

</details>


### [59] [Dependency-Aware Privacy for Multi-turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03188)
*Divyam Anshumaan,Sarthak Choudhary,Nils Palumbo,Somesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RootGuard通过一次性扰动根属性并基于扰动后的根进行后续确定性计算，解决了多轮对话中LLM智能体隐私随每次释放而退化的问题。相比独立噪声注入方法，RootGuard在隐私-效用权衡上实现了2.3-3.0倍的改进，且攻击者查询次数越多，优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于度量差分隐私的提示净化器将每次释放视为独立事件，导致敌手可以通过跨轮次组合释放结果来恢复私有属性，隐私随每次释放而退化。这种退化在私有属性是计算图的根节点时尤为严重，因为独立地对派生值加噪声会放大根节点的可区分性，放大倍数可达派生函数的Lipschitz常数L，对于医疗和金融工作流中的非线性函数，该常数可能远超名义隐私参数。

Method: RootGuard一次性对根值进行净化，后续所有释放均基于扰动后的根进行确定性计算。根据后处理定理，隐私保证仅取决于初始根净化，与敌手函数或交互轮次无关，派生值以零边际成本继承隐私。RootGuard还利用结构领域知识（如从身高体重计算BMI，或已知目标函数）在根之间分配预算，改善隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 在八个NHANES医疗诊断模板上，RootGuard在ε=0.1时实现了比独立噪声注入低2.3-3.0倍的目标误差（7.6% vs 17.1% wMAPE，B=(2k+1)ε）。在MAP重建攻击下，更多查询会增强对独立噪声注入的攻击效果，而RootGuard保持不变。

Conclusion: RootGuard通过一次性扰动根属性而非逐轮加噪，从根本上解决了多轮交互中隐私随释放次数退化的问题。该方法在医疗诊断等敏感场景中展现出显著优势，并且随着交互轮次增加（最坏情况敌手迫使t轮交互），RootGuard的优势进一步扩大，形成"双重不对称"：更多轮次有利于RootGuard而削弱独立噪声注入方法。

Abstract: LLM agents release private data across multi-service interactions. Existing prompt sanitizers based on metric differential privacy treat each release independently, so adversaries combining releases across turns can recover private attributes; privacy degrades with every release.
  This degradation is fundamental: when private attributes are the \emph{roots} of a computation graph, independently noising a derived value amplifies the root's distinguishability by up to the deriving function's Lipschitz constant $L$, which can far exceed the nominal privacy parameter for nonlinear functions in medical and financial workflows.
  RootGuard sanitizes root values once and computes subsequent releases deterministically from the noised roots. By the post-processing theorem, the privacy guarantee depends only on the initial root sanitization, regardless of the adversary's functions or number of turns, and derived values inherit privacy at zero marginal cost. RootGuard further exploits structural domain knowledge (e.g., BMI from height and weight, or a known target function) to allocate budget across roots, improving the privacy-utility tradeoff.
  A worst-case adversary forcing $t$ turns increases the total budget $B = t \cdot \varepsilon$. RootGuard distributes this larger budget across roots, while independent noising spends $\varepsilon$ per release and gives the adversary $t$ observations to combine via MAP reconstruction. This yields a \emph{double asymmetry}: more turns aid RootGuard while weakening independent noising.
  On eight NHANES medical diagnostic templates, RootGuard achieves $2.3$--$3.0\times$ lower target error than independent noising at $\varepsilon = 0.1$ (7.6\% vs.\ 17.1\% wMAPE at $B = (2k{+}1)\varepsilon$). Under MAP reconstruction, more queries strengthen attacks against independent noising while RootGuard remains invariant.

</details>


### [60] [ARGUS: Defending LLM Agents Against Context-Aware Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03378)
*Shihao Weng,Yang Feng,Jinrui Zhang,Xiaofei Xie,Jiongchi Yu,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentLure基准测试，用于评估上下文相关的提示注入攻击；并提出ARGUS防御机制，通过构建影响溯源图追踪不可信上下文传播，将攻击成功率降至3.8%的同时保持87.5%的任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM智能体的提示注入攻击基准和防御方法大多假设上下文无关场景（智能体仅依赖用户指令），忽略了真实部署中智能体行为依赖动态上下文、攻击者可适应不同上下文的现实情况，导致现有防御无法应对实际智能体系统的威胁。

Method: 1）AgentLure：涵盖四个智能体领域和八种攻击向量的上下文感知提示注入攻击基准；2）ARGUS：通过构建影响溯源图（influence provenance graph）追踪不可信上下文在智能体决策中的传播路径，在执行前验证决策是否基于可信证据。

Result: ARGUS将攻击成功率降至3.8%，同时保持87.5%的任务效用，显著优于现有防御方法，并对自适应的白盒攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 现有防御在上下文感知的提示注入攻击下表现不佳；ARGUS通过溯源感知的决策审计有效缓解了这一威胁，为LLM智能体的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Model (LLM) agents, augmented with tool use, skills, and external knowledge, has introduced new security risks. Among them, prompt injection attacks, where adversaries embed malicious instructions into the agent workflow, have emerged as the primary threat. However, existing benchmarks and defenses are fundamentally limited as they assume context-insensitive settings in which the agent works under a fully specified user instruction, and the attacks are straightforward and context-independent. As a result, they fail to capture real-world deployments where agent behavior usually depends on dynamic context, not just the user prompt, and adversaries can adapt their attacks to different context. Similarly, existing defenses built on this narrow threat model overlook the nature of real-world agent delegation.
  In this paper, we present AgentLure, a benchmark that captures context-dependent tasks and context-aware prompt injection attacks. AgentLure spans four agentic domains and eight attack vectors across diverse attack surfaces. Our evaluation shows that existing defenses often struggle in this setting, yielding poor performance against such attacks in agentic systems. To address this limitation, we propose ARGUS, a defense mechanism that enforces provenance-aware decision auditing for LLM agents. ARGUS constructs an influence provenance graph to track how untrusted context propagates into agent decisions and verify whether a decision is justified by trustworthy evidence before execution. Our evaluation shows ARGUS reduces attack success rate to 3.8% while preserving 87.5% task utility, significantly outperforming existing defenses and remaining robust against adaptive white-box adversaries.

</details>


### [61] [MAGE: Safeguarding LLM Agents against Long-Horizon Threats via Shadow Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03228)
*Yuhui Wang,Tanqiu Jiang,Jiacheng Liang,Charles Fleming,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAGE提出了一个受系统安全"影子栈"启发的防御框架，通过维护专用的安全驱动智能体记忆来检测和缓解LLM智能体在长期交互中面临的多步骤威胁，在检测精度、早期检测能力和智能体效用开销方面显著优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的智能体在完成复杂真实世界任务时，面临着利用扩展的用户-智能体-环境交互来实施恶意目的的多步骤攻击（长期威胁），这类攻击在单轮对话中难以发生，对关键领域的智能体安全部署构成重大风险。

Method: MAGE借鉴系统安全中的"影子栈"抽象，维护一个专用的、以安全为中心的智能体记忆，该记忆从完整执行轨迹中提取并保留安全关键上下文，利用该影子记忆在执行前主动评估待执行动作的风险。

Result: 在多种长期威胁场景下，MAGE在检测准确率上显著优于现有防御方法，能对大多数攻击实现早期阶段检测，且对智能体效用引入的开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: MAGE是首个利用智能体记忆方法检测和缓解长期威胁的框架，为该关键挑战建立了新范式，并为未来研究开辟了有前景的方向。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM)-powered agents are increasingly deployed to perform complex, real-world tasks, they face a growing class of attacks that exploit extended user-agent-environment interactions to pursue malicious objectives improbable in single-turn settings. Such long-horizon threats pose significant risks to the safe deployment of LLM agents in critical domains. In this paper, we present MAGE (Memory As Guardrail Enforcement), a novel defensive framework designed to counter a wide range of long-horizon threats. Inspired by the "shadow stack" abstraction in systems security, MAGE maintains a dedicated, safety-focused agentic memory that distills and retains safety-critical context across the agent's full execution trajectory, leveraging this shadow memory to proactively assess the risk of pending actions prior to their execution. Extensive evaluation demonstrates that MAGE substantially outperforms existing defenses across diverse long-horizon threats in detection accuracy, achieves early-stage detection for the majority of attacks, and introduces only negligible overhead to agent utility. To our best knowledge, MAGE represents the first framework to detect and mitigate long-horizon threats using an agentic memory approach, establishing a new paradigm for this critical challenge and opening promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [62] [SILMARILS: Information-Theoretic and Quantum-Secure Designated-Verifier Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03230)
*Hassan Khodaiemehr,Khadijeh Bagheri,Chen Feng,Dariia Porechna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SILMARILS 是一个基于有限域上代数核心的签名框架，支持两方和三方模式，实现可转移指定验证者签名，具有比 Dilithium、Falcon、SPHINCS+ 等标准化后量子方案更小的密钥和签名尺寸，适用于区块链应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一个基于简单代数结构的高效签名框架，在指定验证者场景下提供后量子安全性，同时显著减小密钥和签名尺寸以满足区块链等应用需求。

Method: 基于 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 上的最小代数核心，使用真随机性和完美的 2-out-of-2 Shamir 秘密共享。两方模式下实现可转移指定验证者签名，第三方可凭借收据验证；三方模式下采用 Fitzi 等人的广播模型，获得统计安全的签名协议。安全性在纯IT模型、IT+ROM和QROM下分析。

Result: 两方模式下证明了对非指定验证者的 EUF-CMA^¬DV 安全性（ROM和QROM）；三方模式下实现了基于模拟的安全性和可忽略错误率 1/p。密钥和签名尺寸显著小于 Dilithium、Falcon、SPHINCS+ 等标准化方案。

Conclusion: SILMARILS 以其简单代数结构实现了更小的密钥和签名，在指定验证者签名场景下提供后量子安全性，特别适合区块链应用。

Abstract: SILMARILS is built from a minimal algebraic core over $\mathbb{F}_p$ using true randomness and perfect $2$-out-of-$2$ Shamir secret sharing. The framework supports both two-party and three-party modes. In the two-party setting, SILMARILS realizes a transferable designated-verifier (TDV) signature scheme. The designated verifier can simulate accepting transcripts indistinguishable from real ones, achieving Jakobsson-Sako-Impagliazzo DV security. The verifier may publish a receipt $r$ enabling public verification, yet even with $r$, no external party can tell whether a transcript was signed or simulated. As DV signatures permit simulation, standard EUF-CMA cannot hold for the designated verifier; instead, we prove $\mathsf{EUF\text{-}CMA}^{\neg\mathsf{DV}}$ security for all non-designated verifiers in both the random oracle model (ROM) and quantum random oracle model (QROM). In the three-party mode, adopting the broadcast model of Fitzi et al., we obtain a statistically secure signature protocol with simulation-based security and error~$1/p$. We analyze security in the Pure IT model, the IT+ROM, and the QROM, extending the Fitzi et al. framework to quantum adversaries with classical I/O. Correctness, secrecy, transferability, and unforgeability for non-designated parties remain equivalent to simulation-based security. Thanks to its simple algebraic structure, SILMARILS achieves substantially smaller keys and signatures than standardized post-quantum schemes such as Dilithium, Falcon, and SPHINCS$^+$, while providing post-quantum security in a TDV setting well suited to blockchain applications.

</details>


### [63] [SkCC: Portable and Secure Skill Compilation for Cross-Framework LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03353)
*Yipeng Ouyang,Yi Xiao,Yuhao Gu,Xianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkCC是一个受经典编译器设计启发的编译框架，通过引入强类型中间表示SkIR解耦技能语义与平台特定格式，实现跨异构智能体框架的可移植部署，同时通过编译时分析器强制执行安全约束，将适配复杂度从O(m×n)降至O(m+n)，在多个平台上显著提升任务通过率、降低延迟并节省token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同智能体框架对提示格式的敏感性差异巨大，导致高达40%的性能波动；现有技能多以单一Markdown版本存在，手动为各平台重写维护负担沉重；超过三分之一的社区技能存在安全漏洞。亟需一种系统化方法解决技能的可移植性、安全性和性能一致性问题。

Method: 提出SkCC编译框架，核心包括：(1) SkIR——强类型中间表示，解耦技能语义与平台特定格式；(2) 编译时分析器，通过反技能注入技术强制执行安全约束；(3) 四阶段编译流水线，将适配复杂度从O(m×n)降为O(m+n)。

Result: 在SkillsBench上，编译后技能在Claude Code上通过率从21.1%提升至33.3%，在Kimi CLI上从35.1%提升至48.7%；编译延迟低于10ms；主动安全触发率达94.8%；各平台运行时token节省10-46%。

Conclusion: 将经典编译器设计引入智能体技能开发是可行的，SkCC通过中间表示和解耦策略有效解决了跨平台技能可移植性、安全性和性能问题，为智能体工程化提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-Agents have evolved into autonomous systems for complex task execution, with the SKILL.md specification emerging as a de facto standard for encapsulating agent capabilities. However, a critical bottleneck remains: different agent frameworks exhibit starkly different sensitivities to prompt formatting, causing up to 40% performance variation, yet nearly all skills exist as a single, format-agnostic Markdown version. Manual per-platform rewriting creates an unsustainable maintenance burden, while prior audits have found that over one third of community skills contain security vulnerabilities. To address this, we present SkCC, a compilation framework that introduces classical compiler design into agent skill development. At its core, SkIR - a strongly-typed intermediate representation - decouples skill semantics from platform-specific formatting, enabling portable deployment across heterogeneous agent frameworks. Around this IR, a compile-time Analyzer enforces security constraints via Anti-Skill Injection before deployment. Through a four-phase pipeline, SkCC reduces adaptation complexity from $O(m \times n)$ to $O(m + n)$. Experiments on SkillsBench demonstrate that compiled skills consistently outperform their original counterparts, improving pass rates from 21.1% to 33.3% on Claude Code and from 35.1% to 48.7% on Kimi CLI, while achieving sub-10ms compilation latency, a 94.8% proactive security trigger rate, and 10-46% runtime token savings across platforms.

</details>


### [64] [DECKER: Domain-invariant Embedding for Cross-Keyboard Extraction and Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03384)
*Bikrant Bikram Pratap Maurya,Nitin Choudhury,Daksh Agarwal,Arun Balaji Buduru*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HEAR数据集（53人/37种键盘/3种场景）和DECKER框架（包含键盘签名归一化、域对抗解耦、跨键盘对比对齐、声学风格随机化四阶段），系统研究键盘声学侧信道攻击在跨设备、跨用户和噪声环境下的泛化能力，结果表明ASCA在实际场景中仍构成严重安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有键盘声学侧信道攻击研究受限于小规模数据集，缺乏用户、键盘和环境的多样性，无法充分分析跨设备、跨麦克风和噪声条件下的攻击效果。

Method: 构建HEAR数据集（53名参与者、37种笔记本电脑键盘、3种录制场景）；提出DECKER框架，包含键盘签名归一化、域对抗解耦、跨键盘对比对齐、声学风格随机化四阶段，并引入基于LLM的句子级后处理层。

Result: DECKER在跨键盘和跨用户设置下显著优于强基线方法，语言模型校正进一步提升了性能，表明ASCA在不同用户、设备和噪声环境中仍然有效。

Conclusion: 键盘声学侧信道攻击在多样化实际场景中仍构成严重安全风险，亟需关注其防御措施。

Abstract: Acoustic side-channel attacks (ASCA) on keyboards pose a significant security risk, as keystrokes can be inferred from typing acoustics, revealing sensitive information. Prior ASCA studies are limited by small-scale datasets with restricted diversity in users, keyboards, and environments, constraining analysis across devices, microphones, and noise conditions. We introduce HEAR, a dataset designed to study ASCA along three axes: keyboard generalization, noise adaptation, and user bias. HEAR contains recordings from 53 participants using 37 laptop keyboards, collected in three realistic settings: (1) external microphone capture, (2) device microphone capture without network noise, and (3) VoIP-based streaming capture. This enables controlled evaluation across users, keyboards, and environments. On HEAR, we establish an ASCA benchmark spanning conventional features and pre-trained representations from raw audio and spectrograms in unimodal and multimodal settings. We propose DECKER, a domain-invariant keystroke inference framework with four stages: (1) Keyboard Signature Normalization to reduce device coloration, (2) domain-adversarial disentanglement to suppress keyboard identity, (3) supervised cross-keyboard contrastive alignment to enforce key consistency, and (4) Acoustic Style Randomization to synthesize unseen keyboard responses. We further explore sentence-level inference using an LLM-based post-processing layer to refine keystroke sequences via linguistic context. Results on HEAR show DECKER improves keystroke identification over strong baselines, particularly in cross-keyboard and cross-user settings, with further gains from language-model rectification. These findings highlight that ASCA remains effective across diverse users, devices, and noisy environments, underscoring its practical security risk.

</details>


### [65] [Exposing LLM Safety Gaps Through Mathematical Encoding:New Attacks and Systematic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03441)
*Haoyu Zhang,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 将有害提示编码为形式化数学问题（如集合论、数理逻辑、量子力学）可绕过LLM安全机制，在8个目标模型上实现46%-56%平均攻击成功率。该漏洞的关键在于深度语义重构而非数学符号本身，且对简单提示增强具有鲁棒性。新模型（GPT-5系列）表现出更强的鲁棒性但仍存在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM安全机制主要依赖语义模式匹配来过滤有害输出，但缺乏对数学形式化表述的防御能力，存在安全盲区。

Method: 将有害提示编码为集合论、数理逻辑、量子力学等数学形式化问题；设置规则式编码（仅数学格式化）作为对照；在8个目标模型和2个基准数据集上评估；进行重复后处理增强测试。

Result: 数学重构编码实现46%-56%平均攻击成功率；规则式编码效果与未编码基线相当；形式逻辑编码达到与集合论编码相当的攻击成功率；攻击对重复后处理具有鲁棒性；GPT-5系列新模型鲁棒性显著优于旧模型。

Conclusion: 当前安全框架存在根本性缺陷，无法处理深层数学结构层面的有害内容；需要发展基于数学结构推理而非表层语义匹配的防御机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) employ safety mechanisms to prevent harmful outputs, yet these defenses primarily rely on semantic pattern matching. We show that encoding harmful prompts as coherent mathematical problems -- using formalisms such as set theory, formal logic, and quantum mechanics -- bypasses these filters at high rates, achieving 46%--56% average attack success across eight target models and two established benchmarks. Crucially, the effectiveness depends not on mathematical notation itself, but on whether a helper LLM deeply reformulates the harmful content into a genuine mathematical problem: rule-based encodings that apply mathematical formatting without such reformulation perform no better than unencoded baselines. We introduce a novel Formal Logic encoding that achieves attack success comparable to Set Theory, demonstrating that this vulnerability generalizes across mathematical formalisms. Additional experiments with repeat post-processing confirm that these attacks are robust to simple prompt augmentation. Notably, newer models (GPT-5, GPT-5-Mini) show substantially greater robustness than older models, though they remain vulnerable. Our findings highlight fundamental gaps in current safety frameworks and motivate defenses that reason about mathematical structure rather than surface-level semantics.

</details>


### [66] [MEMSAD: Gradient-Coupled Anomaly Detection for Memory Poisoning in Retrieval-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03482)
*Ishrith Gowda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了面向检索增强型LLM Agent的存储投毒攻击，将其建模为Stackelberg博弈，并提出了一种基于梯度耦合定理的校准式检测防御MEMSAD，理论上证明了其认证检测半径与极小最优性，实验表明组合防御在各类攻击下达到TPR=1.00、FPR=0.00，但同义词替换攻击仍能完全规避检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性外部存储使LLM Agent能够跨会话保持上下文，但其安全性尚未被形式化刻画。现有评估协议存在不一致性，导致攻击成功率被低估，亟需统一的评估框架与可认证的防御方法。

Method: 将存储投毒攻击形式化为Stackelberg博弈，构建涵盖三类递增访问假设的统一评估框架；提出MEMSAD（语义异常检测）防御方法，基于梯度耦合定理证明异常分数梯度与检索目标梯度的一致性，从而获得认证检测半径；通过Le Cam方法证明极小最优性，推导在线校准的遗憾界，并刻画同义词不变性这一离散漏洞。

Result: 纠正评估协议不一致后，攻击成功率提升4倍（ASR-R从0.25升至1.00）；组合防御在所有攻击下达到TPR=1.00、FPR=0.00；同义词替换攻击完全规避检测（Δ ASR-R ≈ 0）。

Conclusion: MEMSAD提供了可认证的检测保障，但同义词替换漏洞揭示了连续空间防御的固有边界，现有嵌入方法无法弥补该差距，为未来离散空间防御设计指明方向。

Abstract: Persistent external memory enables LLM agents to maintain context across sessions, yet its security properties remain formally uncharacterized. We formalize memory poisoning attacks on retrieval-augmented agents as a Stackelberg game with a unified evaluation framework spanning three attack classes with escalating access assumptions. Correcting an evaluation protocol inconsistency in the triggered-query specification of Chen et al. (2024), we show faithful evaluation increases measured attack success by $4\times$ (ASR-R: $0.25 \to 1.00$). Our primary contribution is MEMSAD (Semantic Anomaly Detection), a calibration-based defense grounded in a gradient coupling theorem: under encoder regularity, the anomaly score gradient and the retrieval objective gradient are provably identical, so any continuous perturbation that reduces detection risk necessarily degrades retrieval rank. This coupling yields a certified detection radius guaranteeing correct classification regardless of adversary strategy. We prove minimax optimality via Le Cam's method, showing any threshold detector requires $Ω(1/ρ^2)$ calibration samples and MEMSAD achieves this up to $\log(1/δ)$ factors. We further derive online regret bounds for rolling calibration at rate $O(σ^{2/3}Δ^{1/3})$, and formally characterize a discrete synonym-invariance loophole that marks the boundary of what continuous-space defenses can guarantee. Experiments on a $3 \times 5$ attack-defense matrix with bootstrap confidence intervals, Bonferroni-corrected hypothesis tests, and Clopper-Pearson validation ($n=1{,}000$) confirm: composite defenses achieve TPR $= 1.00$, FPR $= 0.00$ across all attacks, while synonym substitution evades detection at $Δ$ ASR-R $\approx 0$, exposing a gap existing embedding-based defenses cannot close.

</details>


### [67] [ZK-Value: A Practical Zero-Knowledge System for Verifiable Data Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03581)
*Zhaoyu Wang,Pingchuan Ma,Zhantong Xue,Yuguang Zhou,Qixin Zhang,Xiaoqin Zhang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZK-Value是一个实用的端到端零知识数据估值系统，通过LSH-Shapley原语、ZK-LSH-Shapley协议和结构化的证明系统优化，在保护数据隐私的同时实现了可验证的数据估值，证明时间比现有ZK基线快12.6-68.1倍，验证时间低于4.6秒，估值质量与最优基线相当（AUROC差异在0.033以内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据市场利用Shapley值进行数据估值，但市场运营商单独运行归因过程，参与者无法在私有数据上独立重新计算分数，导致信任问题。零知识证明理论上可以解决隐私与可验证性之间的矛盾，但现有ZK估值系统因证明时间过长或需要披露验证集而难以扩展到实际市场规模。

Method: ZK-Value采用完全协同设计的架构：(1) LSH-Shapley——基于局部敏感哈希的估值原语，用每桶碰撞计数替代昂贵的成对距离度量；(2) ZK-LSH-Shapley——定制化的零知识证明协议，通过将碰撞计数编码为桶级直方图而非逐对张量，大幅减少见证大小；(3) 结构化的证明系统优化——超级预言机批处理和稀疏性跳过技术。

Result: 在12个标准数据集上，ZK-Value的估值质量与精确KNN-Shapley基线相当（AUROC差值在0.033以内），证明时间从数秒到数分钟不等，比专门的ZK基线快12.6倍至68.1倍，验证时间低于4.6秒。

Conclusion: ZK-Value成功弥合了数据估值中隐私与可验证性之间的可扩展性差距，为现实世界的数据市场提供了实用的端到端零知识估值解决方案。

Abstract: Data valuation is a foundational task in data marketplaces, where a Shapley-value attribution determines how a buyer's payment is distributed among data providers. Typically, the marketplace operator runs this attribution alone, requiring participants and external auditors to trust scores they cannot independently recompute on the underlying private data. While zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) can theoretically reconcile this conflict between privacy and verifiability, existing ZK valuation systems fail to scale to real-world marketplace demands due to prohibitive proving times or the requirement to disclose validation cohorts.
  We present ZK-Value, a practical, end-to-end ZK data-valuation system. Our solution bridges the scalability gap through a fully co-designed architecture: (1) LSH-Shapley, a locality-based valuation primitive that replaces expensive pairwise distance metrics with per-bucket collision counts; (2) ZK-LSH-Shapley, a tailored ZKP protocol that drastically reduces witness size by encoding these counts into bucket-level histograms rather than naive per-pair tensors; and (3) structural proof-system optimizations, specifically super-oracle batching and sparsity skipping. Evaluated across 12 standard datasets, ZK-Value delivers valuation quality on par with state-of-the-art baselines (within 0.033 AUROC of exact KNN-Shapley), while generating proofs in seconds to minutes and outperforming specialized ZK baselines by 12.6x to 68.1x in proving time, with verification in under 4.6 s.

</details>


### [68] [Tailored Prompts, Targeted Protection: Vulnerability-Specific LLM Analysis for Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03697)
*Xing Zhang,Keyu Zhang,Taohong Zhu,Anbang Ruan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型的智能合约漏洞检测框架，利用精确的AST上下文提取和漏洞特定提示设计，在15个区块链平台的3,200+真实项目中构建了31,165个专业注释的漏洞实例数据集，针对13种漏洞实现了0.92的正向召回率和0.85的负向召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约在区块链上容易遭受多种安全漏洞，且具有不可篡改性，可能导致重大财务损失。现有检测方法缺乏跨漏洞类型的灵活性，且严重依赖人工构建的专家规则。

Method: 提出基于LLM的智能合约漏洞检测框架，构建包含31,165个专业注释漏洞实例的大规模数据集（来自15个区块链平台的3,200+真实项目），采用精确的基于AST的上下文提取和漏洞特定提示设计，为13种常见漏洞类别实例化定制化检测器。

Result: 平均正向召回率0.92，平均负向召回率0.85，展现了精心设计的上下文提示策略在可扩展、高精度智能合约安全分析中的潜力。

Conclusion: 精心设计的上下文提示能够有效实现可扩展、高精度的智能合约安全分析，LLM框架相比传统依赖专家规则的方法具有更好的灵活性和跨漏洞类型适应性。

Abstract: Smart contracts on blockchains are prone to diverse security vulnerabilities that can lead to significant financial losses due to their immutable nature. Existing detection approaches often lack flexibility across vulnerability types and rely heavily on manually crafted expert rules. In this paper, we present an LLM-based framework for practical smart contract vulnerability detection. We construct and release a large-scale dataset comprising 31,165 professionally annotated vulnerability instances collected from over 3,200 real-world projects across 15 major blockchain platforms. Our approach leverages precise AST-based context extraction and vulnerability-specific prompt design to instantiate customized detectors for 13 prevalent vulnerability categories. Experimental results demonstrate strong effectiveness, achieving an average positive recall of 0.92 and an average negative recall of 0.85, highlighting the potential of carefully engineered contextual prompting for scalable and high-precision smart contract security analysis.

</details>


### [69] [Internet of Things Security: A Survey on Common Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03744)
*Dalton Cézane Gomes Valadares,Luiz Antonio Pereira Silva,Daniel Hindemburg de Miranda Marques,Álvaro Alvares de Carvalho César Sobrinho,Andson Marreiros Balieiro,Mohamed Ahmed Hail,Mohammed B. Alshawki,Kyller Costa Gorgônio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对物联网威胁面进行了全面调查，描述28种常见攻击，运用STRIDE模型进行分类、CVSS框架评估，并建立攻击与五类基础漏洞（过程、代码、通信、操作、设备）的映射关系，同时综述现有缓解技术及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备计算能力有限、缺乏标准化、攻击面不断扩大，导致安全风险严峻，亟需对威胁面进行结构化理解和系统化分析，以支撑构建安全的物联网生态系统。

Method: 采用STRIDE模型对28种攻击进行功能威胁分类，结合CVSS框架进行定量关键性评估，并将威胁映射到五类基础漏洞（过程、代码、通信、操作、设备），揭示攻击者利用的具体技术入口点。

Result: 识别并描述了28种常见物联网攻击，建立了威胁与五类漏洞之间的映射关系，提供了基于STRIDE和CVSS的结构化分析框架，并整理了现有缓解技术与新兴研究范式。

Conclusion: 该调查为研究人员和从业者提供了全面的技术基础与未来研究路线图，有助于构建更具韧性和安全性的物联网生态系统。

Abstract: The exponential growth of the Internet of Things (IoT) has integrated connected devices into various sectors like smart cities, digital health, and Industry 4.0, generating vast amounts of real-time data to support intelligent decision-making. However, this widespread adoption is fundamentally challenged by significant security risks, primarily due to the inherent computational limitations of devices, lack of standardization, and an expanding attack surface. Given that security is paramount to ensuring trust in these environments, this paper presents a comprehensive survey and a multi-dimensional analysis of the IoT threat landscape. It describes 28 common attacks, ranging from traditional threats, such as Man-in-the-Middle, to specialized IoT exploits, including node replication and skimming. To provide a structured understanding of these risks, we employ the STRIDE model for functional threat classification alongside the CVSS framework for quantitative criticality assessment. Furthermore, the research establishes a robust mapping between these threats and five foundational vulnerability classes (Process, Code, Communication, Operation, and Device), uncovering the specific technical entry points exploited by adversaries. Beyond threat identification, the survey presents state-of-the-art mitigation techniques and discusses emerging paradigms and research gaps, working as a roadmap for future investigation and providing a consolidated technical foundation for both researchers and practitioners aiming to build resilient and secure IoT ecosystems.

</details>


### [70] [GPUBreach: Privilege Escalation Attacks on GPUs using Rowhammer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03812)
*Chris S. Lin,Yuqin Yan,Guozhen Ding,Joyce Qu,Joseph Zhu,David Lie,Gururaj Saileshwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次证明GPU Rowhammer攻击可实现与CPU同等的权限提升。通过利用GPU页表管理机制，非特权用户CUDA内核可通过Rowhammer比特翻转篡改页表，获取其他进程的GPU内存访问权限，进而泄露cuPQC库的加密密钥、篡改模型汇编代码。进一步可突破IOMMU保护实现CPU端权限提升，获得root shell和系统级控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明GPU存在Rowhammer漏洞，但只能进行非定向比特翻转以降低模型精度，缺乏像CPU攻击那样实现权限提升的能力。作者希望证明GPU Rowhammer攻击的威力和CPU攻击同样强大。

Method: 首先利用GPU页表管理机制，识别新页表分配的位置和时机；然后通过Rowhammer比特翻转篡改驻留在GPU内存中的页表项，使非特权用户CUDA内核获得其他进程/租户的GPU内存访问权限；利用该原语实现GPU端权限提升，并进一步突破IOMMU防护实现CPU端权限提升。

Result: 成功实现首个GPU端权限提升攻击——泄露cuPQC库中的加密密钥、通过篡改模型GPU汇编代码隐蔽降低模型精度；进一步实现CPU端权限提升，突破IOMMU保护，在非多租户场景下获得root shell和系统级控制权。

Conclusion: GPU Rowhammer攻击可以像CPU Rowhammer攻击一样实现权限提升，威胁性远超以往认知，且GPU端权限提升可扩展为CPU端系统级控制，对GPU共享环境下的安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: NVIDIA GPUs with GDDR memories have been shown susceptible to Rowhammer-based bit-flips, similar to CPUs. However, Rowhammer exploits on GPUs have been limited to injecting untargeted bit-flips in victim data like weights of machine learning models, to degrade model accuracy, unlike CPU exploits shown capable of privilege escalation. In this paper, we demonstrate that GPU Rowhammer exploits can be as potent as CPU Rowhammer attacks. By exploiting the GPU page table management to identify when and where new page tables are allocated, we enable an unprivileged user CUDA kernel of one process to use RowHammer bit-flips to gain access to the GPU memory of other processes or co-tenants via targeted tampering of such page-tables resident on the GPU memory. Using this newly found primitive, we demonstrate the first GPU-side privilege escalation attacks, leaking secret data such as cryptographic keys from cuPQC libraries, and even tampering with the model's GPU assembly code to degrade models more stealthily than previous attacks. We further demonstrate that GPU-side privilege escalation can lead to CPU-side privilege escalation, defeating the protections provided by the IOMMU, enabling a malicious user-level program with GPU access to gain root shell and system-wide control, even in a non-multi-tenant setting.

</details>


### [71] [LIPPEN: A Lightweight In-Place Pointer Encryption Architecture for Pointer Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03974)
*Erfan Iravani,Lalit Prasad Peri,Mohannad Ismail,Charitha Tumkur Siddalingaradhya,Changwoo Min,Elif Bilge Kavun,Wenjie Xiong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LIPPEN提出了一种硬件-软件协同设计的全指针加密方案，通过将整个64位指针字段用于加密而非保留原始地址位，在无需额外元数据开销的情况下提供强指针完整性和机密性，有效解决了现有方案（如PAC）熵有限且易受暴力破解和重用攻击的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C/C++程序中的内存安全违规仍然导致控制流劫持和数据导向攻击等复杂利用技术。现有硬件防御要么依赖ASLR，要么为指针附加元数据验证完整性。外部元数据方案开销大，而就地认证机制（如ARM PAC）虽开销低但熵有限且易受暴力破解和重用攻击。

Method: LIPPEN将每个指针视为加密块，密码学上将其绑定到执行上下文，并在解引用时透明解密。通过重新利用整个64位指针字段进行加密而非保留原始地址位，最大化熵，消除截断认证码的暴力破解弱点，并保持与PAC软件的二进制兼容性。在FPGA上基于64位RISC-V Rocket和BOOM核心实现原型。

Result: LIPPEN提供全面的指针保护，运行时开销与PAC方案相当，面积和功耗开销可忽略不计。在微基准测试、nbench和SPEC CPU2017上评估，并与自研RISC-V PAC实现及Apple M1上的PAC进行了比较。

Conclusion: LIPPEN是在实际处理器中部署强指针保护的实用设计方案。

Abstract: Memory-safety violations in C and C++ programs continue to enable sophisticated exploitation techniques such as control-flow hijacking and data-oriented attacks. Existing hardware defenses either rely on address space layout randomization (ASLR) or attach explicit metadata to pointers to verify their integrity. External metadata schemes provide strong guarantees, but incur additional memory accesses and memory footprint overhead. In-place authentication mechanisms, such as ARM Pointer Authentication (PAC), achieve low overhead at the cost of limited entropy and susceptibility to brute-force and reuse attacks. This paper presents LIPPEN, a hardware-software co-design for full-pointer encryption that provides strong pointer integrity and confidentiality with zero metadata overhead. LIPPEN treats every pointer as an encrypted block, cryptographically binding it to its execution context and decrypting it transparently at dereference time. By re-purposing the entire 64-bit pointer field for encryption rather than preserving raw address bits, LIPPEN maximizes entropy, eliminates the brute-force weaknesses of truncated authentication codes, and maintains binary compatibility with existing PAC-enabled software. We prototype LIPPEN on FPGA using 64-bit RISC-V Rocket and BOOM cores, and evaluate it with microbenchmarks, nbench, and SPEC CPU2017. We compare against both an in-house RISC-V PAC implementation and Apple's PAC on the M1 processor. Across these workloads, LIPPEN provides comprehensive pointer protection with runtime overhead comparable to PAC-based schemes, while incurring negligible area and power overhead. These results show that LIPPEN is a practical design point for deploying strong pointer protection in real processors.

</details>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Resource Allocation and AoI-Aware Detection for ISAC with Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03558)
*Elaheh Ataeebojd,Nhan Thanh Nguyen,Seonghoon Yoo,Joonhyuk Kang,Markku Juntti,Matti Latva-aho,Mehdi Rasti*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文针对SIM辅助的下行多用户ISAC系统，研究能效优化的资源分配问题，通过联合优化资源块分配、发射功率控制和SIM相位偏移，提出基于两时间尺度分解的迭代算法，实现了相比无SIM基线高达230%的能效提升，并揭示了能效与异构QoS需求之间的基本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用SIM提供的波域自由度，在降低硬件成本的同时实现灵活波束成形和干扰管理，以支持通信（eMBB和URLLC共存）与感知功能的能效优化。

Method: 利用puncturing机制固有的两时间尺度结构，将原问题分解为两个子问题（时隙级eMBB能效优化和迷你时隙级URLLC/感知能效优化），分别采用迭代算法将非凸问题转化为一系列可解子问题，实现RB分配和功率控制的凸优化更新以及SIM相位偏移的低复杂度更新。

Result: 所提设计相比无SIM基线实现高达230%的能效提升，且所需发射天线数显著减少，同时满足通信和感知QoS需求。

Conclusion: SIM能够有效提升ISAC系统能效并降低硬件开销，能效与通信/感知异构QoS需求之间存在根本性权衡关系。

Abstract: Stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIMs) provide wave-domain degrees of freedom that can empower integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) through flexible beampattern synthesis and interference management, while reducing hardware cost. In this paper, we investigate energy-efficient resource allocation for a downlink SIM-aided multi-user ISAC system that supports the coexistence of enhanced mobile broadband (eMBB) and ultra-reliable and low-latency communication (URLLC) via puncturing, while simultaneously illuminating sensing targets. We formulate an energy efficiency (EE) maximization problem that jointly optimizes resource block (RB) allocation, transmit power control, and SIM phase shifts. The formulated problem is highly challenging due to the large number of variables optimized on different time scales. To overcome this, we leverage the intrinsic two-timescale structure induced by the puncturing approach to decompose the original problem into two tractable subproblems: EE maximization for eMBB users in each time slot and EE maximization for URLLC users and sensing targets in each mini-slot. To address each subproblem, we develop an iterative algorithm that transforms the original non-convex formulation into a sequence of tractable subproblems, yielding convex updates for RB allocation and power control, along with low-complexity updates for SIM phase shifts. Simulation results show that the proposed design achieves up to 230% improvement in EE over a No-SIM baseline. In addition, it requires significantly fewer transmit antennas than conventional BS architectures, while preserving the EE achieved and satisfying the communication and sensing quality of service (QoS) requirements. Moreover, the results reveal fundamental trade-offs between EE and heterogeneous QoS requirements across communication and sensing functionalities.

</details>

### [73] [Joint Energy Management and Coordinated AIGC Workload Scheduling for Distributed Data Centers: A Diffusion-Aided Reward Shaping Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02965)
*Yang Fu,Peng Qin,Liming Chen,Zihao Zhang,Hao Yu,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AIGC云数据中心的联合能量管理与协同工作负载调度框架，通过显式数学刻画服务质量、引入扩散模型辅助奖励塑造的深度强化学习算法，在应对模型异构性、隐式质量评估等挑战的同时，显著降低了数据中心能源成本并提升了系统效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC服务在云数据中心产生快速增长的计算负载，亟需战略性调度来降低能源成本并保证高质量内容生成。现有方法难以应对AIGC服务的模型异构性、隐式服务质量评估和复杂推理过程控制等独特挑战。

Method: 提出联合能量管理与协同AIGC工作负载调度框架，引入服务质量显式数学表征以促进作业跨ASP迁移和细粒度推理配置；联合考虑数据中心多种能源资源以增强用电灵活性；构建系统效用最大化问题；开发扩散模型辅助的奖励塑造方法，通过多步去噪过程合成互补奖励信号，与DRL集成以在稀疏环境反馈下高效学习调度策略。

Result: 基于真实模型和数据集的实验表明，该方案有效适应电价波动和AIGC模型异构性，在学习收敛速度和系统效用上均优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过扩散模型辅助奖励塑造解决了作业调度决策强耦合导致的奖励稀疏性问题，为AIGC云数据中心的高效能源管理和工作负载调度提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-generated content (AIGC) has emerged as a transformative paradigm for automating the creation of diverse and customized content, giving rise to rapidly growing computational workloads in cloud data centers. It is imperative for AIGC service providers (ASPs) to strategically schedule AIGC workloads to reduce data center energy costs while guaranteeing high-quality content generation. However, the distinctive characteristics of AIGC services pose critical challenges, including model heterogeneity across ASPs, implicit service quality evaluation, and complex inference process control. To tackle these challenges, we propose a joint energy management and coordinated AIGC workload scheduling framework, which introduces an explicit mathematical characterization of service quality to promote both job transfer among ASPs and fine-grained inference process configuration. Moreover, various energy resources within data centers are jointly considered to enhance power usage flexibility. Subsequently, a system utility maximization problem is formulated to balance AIGC service revenue with operational penalties and costs. Nevertheless, the strong coupling among job scheduling decisions induces severe reward sparsity, which limits the effectiveness of existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms. To address this issue, we develop a diffusion model-aided reward shaping approach to synthesize complementary reward signals through a multi-step denoising process. This approach is seamlessly integrated with DRL to enable efficient learning of scheduling policies under sparse environmental feedback. Experiments based on real-world models and datasets demonstrate that our scheme effectively accommodates electricity price fluctuations and AIGC model heterogeneity, while achieving superior learning convergence and system utility compared with benchmark methods.

</details>


### [74] [eOptShrinkQ: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression Through Optimal Spectral Denoising and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02905)
*Pei-Chun Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出eOptShrinkQ，一种基于随机矩阵理论的KV缓存压缩方法，通过最优奇异值收缩提取低秩共享上下文，再对残差进行近最优量化。在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上，~2.2 bits/entry下LongBench超越TurboQuant的3.0 bits，且Multi-needle检索接近或超过FP16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer注意力头的KV缓存存在低秩共享分量与全秩逐token残差的自然分解（符合spiked随机矩阵模型），现有量化方法未充分利用这一结构；此外，标量量化假设各向同性分布，而实际残差因异常值和坐标相关性导致性能损失，需要消除异常值处理和内积偏差校正的额外开销。

Method: 两阶段压缩流水线：(1) eOptShrink——基于随机矩阵理论的最优奇异值收缩，自动通过BBP相变选择秩，提取共享结构；(2) TurboQuant——对满足薄壳性质（坐标离域化）的残差进行逐向量标量量化，恢复各向同性后无需异常值处理和内积偏差校正。

Result: 三种理论保证：自动秩选择、近零内积偏差、近最优量化失真。实验：Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上，同等质量下每entry节省近1 bit；LongBench中~2.2 bits/entry优于TurboQuant的3.0 bits；多针检索中2.2 bits接近或超过FP16。

Conclusion: 谱去噪可充当检索密集型任务的有益正则化器，通过恢复各向同性消除异常值处理和偏差校正的需求，实现更高效的KV缓存压缩。

Abstract: We show that the key-value (KV) cache in transformer attention heads admits a natural decomposition into a low-rank \emph{shared context} component and a full-rank \emph{per-token} residual, well described by the spiked random matrix model. This observation leads to eOptShrinkQ, a two-stage compression pipeline: optimal singular value shrinkage (eOptShrink) automatically extracts the shared structure, and the residual -- which satisfies the \emph{thin shell property} with delocalized coordinates -- is quantized by TurboQuant~\citep{zandieh2025turboquant}, a recently proposed per-vector scalar quantizer with near-optimal distortion guarantees. By restoring the isotropy that scalar quantization assumes, spectral denoising eliminates the need for both outlier handling and dedicated inner product bias correction, freeing those bits for improved reconstruction.
  The theoretical grounding in random matrix theory provides three guarantees: automatic rank selection via the BBP phase transition, provably near-zero inner product bias on the residual, and coordinate delocalization ensuring near-optimal quantization distortion. Experimentally, we validate eOptShrinkQ on Llama-3.1-8B and Ministral-8B across three levels: per-head MSE and inner product fidelity, where eOptShrinkQ saves nearly one bit per entry over TurboQuant at equivalent quality; end-to-end on LongBench (16 tasks), where eOptShrinkQ at $\sim$2.2 bits per entry outperforms TurboQuant at 3.0 bits; and multi-needle retrieval, where eOptShrinkQ at 2.2 bits closely matches or exceeds uncompressed FP16, suggesting that spectral denoising can act as a beneficial regularizer for retrieval-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [75] [An End-to-End Framework for Building Large Language Models for Software Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02906)
*Jingkai He,Pengfei Chen,Chenghui Wu,Shuang Liang,Ye Li,Gou Tan,Xiadao Wen,Chuanfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OpsLLM，一种面向软件运维领域的领域特定大语言模型，通过人工参与的数据筛选、监督微调及领域过程奖励模型（DPRM），在知识问答和根因分析任务上显著优于现有开源和闭源模型，并将开源7B/14B/32B三个版本及15K微调数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在软件运维领域尚未实现高效端到端智能运维，主要受限于低质量数据、碎片化知识以及学习不充分的问题。

Method: 1) 引入Human-in-the-Loop机制从原始运维数据中构建高质量微调数据集；2) 基于该数据进行监督微调得到基座模型；3) 在强化学习阶段引入领域过程奖励模型（DPRM），优化模型在根因分析任务上的准确性和可靠性。

Result: 在多种难度的实验任务上，OpsLLM在QA任务上提升0.2%~5.7%，在RCA任务上提升2.7%~70.3%，且表现出强迁移性。

Conclusion: OpsLLM有效学习并与注入的运维领域知识对齐，在准确率上超越现有开源及闭源LLM，证明了所提构建流程的有效性。

Abstract: In the field of software operations, Large Language Models (LLMs) have attracted increasing attention. However, existing research has not yet achieved efficient and effective end-to-end intelligent operations due to low-quality data, fragmented knowledge and insufficient learning. To explore the potential of LLMs in software operations, we propose OpsLLM, a domain-specific LLM that supports both knowledge-based question answering (QA) and root cause analysis (RCA). Moreover, we disclose the detailed workflow for building LLMs specifically in the software operations domain. First, a Human-in-the-Loop mechanism is introduced to curate highquality data from a large collection of operational raw data and construct a fine-tuning dataset. Then, based on the data, supervised fine-tuning is conducted to achieve a base model. Furthermore, we introduce a domain process reward model (DPRM) during the reinforcement learning stage to optimize the accuracy and reliability of the fine-tuned model on RCA tasks. Experimental results on the tasks with diverse difficulties demonstrate that OpsLLMs effectively learns and aligns with the operational domain knowledge infused, outperforming existing open-source and closed-source LLMs in accuracy with improvements of 0.2%~5.7% on QA tasks and 2.7% ~70.3% on RCA tasks, while exhibiting strong transferability. Moreover, we will open-source three versions of OpsLLM with 7B, 14B and 32B parameters, along with a 15K fine-tuning dataset.

</details>


### [76] [On the Invariants of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02907)
*Wonsuk Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现softmax注意力机制中"能量场"（行中心化注意力logit）存在两类不变性：机制层面的代数不变性（每行零和约束、头维度决定的秩界、谱特征）和模型层面的规律性（能量方差去局域化源于关键矩阵的"关键非相干性"）。这些不变性导致能量场被限制在低维子空间，并可用于训练监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索softmax注意力映射中query-key交互的底层结构，揭示其内在不变性及其来源。

Method: 定义"能量场"（行中心化注意力logit），从机制代数结构和模型实验两个层面分析其不变性，并通过多架构自回归语言模型验证。

Result: 发现两类不变性：机制层面（每行零和、秩界、谱特征）和模型层面（能量方差去局域化）；识别出"关键非相干性"是去局域化的根源。

Conclusion: 能量场的不变性具有实际意义：秩界将其限制在低维子空间，关键非相干性可用于每头训练监控。

Abstract: Softmax attention maps every query--key interaction into a probability distribution, but the underlying structure remains largely unexplored. We define the \emph{energy field}, the row-centered attention logit, and show that it exhibits invariant properties across models, architectures, and inputs.
  Two classes of invariants emerge. \emph{Mechanism-level} invariants follow from the algebraic structure of softmax attention. They include a per-row zero-sum constraint, a rank bound determined by the head dimension, and spectral signatures that follow from them. \emph{Model-level} regularities are not required by the mechanism, yet hold in every autoregressive language model we test, spanning several architecture families. The energy field distributes its variance over key positions without concentrating at a few. This delocalization traces to a property of the key matrix we call \emph{key incoherence}.
  These invariants have practical consequences. The rank bound confines the energy field to a low-dimensional subspace. Key incoherence yields a per-head training monitor. All results are verified at multiple context lengths and input texts.

</details>


### [77] [Delay, Plateau, or Collapse: Evaluating the Impact of Systematic Verification Error on RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02909)
*Kazuki Egashira,Mark Vero,Jasper Dekoninck,Florian E. Dorner,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用带有验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练大语言模型时，验证器系统性错误（而非随机独立错误）对模型推理能力的影响。实验表明，系统性假阴性与随机噪声效果相似，但系统性假阳性会导致次优甚至性能崩溃，且影响取决于错误的特定模式而非整体错误率，挑战了以往认为验证错误影响有限的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献通常将验证器错误视为随机且独立的噪声，认为其对RLVR训练仅有减缓作用而不影响最终性能。然而实际场景中验证器（如静态代码检查器）往往表现出系统性错误，可能导致模型学到不期望的持续行为。作者旨在系统研究这种系统性验证错误对RLVR训练的真实影响。

Method: 在算术任务上进行受控实验，分别研究系统性假阴性错误和系统性假阳性错误对RLVR训练的影响，并与以往随机噪声假设下的结论进行对比分析。

Result: 系统性假阴性错误的效果与随机噪声类似；而系统性假阳性错误会导致从次优平台期到性能崩溃的广泛不良行为。这些结果并非由总体错误率决定，而是由错误的特定模式决定，使得事前缓解变得困难。

Conclusion: 与以往结论相反，现实中的验证器系统性错误会显著影响RLVR的训练结果；验证器质量的评估不能仅看样本级错误率，还需考虑其错误模式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). While RLVR is designed for tasks with verifiable ground-truth answers, real-world verifiers (e.g., static code checkers) can introduce errors into the reward signal. Prior analyses have largely treated such errors as random and independent across samples, concluding that errors merely slow training with limited effect on final performance. However, practical verifiers tend to exhibit systematic errors. This introduces a risk of models learning unwanted consistent behavior from a structurally incorrect reward signal. In this work, we study the impact of such systematic verification errors on RLVR. Through controlled experiments on arithmetic tasks, we show that systematic false negatives lead to similar effects as random noise. On the other hand, systematic false positives can cause a wide range of behaviors from sub-optimal plateaus to performance collapse. Crucially, these outcomes are not determined by the overall error rate but by the specific pattern of introduced errors, making pre-hoc mitigation difficult.
