This benchmark assesses a model's capacity to learn meaningful information about each new domain while retaining key features from the original domain.
  Using this benchmark, we frame the transfer of a machine learning model from one region to another as a domain incremental learning problem. While the task of patient outcome prediction remains the same, the input data distribution varies, necessitating a model that can effectively manage these shifts. We evaluate two popular domain incremental learning methods: data replay, which stores examples from previous data sources for fine-tuning on the current source, and Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), a model parameter regularization method that maintains features important for both data sources.

</details>


### [152] [Complex Equation Learner: Rational Symbolic Regression with Gradient Descent in Complex Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03841)
*Sergei Garmaev,Maurice Gauché,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出复数权重扩展的方程学习器，通过允许优化轨迹绕过实轴退化，解决了含奇点算子（除法、对数、平方根）的符号回归问题，在基准测试和实验数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于梯度的符号回归方法无法处理引入奇点或域约束的算子（如除法、对数、平方根），现有Equation Learner模型要么避免这些算子，要么施加限制（如约束分母防止极点），从而窄化了假设空间。

Method: 提出Equation Learner的复数权重扩展，通过将优化从实数域扩展到复数域，使优化轨迹能够绕过实轴上的退化区域（如极点），从而允许无约束地使用除法、对数、平方根等算子。

Result: 在符号回归基准测试上验证了该方法，并展示了其能够从实验频率响应数据中恢复奇异性行为。

Conclusion: 复数权重扩展有效缓解了实值优化的病态问题，即使目标表达式具有实域极点也能稳定收敛，并实现了对数、平方根等算子的无约束使用。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to discover interpretable equations from data, yet modern gradient-based methods fail for operators that introduce singularities or domain constraints, including division, logarithms, and square roots. As a result, Equation Learner-type models typically avoid these operators or impose restrictions, e.g. constraining denominators to prevent poles, which narrows the hypothesis class. We propose a complex weight extension of the Equation Learner that mitigates real-valued optimization pathologies by allowing optimization trajectories to bypass real-axis degeneracies. The proposed approach converges stably even when the target expression has real-domain poles, and it enables unconstrained use of operations such as logarithm and square root. We Validate the method on symbolic regression benchmarks and show it can recover singular behavior from experimental frequency response data.

</details>


### [153] [Memory-Efficient Continual Learning with CLIP Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03866)
*Ryan King,Gang Li,Bobak Mortazavi,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种内存高效、分布鲁棒的方法，通过对每个类别动态重新加权损失来训练CLIP模型，在类增量学习和领域增量学习场景中减少灾难性遗忘，即使使用极小内存也能保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型在适应新数据时容易遗忘先前知识，而传统的对比损失在小内存缓冲区下性能严重下降，导致旧任务表现退化。

Method: 提出一种分布鲁棒的方法，在训练过程中动态地对每个类别的损失进行重新加权，以平衡新旧任务的学习，实现内存高效的自适应。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet1K（类增量设置）和DomainNet（领域增量设置）上验证，该方法在极小内存下快速适应新数据，同时有效减少灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 动态类别级损失重加权是一种内存高效的策略，能够在不牺牲旧任务性能的前提下，使CLIP模型更好地适应新数据。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models excel at understanding image-text relationships but struggle with adapting to new data without forgetting prior knowledge. To address this, models are typically fine-tuned using both new task data and a memory buffer of past tasks. However, CLIP's contrastive loss suffers when the memory buffer is small, leading to performance degradation on previous tasks. We propose a memory-efficient, distributionally robust method that dynamically reweights losses per class during training. Our approach, tested on class incremental settings (CIFAR-100, ImageNet1K) and a domain incremental setting (DomainNet) adapts CLIP models quickly while minimizing catastrophic forgetting, even with minimal memory usage.

</details>


### [154] [On Adaptivity in Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03869)
*Hassan Dbouk,Nidham Gazagnadou,Matthias Reisser,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现自适应零阶优化方法（如ZO-Adam）相比调优良好的ZO-SGD并无收敛优势，且内存开销大。基于高维零阶梯度缺乏坐标异质性的分析，提出内存高效的MEAZO优化器，仅跟踪单个标量进行全局步长自适应，在保持ZO-Adam性能的同时达到ZO-SGD的内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大语言模型内存受限微调场景，研究自适应零阶优化的有效性，挑战先前认为自适应方法必然优越的观点，并解决其内存开销大的问题。

Method: MEAZO：一种内存高效的自适应零阶优化器，仅跟踪单个标量实现全局步长自适应，替代传统自适应方法中逐坐标的梯度缩放。

Result: 1) ZO-SGD与ZO-Adam在收敛性能上无显著差异；2) MEAZO达到与ZO-Adam相当的性能，内存占用与ZO-SGD一致；3) MEAZO在步长选择鲁棒性上更优，尤其在分组或块结构优化场景中。

Conclusion: 高维零阶梯度缺乏坐标异质性，自适应机制内存低效；MEAZO通过全局标量自适应在内存效率和性能之间取得最优平衡，并具有理论收敛保证。

Abstract: We investigate the effectiveness of adaptive zeroth-order (ZO) optimization for memory-constrained fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Contrary to prior claims, we show that adaptive ZO methods such as ZO-Adam offer no convergence advantage over well-tuned ZO-SGD, while incurring significant memory overhead. Our analysis reveals that in high dimensions, ZO gradients lack coordinate-wise heterogeneity, rendering adaptive mechanisms memory inefficient. Leveraging this insight, we propose MEAZO, a memory-efficient adaptive ZO optimizer that tracks only a single scalar for global step size adaptation. We support our method with theoretical convergence guarantees under standard assumptions. Experiments across multiple LLM families and tasks demonstrate that MEAZO matches ZO-Adam's performance with the memory footprint of ZO-SGD. Additional experiments on synthetic quadratic problems and LLM fine-tuning further demonstrate MEAZO's enhanced robustness to step size choices, particularly in grouped or block-structured optimization settings.

</details>


### [155] [Spatiotemporal Convolutions on EEG signal -- A Representation Learning Perspective on Efficient and Explainable EEG Classification with Convolutional Neural Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03874)
*Laurits Dixen,Stefan Heinrich,Paolo Burelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在BCI运动想象分类任务中，使用2D时空卷积替代1D卷积可以显著减少高维任务训练时间，且保持性能不变；虽然内部表征几何结构不同，但频谱特征重要性无差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浅层CNN分类EEG信号时，通常使用独立的1D卷积分别处理空间和时间维度，并在拼接时不使用非线性激活层。本文旨在探究使用2D时空卷积作为替代编码方式的效果。

Method: 在低维(3通道)和高维(22通道)BCI运动想象分类任务上，对比了1D CNN、2D CNN以及CNN+Transformer混合模型的性能。通过训练时间、分类性能、频谱特征重要性和表征相似性分析进行综合评估。

Result: 2D卷积在高维任务中显著减少训练时间，同时保持分类性能；1D和2D模型在频谱特征重要性上无差异，但在表征几何结构上存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 建议在高维EEG信号处理中使用2D卷积层以加速训练和推理；同时指出架构驱动的编码方式在处理复杂多变量信号时的重要性，这种差异体现在内部表征而非单纯性能指标上。

Abstract: Classification of EEG signals using shallow Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) is a prevalent and successful approach across a variety of fields. Most of these models use independent one-dimensional (1D) convolutional layers along the spatial and temporal dimensions, which are concatenated without a non-linear activation layer between. In this paper, we investigate an alternative encoding that operates a bi-dimensional (2D) spatiotemporal convolution. While 2D convolutions are numerically identical to two concatenated 1D convolutions along the two dimensions, the impact on learning is still uncertain. We test 1D and 2D CNNs and a CNN+transformer hybrid model in a low-dimensional (3-channel) and a high-dimensional (22-channel) BCI motor imagery classification task. We observe that 2D convolutions significantly reduce training time in high-dimensional tasks while maintaining performance. We investigate the root of this improvement and find no difference in spectral feature importance. However, a clear pattern emerges in representational similarity across models: 1D and 2D models yield vastly different representational geometries. Overall, we suggest an improved model with a 2D convolutional layer for faster training and inference. We also highlight the importance of architecturally-driven encoding when processing complex multivariate signals, as reflected in internal representations rather than purely in performance metrics.

</details>


### [156] [Optimal Posterior Sampling for Policy Identification in Tabular Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03921)
*Cyrille Kone,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域情景马尔可夫决策过程中的(ε, δ)-PAC策略识别问题，提出了一种结合后验采样与在线学习的高效随机算法，在样本复杂度上达到渐近最优，且每幕计算复杂度为O(S²AH)，避免了现有方法在log(1/δ)上的次优依赖和高计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在近似设定(ε>0)下虽有有限时间保证，但计算成本高、难以实现，且对log(1/δ)的依赖次优，亟需一种计算高效且样本最优的算法。

Method: 将后验采样（posterior sampling）与在线学习算法结合以引导MDP中的探索，提出随机化且计算高效的策略识别算法。

Result: 算法在样本复杂度上达到渐近最优（包括后验收缩率），每幕运行时间为O(S²AH)，与标准基于模型的方法匹配；避免了MOCA和PEDEL等先前算法在渐近情形下的保证失效问题以及对log(1/δ)的次优多项式依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作为表格型MDP中的高效策略识别提供了理论洞见和实用工具，在渐近最优性和计算效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: We study the $(\varepsilon, δ)$-PAC policy identification problem in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes. Existing approaches provide finite-time guarantees for approximate settings ($\varepsilon>0$) but suffer from high computational cost, rendering them hard to implement, and also suffer from suboptimal dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. We propose a randomized and computationally efficient algorithm for best policy identification that combines posterior sampling with an online learning algorithm to guide exploration in the MDP. Our method achieves asymptotic optimality in sample complexity, also in terms of posterior contraction rate, and runs in $O(S^2AH)$ per episode, matching standard model-based approaches. Unlike prior algorithms such as MOCA and PEDEL, our guarantees remain meaningful in the asymptotic regime and avoid sub-optimal polynomial dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. Our results provide both theoretical insights and practical tools for efficient policy identification in tabular MDPs.

</details>


### [157] [Integrating Feature Correlation in Differential Privacy with Applications in DP-ERM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03945)
*Tianyu Wang,Luhao Zhang,Rachel Cummings*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关联感知的差分隐私框架CorrDP，允许在不敏感特征上放松隐私约束，同时考虑其与敏感特征的相关性（用总变差距离量化），并设计了基于距离相关噪声的DP-ERM算法，在理论和实验上均优于标准差分隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分隐私对所有特征施加统一的隐私约束，忽略了实际应用中敏感特征与不敏感特征之间的本质区别，导致隐私预算利用效率低下。

Method: 提出CorrDP框架，使用总变差距离量化敏感与不敏感特征之间的相关性；在DP-ERM算法中引入距离相关噪声；当相关性距离未知时从数据集中估计。

Result: CorrDP框架在存在不敏感特征的情况下，相比标准差分隐私框架在隐私-效用权衡上取得了一致且显著的提升，并能在相关性未知时达到可比的隐私-效用保证。

Conclusion: 通过放松对不敏感特征的隐私保护并建模其与敏感特征的相关性，可以在不牺牲整体隐私的前提下显著提升模型效用，CorrDP为异构隐私保护需求提供了更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard differential privacy imposes uniform privacy constraints across all features, overlooking the inherent distinction between sensitive and insensitive features in practice. In this paper, we introduce a relaxed definition of differential privacy that accounts for such heterogeneity, allowing certain features to be treated as insensitive even when correlated with sensitive ones. We propose a correlation-aware framework, $\textsf{CorrDP}$, which relaxes privacy for insensitive features while accounting for their correlations with sensitive features, with the correlations quantified using total variation distance. We design algorithms for differentially private empirical risk minimization (DP-ERM) under the $\textsf{CorrDP}$ framework, incorporating distance-dependent noise into gradients for improved theoretical utility guarantees. When the correlation distance is unknown, we estimate it from the dataset and show that it achieves a comparable privacy-utility guarantee. We perform experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets and show that $\textsf{CorrDP}$-based DP-ERM algorithms consistently outperform the standard DP framework in the presence of insensitive features.

</details>


### [158] [Transformers with Selective Access to Early Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03953)
*Skye Gunasekaran,Téa Wright,Rui-Jie Zhu,Jason Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SATFormer通过上下文相关的门控机制将早期层表示复用问题建模为检索问题，在130M到1.3B参数规模上优于静态值残差和Transformer基线，尤其在检索密集型任务上提升约1.5个平均点，同时保持接近基线的吞吐量和内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如静态值残差）对早期层V_1的访问在所有token、头和上下文中是均匀固定的，但不同位置对早期词汇或语义信息的需求不同，因此需要更灵活的、上下文相关的访问控制。

Method: 提出Selective Access Transformer (SATFormer)，将早期表示复用视为检索问题而非连接问题，保留第一层值路径，并通过上下文相关的门控（context-dependent gate）控制对其的访问。

Result: 在130M至1.3B参数规模的模型上，SATFormer在验证损失和零样本准确率上一致优于静态值残差和Transformer基线；在检索密集型基准上比静态值残差提升约1.5个平均点，且吞吐量和内存接近基线Transformer。

Conclusion: SATFormer学习的是早期表示的选择性复用而非均匀的残差复制，门控分析显示其访问模式具有稀疏性、深度依赖性、头特异性和类别敏感性。

Abstract: Several recent Transformer architectures expose later layers to representations computed in the earliest layers, motivated by the observation that low-level features can become harder to recover as the residual stream is repeatedly transformed through depth. The cheapest among these methods add static value residuals: learned mixing coefficients that expose the first-layer value projection V_1 uniformly across tokens and heads. More expressive dense or dynamic alternatives recover finer-grained access, but at higher memory cost and lower throughput. The usefulness of V_1 is unlikely to be constant across tokens, heads, and contexts; different positions plausibly require different amounts of access to early lexical or semantic information. We therefore treat early-representation reuse as a retrieval problem rather than a connectivity problem, and introduce Selective Access Transformer (SATFormer), which preserves the first-layer value pathway while controlling access with a context-dependent gate. Across models from 130M to 1.3B parameters, SATFormer consistently improves validation loss and zero-shot accuracy over the static value-residual and Transformer baselines. Its strongest gains appear on retrieval-intensive benchmarks, where it improves over static value residuals by approximately 1.5 average points, while maintaining throughput and memory usage close to the baseline Transformer. Gate analyses suggest sparse, depth-dependent, head-specific, and category-sensitive access patterns, supporting the interpretation that SATFormer learns selective reuse of early representations rather than uniform residual copying. Our code is available at https://github.com/SkyeGunasekaran/SATFormer.

</details>


### [159] [Pretrained Model Representations as Acquisition Signals for Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03964)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Shikha Surana,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Zachary Weller-Davies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练MLIP（MACE）潜空间中的神经正切核（NTK）和激活核作为主动学习采集信号，无需额外不确定性头、贝叶斯训练或委员会集成，在反应性化学基准上将达到目标性能所需数据量平均减少38%（能量误差）和28%（力误差）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应性化学中训练机器学习原子间势（MLIP）常受限于量子化学标签的高成本和过渡态构型的稀缺性。主动学习可降低成本，但其效果取决于采集规则。现有方法需要辅助不确定性头、贝叶斯训练或委员会集成，增加了复杂性。

Method: 从预训练MACE势中直接提取两种采集信号：有限宽神经正切核（NTK）和基于隐层潜空间特征的激活核。在反应性化学基准上比较固定描述子基线、委员会分歧、随机采集等策略。

Result: 两种核方法一致优于基线，数据需求减少38%（能量误差）和28%（力误差）。预训练模型诱导的相似性空间保留了化学有意义的几何结构，残差不确定性估计比随机初始化或固定描述子核更可靠。

Conclusion: 预训练使得潜空间几何与模型误差对齐，为反应性MLIP微调提供了实用且充分的采集信号，无需额外复杂组件。

Abstract: Training machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for reactive chemistry is often bottlenecked by the high cost of quantum chemical labels and the scarcity of transition state configurations in candidate pools. Active learning (AL) can mitigate these costs, but its effectiveness hinges on the acquisition rule. We investigate whether the latent space of a pretrained MLIP already contains the information necessary for effective acquisition, eliminating the need for auxiliary uncertainty heads, Bayesian training and fine-tuning, or committee ensembles. We introduce two acquisition signals derived directly from a pretrained MACE potential: a finite-width neural tangent kernel (NTK) and an activation kernel built from hidden latent space features. On reactive-chemistry benchmarks, both kernels consistently outperform fixed-descriptor baselines, committee disagreement, and random acquisition, reducing the data required to reach performance targets by an average of 38% for energy error and 28% for force error. We further show that the pretrained model induces similarity spaces that preserve chemically meaningful structure and provide more reliable residual uncertainty estimates than randomly initialised or fixed-descriptor-based kernels. Our results suggest that pretraining aligns latent-space geometry with model error, yielding a practical and sufficient acquisition signal for reactive MLIP fine-tuning.

</details>


### [160] [Flow Sampling: Learning to Sample from Unnormalized Densities via Denoising Conditional Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03984)
*Aaron Havens,Brian Karrer,Neta Shaul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flow Sampling 是一个基于扩散模型和流匹配的无数据采样框架，通过从能量函数构建去噪扩散漂移来学习高效采样器，并在欧几里得空间和黎曼流形上均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从未归一化密度中采样相当于生成建模问题，但目标分布由已知能量函数而非数据样本定义。由于评估能量函数通常成本高昂，主要挑战是学习一个高效的采样器。

Method: Flow Sampling 基于扩散模型和流匹配，训练目标以噪声样本为条件，回归到从能量函数构建的去噪扩散漂移。利用插值过程最小化训练期间的能量函数评估次数。该方法自然扩展到黎曼流形，并在常曲率流形（包括超球面和双曲空间）上推导出条件漂移的闭式公式。

Result: 在合成能量基准、小肽、大规模摊销分子构象生成以及球面分布上进行了评估，展示了强大的实证性能。

Conclusion: Flow Sampling 提供了一种高效且可扩展的未归一化密度采样方法，适用于欧几里得空间和黎曼流形，在多个任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Sampling from unnormalized densities is analogous to the generative modeling problem, but the target distribution is defined by a known energy function instead of data samples. Because evaluating the energy function is often costly, a primary challenge is to learn an efficient sampler. We introduce Flow Sampling, a framework built on diffusion models and flow matching for the data-free setting. Our training objective is conditioned on a noise sample and regresses onto a denoising diffusion drift constructed from the energy function. In contrast, diffusion models' objective is conditioned on a data sample and regresses onto a noising diffusion drift. We utilize the interpolant process to minimize the number of energy function evaluations during training, resulting in an efficient and scalable method for sampling unnormalized densities. Furthermore, our formulation naturally extends to Riemannian manifolds, enabling diffusion-based sampling in geometries beyond Euclidean space. We derive a closed-form formula for the conditional drift on constant curvature manifolds, including hyperspheres and hyperbolic spaces. We evaluate Flow Sampling on synthetic energy benchmarks, small peptides, large-scale amortized molecular conformer generation, and distributions supported on the sphere, demonstrating strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [161] [A Closed-Form Adaptive-Landmark Kernel for Certified Point-Cloud and Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04046)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PALACE是一种数据自适应的持久同调图分析方法，通过覆盖论核心提供四种封闭形式保证，在多个基准数据集上达到领先性能，且无需梯度训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久图分析方法（如均匀网格）对数据分布不敏感，在高维或数据集中区域效率低下。PLACE框架虽提供了结构化分析，但缺乏数据自适应能力。本文旨在设计一个能根据数据分布自动调整的、具有理论保证的持久图分析引擎。

Method: (1) 在PLACE基础上引入三个超参数（预算、半径、带宽）的小规模交叉验证（≤5个选择）；(2) 利用覆盖论核心（基于Lebesgue数准则的地标覆盖）推导四种封闭形式保证；(3) 使用等权重 $w_k = K^{-1/2}$ 最大化结构下界，最远点采样实现最优k-中心覆盖半径的2-逼近；(4) 提出核-RKHS分类率及封闭形式的过滤选择规则；(5) 提供非渐近Pinelis和渐近高斯形式的逐预测置信度证书。

Result: (1) 结构下界 $λ(τ;ν)$ 在图表集中时比均匀网格节省 $(D/L)^2$ 的预算；(2) 核马氏距离边际 $\hatρ_{\mathrm{Mah}}$ 是化学图集上最强的封闭形式排序器（平均Spearman ρ≈+0.60）；(3) Orbit5k上达到91.3±1.0%（匹配Persformer），COX2和MUTAG上领先所有基于图的方法，DHFR上与ECP差距在1个百分点内；(4) 在8倍域膨胀下，自适应放置保持94%准确率而均匀网格降至25%（随机水平）。

Conclusion: PALACE作为PLACE的数据自适应扩展，以极小的交叉验证代价实现了持久图分析的封闭形式理论保证与实证性能的双重优势。其无需梯度训练的特性、逐预测置信度证书以及显著的自适应鲁棒性，使其成为持久同调图分析领域极具竞争力的方法。

Abstract: We introduce PALACE (Persistence Adaptive-Landmark Analytic Classification Engine), the data-adaptive companion to PLACE, paying a small cross-validation tier on three knobs (budget, radii, bandwidth; $\leq 5$ choices each). A cover-theoretic core (Lebesgue-number criterion on the landmark cover) yields four closed-form guarantees. (i) A structural lower distortion bound $λ(τ;ν)$ on $\mathcal{D}_n$ under cross-diagram non-interference, with a $(D/L)^2$ budget reduction over the uniform grid when diagrams concentrate. (ii) Equal weights $w_k = K^{-1/2}$ maximizing $λ$, and farthest-point-sampling positions $2$-approximating the optimal $k$-center covering radius; both derived from training labels alone, no gradient training. (iii) A kernel-RKHS classification rate $O((k-1)\sqrt{K}/(γ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$ with binary necessity threshold $m = Ω(\sqrt K/γ)$ from a matching Le Cam lower bound, and a closed-form filtration-selection rule. The kernel-Mahalanobis margin $\hatρ_{\mathrm{Mah}}$ is the strongest closed-form ranker across the chemical-graph pool (mean Spearman $ρ\approx +0.60$); the isotropic surrogate $\hatγ/\sqrt{K}$ admits a selection-consistency rate, and $\widehatλ$ from (i) provides an independent data-level signal (positive on COX2 and PTC). (iv) A per-prediction certificate, in non-asymptotic Pinelis and asymptotic Gaussian forms, with no calibration split. Empirically, PALACE is the strongest closed-form diagram-based method on Orbit5k ($91.3 \pm 1.0\%$, matching Persformer), leads every diagram-based competitor on COX2 and MUTAG, and is competitive on DHFR (within 1 pp of ECP). At $8\times$ domain inflation, adaptive placement maintains $94\%$ while the uniform grid collapses to chance ($25\%$ on 4-class data).

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [PERFECT: Personalized Federated Learning for CBRS Radar Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03199)
*Shafi Ullah Khan,Madan Baduwal,Vini Chaudhary,Debashri Roy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对CBRS频段中雷达信号检测面临的数据隐私和非独立同分布挑战，提出了一种基于联邦学习与ESC层级个性化相结合的PERFECT框架，在保护隐私的同时实现了99%召回率的雷达检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBRS频段中保护现有雷达用户免受干扰至关重要，但传统集中式检测模型存在显著的数据隐私问题，且难以适应地理分布传感器产生的非独立同分布数据。

Method: 提出PERFECT联邦学习框架，在ESC传感器上本地训练模型以保护隐私，并通过模型个性化处理不同ESC观察到不同雷达类型的非独立同分布场景。

Result: 广泛仿真表明PERFECT达到了规定的99%雷达检测召回率，性能与集中式方案相当，同时在隐私、效率和可扩展性方面显著提升。

Conclusion: PERFECT框架为动态频谱共享中的雷达检测提供了一种隐私保护、高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The Citizens Broadband Radio Service (CBRS) band is pivotal for expanding next-generation wireless services, but its success hinges on robustly protecting incumbent users, such as naval radar systems, from interference. This task is delegated to a network of Environmental Sensing Capability (ESC) sensors, which must detect faint radar signals amidst heavy co-channel interference from commercial LTE and 5G users. Traditional centralized detection models raise significant data privacy concerns and are ill-suited for the Non-Independent and Identically Distributed (non-IID) nature of data from geographically dispersed sensors. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel Federated Learning (FL) framework PERFECT that leverages ESC level personalization for robust and efficient radar detection. PERFECT preserves privacy by training models locally on ESC sensors. Furthermore, our framework is the first to effectively handle non-IID scenarios through model personalization where different ESCs observe distinct radar types. We demonstrate through extensive simulations that PERFECT achieves the mandated 99% recall for radar detection, matching centralized performance while significantly enhancing privacy, efficiency, and scalability for dynamic spectrum sharing.

</details>


### [163] [Single-Step Six-Dimensional Movable Antenna Reconfiguration for High-Mobility IoV: Modeling, Analysis, and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03321)
*Maoxin Ji,Qiong Wu,Pingyi Fan,Kezhi Wang,Wen Chen,Cui Zhang,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对六维可移动天线（6DMA）在高动态车联网（IoV）场景中的应用挑战，提出了一种低复杂度、无需信道状态信息（CSI）的单步重配置框架。该框架基于经纬度网格的确定性离散位置生成方案，利用图论建模天线重配置的移动和时间成本下界，融合离线环境先验与在线历史反馈的自适应优化策略，以及基于预测车辆累积分布的周期性重配置机制。仿真表明，该方案在上行和速率上显著优于传统固定和全局搜索基准，同时机械开销和时延可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6DMA系统在利用空间自由度提升无线容量方面具有潜力，但应用于高移动性车联网时面临两大挑战：瞬时信道状态信息（CSI）难以获取，以及机械重配置时延导致的服务中断风险。

Method: 1) 基于经纬度网格的确定性离散位置生成方案，利用图论建模并推导天线重配置的移动和时间成本下界；2) 利用6DMA信道方向稀疏性，融合离线环境先验与在线历史反馈的自适应优化策略；3) 基于预测车辆累积分布的周期性重配置机制，将天线调整严格限制在一阶空间邻域内，消除服务中断。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案在上行和速率上显著优于传统固定天线和全局搜索基准方案，同时机械开销和时延可忽略不计，验证了在高动态车联网中的可行性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的低复杂度、CSI-free单步重配置框架有效解决了6DMA在高速移动车联网场景中的CSI获取困难和机械时延问题，实现了性能与开销的良好权衡。

Abstract: The Six-Dimensional Movable Antenna (6DMA) system has emerged as a promising technology to enhance wireless capacity by fully exploiting spatial degrees of freedom. However, applying 6DMA to high-mobility Internet of Vehicles (IoV) scenarios faces significant challenges, primarily due to the difficulty of acquiring instantaneous Channel State Information (CSI) and the risk of service interruptions caused by mechanical reconfiguration delays. To address these issues, this paper proposes a low-complexity, CSI-free single-step reconfiguration framework. First, we design a deterministic discrete position generation scheme based on a latitude-longitude grid with inherent topological structures. Leveraging graph theory, we explicitly model and theoretically derive the lower bounds of movement and time costs for antenna reconfiguration. Subsequently, utilizing the directional sparsity of 6DMA channels, we develop an adaptive optimization strategy that fuses offline environmental priors with online historical feedback. Furthermore, a periodic reconfiguration mechanism based on predicted cumulative vehicle distributions is introduced. By strictly restricting antenna adjustments to the first-order spatial neighborhood, the proposed single-step method effectively eliminates service interruptions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly outperforms traditional fixed and global-search-based benchmarks in terms of uplink sum rate, while incurring negligible mechanical overhead and latency, thereby validating its feasibility and robustness in highly dynamic vehicular networks.

</details>


### [164] [Dynamic Hypergame for Task Assignment in Multi-platform Mobile Crowdsensing Under Incomplete Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03569)
*Sumedh J. Dongare,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Andrea Ortiz,Walid Saad,Anja Klein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACMAB，一种面向多平台竞争移动群智感知场景的去中心化感知感知双边学习框架，通过动态超博弈建模未知偏好，使MCSPs和MUs在不完全信息下分别学习自适应任务提议策略和任务接受策略，相比基准方法至少提升41%任务完成量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多假设单一MCSP，但实际部署中存在多个竞争性MCSPs同时向MUs发起任务邀约，且MUs会选择最大化自身收益的邀约。这种双向信息不对称（MCSPs缺乏其他平台偏好和MU质量信息，MUs缺乏任务执行努力信息）导致传统方法无法直接应用，亟需一种在不完全信息下实现双边匹配的解决方案。

Method: 将多MCSP竞争场景建模为双边匹配博弈合同（MWC），分解为MCSPs的任务提议问题（i）和MUs的任务接受问题（ii）。针对信息不可得问题，将MWC重新表述为动态超博弈，每个MCSP通过感知建模其他平台的未知偏好并在重复交互中逐步修正。提出PACMAB框架：（i）每个MCSP使用感知感知学习算法自适应调整竞争下的任务提议策略；（ii）每个MU通过估计任务执行努力学习任务接受策略。该框架完全去中心化且计算复杂度对双方均友好。

Result: PACMAB在计算复杂度上对MCSPs和MUs均具有良好可扩展性。大量仿真表明，PACMAB在不假设完全信息的前提下，始终优于基准方法，任务完成量至少提升41%。

Conclusion: PACMAB有效解决了多竞争MCSP场景下不完全信息的双边匹配问题，验证了动态超博弈建模和去中心化感知学习框架在MCS中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Mobile crowdsensing (MCS) is a promising distributed sensing paradigm for future wireless networks, where MCS platforms (MCSPs) recruit mobile units (MUs) through monetary incentives for sensing data collection. While most existing studies assume a single MCSP, practical deployments involve multiple competing MCSPs that simultaneously propose task offers to MUs, and MUs accept offers that maximize their revenue. This interaction gives rise to a two-sided matching game with contracts (MWC), decomposed into two components: (i) task proposal problem of the MCSPs and (ii) task acceptance problem of the MUs. To optimally solve (i), every MCSP requires information about other platforms' preferences and the qualities of the MUs in advance. Similarly, to solve (ii) optimally, the MUs require information about the task execution efforts of all tasks in advance. Such information is unavailable at the MCSPs and at the MUs. To address the challenge of unknown preferences of the other MCSPs, the MWC is posed as a dynamic hypergame, where every MCSP models the unknown preferences through perceptions and refines them over repeated interactions. To solve the dynamic hypergame under incomplete information, we propose PACMAB, a fully decentralized perception-aware two-sided learning framework where, (i) each MCSP learns an adaptive task proposal strategy under competition, and (ii) each MU learns task acceptance policy by estimating task execution efforts. Computational complexity of PACMAB shows that it scales favorably for the MCSPs as well as the MUs. Extensive simulations show that PACMAB consistently outperforms the benchmarks by completing at least 41% more tasks without assuming complete information.

</details>


### [165] [Nested array design of extended coprime sets for DOA estimation of non-circular signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03856)
*Dongqi Chen,Kun Ye,Chuanxi Xing,Waqas Khalid,Huiping Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩展互质框架的滑动平移阵列结构，通过优化传感器布局并融合差和协同阵列，显著提升非圆信号DOA估计的自由度与虚拟孔径，仿真验证其性能优于嵌套阵等传统结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非圆信号DOA估计中接收阵列自由度不足的问题，旨在设计一种新型阵列构型，以扩大虚拟孔径阵列和自由度，提高估计精度。

Method: 基于扩展互质框架，采用滑动平移技术优化传感器布局，保持差协同阵列连续性，并将和协同阵列平移后与差协同阵列无缝融合，消除冗余，扩展虚拟孔径阵列和自由度。

Result: 相比传统嵌套阵列、扩展滑动嵌套阵列等基准结构，所提阵列在自由度、虚拟孔径阵列和估计精度上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提阵列在非圆信号DOA估计中具有优越性能，仿真结果验证了其有效性与先进性。

Abstract: In recent years, direction of arrival estimation utilizing non-circular signals has become a focal point for scholarly research. To enhance the degrees of freedom (DOF) in receiver arrays specifically for non-circular signal DOA estimation, this study introduces a novel array configuration. This design leverages an extended coprime framework, applying a sliding translation technique to optimize sensor placement. Crucially, this rearranged structure preserves the continuity of the difference co-array (DCA). Furthermore, the sum co-array (SCA) is shifted to merge seamlessly with the DCA, eliminating redundancy and substantially expanding both the virtual aperture array (VAA) and the DOF. Consequently, the proposed array demonstrates superior performance in practical DOA estimation tasks involving non-circular signals. Simulation results and comparative analyses confirm that, relative to traditional Nested Arrays (NA), Extended Sliding Nested Array (ESNA), and other benchmark structures, the proposed array achieves better DOF and VAA, leading to enhanced estimation accuracy in practical scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [ARIS: Autonomous Research via Adversarial Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03042)
*Ruofeng Yang,Yongcan Li,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍ARIS（Auto-Research-in-sleep），一个用于自主研究的开源研究框架，通过跨模型家族对抗协作协调机器学习研究流程，包含执行层、编排层和保障层三层架构，并内置完整性验证、结果到声明映射、声明审计等保障机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM上的智能体系统性能既取决于模型权重也取决于框架（harness），而长期研究流程的核心故障模式不是可见的崩溃，而是看似合理但缺乏证据支持的"成功"——智能体可能产出声明，但其证据支持不完整、被错误报告或隐式继承自执行者的框架。因此需要一个能协调研究流程并确保声明有充分证据支持的框架。

Method: ARIS采用三层架构：1) 执行层：提供65+个Markdown定义的可复用技能、MCP模型集成、持久化研究Wiki、确定性的图表生成；2) 编排层：协调5个端到端工作流，支持可调节的努力设置和可配置的评审模型路由；3) 保障层：包含三步声明证据检查流程（完整性验证、结果到声明映射、声明审计）和五遍科学编辑管线、数学证明检查、PDF视觉检查。默认配置采用跨模型家族对抗协作（执行模型推进工作，不同模型家族的评审模型批判中间产物并请求修订）。

Result: ARIS已作为开源工具发布并进行了早期部署，包含一个原型自我改进循环，可记录研究轨迹并提出框架改进，改进仅在评审模型批准后采纳。

Conclusion: ARIS通过引入跨模型对抗协作和三层保障架构，为解决自主研究中"看似合理但缺乏证据支持"这一核心故障模式提供了系统化方案，未来可进一步扩展其自我改进能力。

Abstract: This report describes ARIS (Auto-Research-in-sleep), an open-source research harness for autonomous research, including its architecture, assurance mechanisms, and early deployment experience. The performance of agent systems built on LLMs depends on both the model weights and the harness around them, which governs what information to store, retrieve, and present to the model. For long-horizon research workflows, the central failure mode is not a visible breakdown but a plausible unsupported success: a long-running agent can produce claims whose evidential support is incomplete, misreported, or silently inherited from the executor's framing. Therefore, we present ARIS as a research harness that coordinates machine-learning research workflows through cross-model adversarial collaboration as a default configuration: an executor model drives forward progress while a reviewer from a different model family is recommended to critique intermediate artifacts and request revisions. ARIS has three architectural layers. The execution layer provides more than 65 reusable Markdown-defined skills, model integrations via MCP, a persistent research wiki for iterative reuse of prior findings, and deterministic figure generation. The orchestration layer coordinates five end-to-end workflows with adjustable effort settings and configurable routing to reviewer models. The assurance layer includes a three-stage process for checking whether experimental claims are supported by evidence: integrity verification, result-to-claim mapping, and claim auditing that cross-checks manuscript statements against the claim ledger and raw evidence, as well as a five-pass scientific-editing pipeline, mathematical-proof checks, and visual inspection of the rendered PDF. A prototype self-improvement loop records research traces and proposes harness improvements that are adopted only after reviewer approval.

</details>


### [167] [ARISE: A Repository-level Graph Representation and Toolset for Agentic Fault Localization and Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03117)
*Shahd Seddik,Fatemeh Fard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ARISE通过构建多粒度程序图（包含语句级定义-使用边）并暴露为可查询的数据流切片工具API，使LLM智能体能够高效追踪变量依赖，在SWE-bench Lite上将函数级Recall@1提升17.0%、行级Recall@1提升15.0%，修复成功率Pass@1达22.0%，较基线提升4.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的仓库级表示仅建模文件、类、函数等结构关系，缺乏对过程内变量值流（数据流）的语义精确建模，导致智能体在函数级和行级定位时精度不足。

Method: 提出ARISE框架，构建多粒度程序图，在结构关系基础上增加语句级节点及过程内定义-使用边；通过三层工具API将数据流切片暴露为可查询的一等智能体原语，使模型单次调用即可追溯变量的定义与消费语句。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite（300个GitHub真实问题，11个Python仓库）上，以Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct为骨干，相比SWE-agent基线：Function Recall@1提升17.0个百分点，Line Recall@1提升15.0个百分点，Pass@1达22.0%（66/300），提升4.7个百分点。消融实验验证改进源于数据流图而非工具模式，且大代码模型可直接消费结构化切片输出。

Conclusion: 数据流图增强能显著提升LLM智能体在仓库级缺陷定位与自动修复中的精度；ARISE的图构建与切片API可作为框架无关的即插即用工具集，支持未来APR研究。

Abstract: Repository-level fault localization (FL) and automated program repair (APR) require an agent to identify the relevant code units across files, follow call and data dependencies, and generate a valid patch. Existing graph-based systems provide structural representations of repositories (files, classes, functions and their relationships) but do not model how variable values flow within procedures, leaving agents without the semantic precision needed for function- and line-level localization. We present ARISE (Agentic Repository-level Issue Solving Engine), which augments an LLM-based agent with a multi-granularity program graph that extends structural relationships down to statement-level nodes connected by intra-procedural definition-use edges. ARISE exposes this graph through a three-tier tool API, which brings data-flow slicing as a first-class, queryable agent primitive that allows the model to trace, in a single call, which statements define or consume a variable of interest. We evaluate on SWE-bench Lite (300 real GitHub issues, 11 Python repositories) using Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct as the backbone. Compared to the unmodified SWE-agent baseline, ARISE improves Function Recall@1 by 17.0 points and Line Recall@1 by 15.0 points. These localization gains translate directly into repair success, with ARISE achieving 22.0% Pass@1 (66/300), a 4.7 percentage-point improvement over SWE-agent. Controlled ablations confirm that the improvement is driven by the data-flow graph rather than the tool schema, and that large code models consume structured slice output directly without requiring a natural-language summarization layer. The graph builder and slicing API are designed as a framework-agnostic, drop-in toolset for future APR research.

</details>


### [168] [TeamUp: Semantic Project Matching and Team Formation for Learning at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03237)
*Dhruv Gulwani,Basem Suleiman,Aditya Joshi,Sonit Singh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TeamUp是一个基于嵌入向量的轻量级团队组建系统，通过预训练语言模型的语义嵌入和混合排序算法，在大规模项目式课程中实现学生与项目的精准匹配及认知多样化团队构建，在虚拟实验中匹配质量、难度对齐、团队多样性等指标均显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统项目分配方法（手动表格、偏好调查）无法构建认知多样化的团队，导致高绩效学生自选可见项目而弱势学生机会受限，加剧了公平性问题。

Method: 使用预训练语言模型生成语义嵌入，通过混合排序算法（余弦相似度结合难度对齐、领域偏好、需求平衡等教学约束）进行个性化推荐，并通过嵌入方差建模技能互补性以构建认知多样化团队。

Result: 虚拟实验基于250个学生档案和60个项目描述：匹配质量显著提升（平均余弦相似度0.74 vs 0.43）；难度对齐改善（83%匹配在一个级别内 vs 34%）；团队多样性提升（82%覆盖三个以上技术领域 vs 41%）；亚秒级推荐延迟，每名学生运营成本低于0.10美元。

Conclusion: TeamUp在大规模项目式学习中能同时提升学习效果和公平性，是一种高效、透明的团队组建方案。

Abstract: Project-based learning improves student engagement and learning outcomes, yet allocating students to appropriately challenging projects while forming cognitively diverse teams remains difficult at scale. Traditional allocation methods (manual spreadsheets, preference surveys) can't construct the cognitively diverse teams that that collaborate cognitively. This mismatch perpetuates equity issues: high-performing students self-select visible projects while under-represented students face reduced access to opportunity. We propose TeamUp, a lightweight, embedding-based team-forming system designed to improve learning outcomes and equity in large-scale project-based courses. TeamUp uses semantic embeddings from pretrained language models to match students to projects aligned with their skill level. The system employs a hybrid ranking algorithm combining cosine similarity with pedagogical constraints (difficulty alignment, domain preferences, and demand balancing) to generate personalised and transparent recommendations. Beyond individual matching, TeamUp constructs cognitively diverse teams by modelling skill complementarity through embedding variance, ensuring teams possess well-distributed capabilities rather than homogeneous strengths. We evaluated TeamUp through a virtual experiment using 250 student profiles and 60 project descriptions. Results show: (1) substantially higher match quality (mean cosine similarity of 0.74 vs. 0.43); (2) better difficulty alignment (83% placed within one level vs. 34%); (3) more diverse teams (82% covering three or more technical areas vs. 41%); and (4) sub-second recommendation latency at operational costs under $0.10 per student.

</details>


### [169] [Exploring Sustainability in Scientific Software through Code Quality & Test Coverage Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03243)
*Sheikh Md. Mushfiqur Rahman,Gregory R. Watson,Nasir U. Eisty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了科学开源软件（SciOSS）的长期可持续性，通过对CASS软件组合项目的代码与测试质量分析发现，可持续项目具有更高、更一致性的测试覆盖率及更清晰的代码-测试关联，而不可持续项目则呈现弱模式，且整体测试覆盖率偏低，高复杂度和耦合度降低了可测试性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学开源软件在研究与工程中扮演基础角色，但其长期可持续性常被忽视，成为一个重要但未被充分关注的问题。

Method: 分析CASS软件组合项目，按可持续性分类，比较代码结构、测试覆盖率以及代码质量与测试之间的关联。

Result: 可持续项目表现出更高、更一致的测试覆盖率以及更清晰的代码-测试相关性；不可持续项目模式较弱；整体科学软件测试覆盖率较低，高复杂度和耦合度降低了可测试性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用的、数据驱动的评估科学软件可持续性的方法，为评估长期软件健康、支持未来质量保证和可持续性监测提供了基础。

Abstract: Context: Scientific open-source software (SciOSS) plays a foundational role in research and engineering, yet its long-term sustainability has often been overlooked and remains a significant concern. Objective: This study investigates the long-term sustainability of SciOSS through code and test quality metrics. Method: We analyze CASS Software Portfolio projects, classifying them by sustainability and comparing their code structure, test coverage, and links between code quality and testing across the dataset. Results: Sustainable projects show higher, more consistent test coverage and clearer code-test correlations, while unsustainable ones show weaker patterns. Overall, test coverage is low in scientific software, and high complexity and coupling reduce testability. Conclusion: In this study, we present a practical, data-driven approach for assessing sustainability in scientific software, offering a foundation for evaluating long-term software health and supporting future efforts in quality assurance and sustainability monitoring.

</details>


### [170] [Operationalizing Software Engineering Theories for Practical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03257)
*Isaque Alves,Fabio Kon,Jessica Diaz,Carla Rocha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的操作化流程，用于将软件工程中的抽象理论概念转化为可测量、可验证的要素，弥合理论与实证验证之间的差距，并以DevOps团队分类学理论为例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程作为应用科学，需要将抽象的理论概念转化为可操作的实证要素，以实现实践效用。现有操作化框架缺乏系统性和可复现性，导致理论与实证验证之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 扩展Sjøberg等人的操作化框架，结合Dubin的非因果假设方法，定义了变量选择、指标确定和假设系统推导的三步流程，形成可复现的循证方法指南。

Result: 提出了一套透明的、可复制的操作化方法论指南，能够保持从理论到可测试要素的完整证据链，并通过DevOps团队分类学理论进行了实例验证。

Conclusion: 该操作化流程能够帮助软件工程研究者将理论进步建立在实证证据基础上，并为实践者提供可操作的见解，从而增强理论研究的严谨性和实用性。

Abstract: Software Engineering often adapts theory-building frameworks from the social sciences to address socio-technical complexity. The key phases of the theory-building process are conceptual development, operationalization, testing, and application. Operationalization translates abstract concepts into measurable elements for empirical validation. This phase is essential for delivering the practical utility required by an applied science like Software Engineering. We propose a systematic procedure for the operationalization phase that bridges the gap between abstract concepts and empirical validation, ensuring the resulting theory is both rigorous and practically useful. We extend the operationalization framework proposed by Sjøberg et al. and formulate non-causal hypotheses following Dubin's approach. Our procedure defines variables, selects indicators, and systematically derives hypotheses. We present a replicable, evidence-based methodological guideline that preserves a clear chain of evidence and supports practical validation. We illustrate the procedure using the DevOps Team Taxonomies Theory. This guideline provides a transparent chain of evidence from theory to testable elements, empowering researchers to ground theoretical advancements in empirical evidence and deliver actionable insights for practitioners.

</details>


### [171] [POSTCONDBENCH: Benchmarking Correctness and Completeness in Formal Postcondition Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03356)
*Gehao Zhang,Juan Zhai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 POSTCONDBENCH，一个包含 420 个 Python/Java 任务的多语言基准测试，用于评估后置条件生成。通过缺陷判别指标衡量完整性，发现 SOTA LLM 在正确性与完整性之间存在显著差距，且仓库级依赖和方法复杂度加剧了这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后置条件自动生成方法依赖表面形式匹配或人工评估，缺乏高质量、可自动评估的真实世界基准，导致评估存在瓶颈。

Method: 构建 POSTCONDBENCH：从 121 个开源项目收集 420 个 Python/Java 任务，由专家参与构建高质量真值后置条件集；提出基于缺陷判别的完整性度量；设计三种生成设置，评估五个 SOTA LLM。

Result: LLM 在正确性与完整性之间存在显著差距；仓库级依赖和方法复杂度进一步扩大了这一差距。

Conclusion: 现有 LLM 生成的后置条件在正确性上尚可，但完整性不足，尤其在复杂真实场景下表现不佳，亟需更关注完整性的评估方法和生成技术。

Abstract: Formal postconditions precisely characterize program behavior and support debugging, testing, and verification, but writing them requires substantial expertise and effort. This has motivated recent work on automatically generating postconditions from code and natural-language artifacts using large language models (LLMs). However, evaluation remains a key bottleneck. Existing benchmarks primarily emphasize correctness under limited evaluation settings, often relying on surface-form matching or manual assessment on small or synthetic datasets.
  We introduce POSTCONDBENCH, a multilingual benchmark for evaluating method-level postcondition generation from real-world software. POSTCONDBENCH comprises 420 Python and Java tasks drawn from 121 open-source projects, each paired with a high-quality ground-truth postcondition set constructed with expert involvement. To enable automatic evaluation, POSTCONDBENCH provides a runnable execution environment and operationalizes completeness via defect discrimination: a postcondition set is more complete if it is violated by more defective implementations while remaining satisfied on correct executions. Using POSTCONDBENCH, we formulate three generation settings and evaluate five SOTA LLMs. Our results reveal a substantial gap between correctness and completeness, and show that repository-level dependencies and method complexity exacerbate this gap.

</details>


### [172] [ProgramBench: Can Language Models Rebuild Programs From Scratch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03546)
*John Yang,Kilian Lieret,Jeffrey Ma,Parth Thakkar,Dmitrii Pedchenko,Sten Sootla,Emily McMilin,Pengcheng Yin,Rui Hou,Gabriel Synnaeve,Diyi Yang,Ofir Press*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProgramBench是一个衡量软件工程智能体从零开始构建完整软件项目能力的基准测试，包含200个任务，从CLI工具到FFmpeg、SQLite等大型软件。9个LM评估显示无模型能完全解决任何任务，最佳模型仅3%任务通过95%测试，且倾向生成单文件单体架构，与人工编写代码差异大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试集中于修复单bug或开发单一功能等有限任务，无法衡量智能体的整体软件架构决策能力，因此需要能端到端评估智能体构建完整软件项目能力的基准。

Method: 构建200个任务，给定程序及其文档，要求智能体搭建代码库以匹配参考可执行文件行为；通过智能体驱动的模糊测试生成端到端行为测试，评估时不规定实现结构。

Result: 9个LM中无模型能完全解决任何任务；最佳模型仅在3%的任务上通过了95%的测试；模型倾向于生成单文件单体实现，与人工编写的代码风格差异显著。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在整体软件开发能力上仍存在显著不足，无法完成复杂的软件架构任务，且生成代码的结构与人工代码差异大，表明该方向仍需大量改进。

Abstract: Turning ideas into full software projects from scratch has become a popular use case for language models. Agents are being deployed to seed, maintain, and grow codebases over extended periods with minimal human oversight. Such settings require models to make high-level software architecture decisions. However, existing benchmarks measure focused, limited tasks such as fixing a single bug or developing a single, specified feature. We therefore introduce ProgramBench to measure the ability of software engineering agents to develop software holisitically. In ProgramBench, given only a program and its documentation, agents must architect and implement a codebase that matches the reference executable's behavior. End-to-end behavioral tests are generated via agent-driven fuzzing, enabling evaluation without prescribing implementation structure. Our 200 tasks range from compact CLI tools to widely used software such as FFmpeg, SQLite, and the PHP interpreter. We evaluate 9 LMs and find that none fully resolve any task, with the best model passing 95\% of tests on only 3\% of tasks. Models favor monolithic, single-file implementations that diverge sharply from human-written code.

</details>


### [173] [AI Advocate: Educational Path to Transform Squads to the Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03800)
*Carla Soares,Gabriel Moreira,Ana Paula Camargo,Fabio Henrique Scacabarozi,Nicole Davila,Marselle Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了巴西一家私营科技公司通过培养“AI倡导者”角色，将传统软件开发团队转型为人机协作混合结构的战略教育过程，总结了关键经验教训与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能显著提升生产力的背景下，传统软件开发团队需要向人机协作的混合结构转型，但缺乏系统化的教育和赋能方法来推动这一文化和技术变革。

Method: 采用经验报告（experience report）方法，在巴西一家私营科技公司中设计并实施了AI倡导者（AI Advocates）的培养与赋能流程，通过实践观察总结关键经验。

Result: 成功将传统软件团队转型为混合结构，AI倡导者作为催化剂推动了文化和技术转变，识别出具体的经验教训和挑战。

Conclusion: 培养AI倡导者是推动传统软件团队向人机协作混合结构转型的有效策略，其教育和赋能过程中的经验可为同类组织提供参考。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the strategic education process aimed at transitioning traditional software development squads into hybrid structures centered on collaborative work between humans and Artificial Intelligence (AI). In a context where human-AI collaboration can significantly increase productivity, this study explores how the upskilling of XPTO professionals, referred to as AI Advocates, acts as a catalyst for cultural and technical transformation. The objective is to present an experience report on the education and enablement process of AI Advocates within a private Brazilian technology company, highlighting key lessons learned and identified challenges.

</details>


### [174] [KVerus: Scalable and Resilient Formal Verification Proof Generation for Rust Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03822)
*Yuwei Liu,Xinyi Wan,Yanhao Wang,Minghua Wang,Lin Huang,Tao Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: KVerus是一个检索增强的自适应验证系统，通过构建动态知识库（包含代码元数据、引理语义和工具链信息），结合依赖感知程序分析、语义引理索引和错误驱动自我优化，弥合LLM语义模式与形式验证结构性依赖之间的"语义-结构鸿沟"，在单文件、跨文件依赖库及操作系统内核验证中均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式验证对大型演化系统应用困难，LLM生成的证明在现实场景中因无法处理复杂跨模块依赖或代码库/工具链变更而频繁失败，根本问题在于LLM的语义代码模式与形式验证的刚性结构依赖之间存在"语义-结构鸿沟"。

Method: 提出自适应性验证范式KVerus：构建包含代码元数据、引理语义和工具链信息的动态知识库，通过依赖感知程序分析、语义引理索引和错误驱动自我优化三个核心机制，实现跨文件依赖导航、证明合成和自动化证明修复。

Result: 在三个单文件基准上验证80.2%任务（优于SOTA AutoVerus的56.9%），在Verus更新下退化更少；三个仓库级跨文件依赖基准上实现51.0%成功率（vs多轮提示基线4.5%）；在Asterinas Rust OS内核中产生被上游接受的证明，验证了内存管理模块中23个先前未验证函数（占证明代码21.0%）。

Conclusion: KVerus代表了使形式验证成为现代安全关键软件可扩展且可持续实践的重要一步。

Abstract: Formal verification provides the highest assurance of software correctness and security, but its application to large-scale, evolving systems remains a major challenge. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating proof generation, they often fail in real-world settings due to their inability to handle complex cross-module dependencies or changes in the codebase or the verification toolchain. We identify the fundamental problem as the Semantic-Structural Gap: LLMs operate on semantic code patterns, whereas formal verification is governed by rigid structural dependencies, a disconnect that leads to brittle, unsustainable proofs.
  To bridge this gap, we propose a new paradigm of self-adaptive verification and present KVerus, a retrieval-augmented system for Verus-based Rust verification that can adapt to an evolving software environment. KVerus constructs a dynamic knowledge base of code metadata, lemma semantics, and toolchain specifics. By combining dependency-aware program analysis, semantic lemma indexing, and error-driven self-refinement, it can navigate intricate cross-file dependencies to synthesize proofs and automatically repair proofs when faced with common evolutionary changes. Across three single-file benchmarks, KVerus verifies 80.2% of tasks, outperforming the state-of-the-art AutoVerus (56.9%) and degrades less than AutoVerus under breaking Verus updates. On three repository-level benchmarks with cross-file dependencies, KVerus achieves a 51.0% success rate, compared to 4.5% for a multi-round prompting baseline. Finally, on the Asterinas Rust OS kernel, KVerus produces upstream-accepted proofs that verify 23 previously unverified functions (21.0% of proof code) in the memory-management module. KVerus represents a significant step towards making formal verification a scalable and sustainable practice for modern, security-critical software.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Rules: LLM-Powered Linting for Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03943)
*Pietro Cassieri,Giuseppe Scanniello,Seung Yeob Shin,Fabrizio Pastore,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LintQ-LLM+CoT和LintQ-LLM+RAG两种基于大语言模型的方法，用于检测量子编程问题。在55个Qiskit程序上的评估显示，LLM方法显著优于传统规则检测工具LintQ（F1分数0.70和0.68 vs 0.41），其中RAG增强版本在精确率上表现更优，有效降低了误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算从理论走向实际应用，量子软件的可靠性成为关键瓶颈。传统基于规则的静态分析工具（如linter）难以跟上快速演变的API，且无法捕获复杂的上下文相关量子编程问题，维护成本高且检测能力有限。

Method: 提出了两种基于LLM的量子编程问题检测方法：LintQ-LLM+CoT（利用思维链提示）和LintQ-LLM+RAG（在检索增强生成系统中，将模型推理锚定在经过验证的量子编程问题和最佳实践知识库上）。

Result: 在55个Qiskit程序上的严格人工对比评估中，两种LLM方法在检测正确性（精确率）和完整性（召回率）方面均优于LintQ。LLM方法的F1分数为0.70和0.68，而LintQ仅为0.41。RAG增强变体在精确率上略优，有效减少了误报。

Conclusion: LLM为量子软件工程中下一代linter提供了可扩展且自适应的基础。

Abstract: As quantum computing transitions from theoretical experimentation to its practical application, the reliability of quantum software has become a critical bottleneck. Traditional static analysis techniques for quantum programs, primarily rule-based linters, are increasingly inadequate; they struggle to keep pace with rapidly evolving APIs and fail to capture complex, context-dependent quantum programming problems. This results in high maintenance overhead and limited detection capabilities. In this paper, we introduce LintQ-LLM+CoT and LintQ-LLM+RAG, novel approaches that redefine the detection of quantum programming problems by employing Large Language Models (LLMs) specialized, respectively, via Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting and a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system that grounds the model's reasoning in a curated knowledge base of verified quantum programming problems and best practices. We conducted a rigorous and manual comparative evaluation against the state-of-the-art rule-based tool, LintQ, using a corpus of 55 Qiskit programs. Our results show that LLM-based approaches, with and without RAG, outperform LintQ in terms of quantum programming problems detection correctness (precision) and completeness (recall). Overall, LLM-based approaches were more effective than LintQ (F1-score equal to 0.70 and 0.68 vs. 0.41). Furthermore, the RAG-enhanced variant demonstrated a slightly superior precision, effectively reducing false positives. Our findings suggest that LLMs provide a scalable and adaptive foundation for the next generation of linters in quantum software engineering.

</details>


### [176] [Randomized and Diverse Input State Generation for Quantum Program Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03957)
*Maryse Ernzer,Seung Yeob Shin,Fabrizio Pastore,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种评估量子测试电路生成器对量子态空间覆盖度的框架，包括多样性评分、基于砖块电路（BC）的测试输入生成器，以及对比实验。实验表明，硬件兼容的BC生成器在更浅的电路深度下实现了更高的表达能力和纠缠性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的可靠性至关重要，但现有软件测试主要关注幅值、相位和纠缠等量子特性，而对量子态空间的完整探索（完全状态空间覆盖）关注不足。

Method: 1) 开发了涵盖局部与全局指标的多样性评分，用于量化态空间的幅值、相位和纠缠的局部相关性与全局分布；2) 提出基于砖块电路（Brick-Circuit, BC）的测试输入生成器，利用硬件兼容门逼近理想随机态；3) 将BC生成器与现有生成器在均匀分布随机测试输入态的能力上进行对比。

Result: BC生成器在更浅的电路深度下实现了更高的表达能力（均匀覆盖量子态空间的能力）和纠缠性能，优于现有电路生成器。

Conclusion: 硬件兼容的BC生成器能够更高效地探索量子态空间，为量子软件测试提供了更可靠的测试输入生成方案。

Abstract: With the accelerating development of quantum technologies and their growing computational potential, quantum systems are being adapted for simulations and other critical tasks across diverse domains, making the reliability of the corresponding quantum software an essential concern. Although recent efforts have started to incorporate quantum-specific properties such as magnitude, phase, and entanglement under the form of input-coverage criteria into software testing, the unique structure of the quantum state space demands for more comprehensive testing. In particular, the notion of complete state-space exploration has so far received little attention.
  To address this gap, we propose a framework for evaluating test circuit generators with respect to their coverage of the quantum state space. Our contribution is threefold: we develop a set of diversity scores that capture both local and global indicators of the extent to which the state space is explored; we propose a test circuit generator that produces test input states via a Brick-Circuit (BC) construction designed to approximate ideal random states using hardware-compatible gates; we compare the proposed construction with existing generators based on their ability to generate uniformly distributed random test input states.
  Our extended diversity scores quantify the local correlations and global spread of magnitude, phase and entanglement. Using these scores, we evaluate the expressibility, defined as the capability to span the quantum state space uniformly, and entangling capabilities of existing generators relative to the BC generator. Our results show that the hardware-compatible BC generator achieves higher expressibility and entanglement performance at shallower depths than existing circuit generators.

</details>


### [177] [Mitigating False Positives in Static Memory Safety Analysis of Rust Programs via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04000)
*P Akilesh,Leuson Da Silva,Foutse Khomh,Sridhar Chimalakonda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的方法，用于自动分类和抑制Rust静态内存安全分析中的误报。该方法通过从Rust的MIR中提取上下文特征，并结合cargo-fuzz动态验证作为辅助反馈机制，显著降低了误报率。实验结果表明，该方法在准确率（65.2%）和F1分数（0.659）上超越了最先进的LLM基线，将精确率从原始Rudra输出的25.6%提升至59.0%，召回率达74.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Rust静态内存安全分析工具（如Rudra、MirChecker）存在高误报率问题，降低了开发者信任度，增加了人工审查负担，并可能掩盖真实漏洞。

Method: 设计了一个强化学习智能体，从Rust的MIR中提取上下文特征，学习警告抑制策略；同时集成cargo-fuzz动态模糊测试作为辅助反馈机制，对可疑警告进行选择性验证，优化决策质量。

Result: 准确率65.2%，F1分数0.659（比最佳LLM基线提升17.1%），召回率74.6%，精确率从25.6%提升至59.0%。结合动态模糊测试进一步使准确率提升10.7个百分点，F1分数提升8.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与混合静态-动态分析相结合，能够显著降低误报率，提高Rust内存安全验证工具的实际可用性。

Abstract: Static analysis tools are essential for ensuring memory safety in Rust programs, particularly as Rust gains adoption in safety-critical domains. However, existing tools such as Rudra and MirChecker suffer from high false positive rates, which diminish developer trust, increase manual review effort, and may obscure genuine vulnerabilities. This paper presents a novel reinforcement learning (RL)-based approach for automatically classifying and suppressing spurious warnings in static memory safety analysis for Rust. To achieve this, we design an RL agent that learns a warning suppression policy by extracting contextual features from Rust's Mid-level Intermediate Representation (MIR) and optimizing its decisions through interaction with static analysis outputs. To improve decision quality, we integrate dynamic validation via cargo-fuzz as an auxiliary feedback mechanism, allowing the agent to selectively validate suspicious warnings through targeted fuzz testing. Our evaluation shows that the proposed approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines, achieving 65.2% accuracy and an F1 score of 0.659, an improvement of 17.1% over the best LLM baseline. With a recall of 74.6%, our method successfully identifies nearly three-quarters of true bugs while substantially reducing false positives, improving precision from 25.6% in raw Rudra output to 59.0%. Incorporating dynamic fuzzing further boosts performance, yielding additional improvements of 10.7 percentage points in accuracy and 8.6 percentage points in F1 score over the RL-only variant. Overall, our work demonstrates that combining reinforcement learning with hybrid static-dynamic analysis can substantially reduce false positives and improve the practical usability of memory safety verification tools for Rust.

</details>
